# Maat Continuous Integration Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Add one secret-free GitHub Actions workflow that runs every offline test suite, scans the full history and every commit message for secrets, lints itself, and ends in one `summary` gate whose job summary shows the counts each runner reported, plus `SECURITY.md`, Dependabot for the pinned actions, a dispatch-only pre-flip scan, and README and docs text that says what CI runs.

**Architecture:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` runs four parallel work jobs (`contracts`, `agent`, `web`, `hygiene`) on `ubuntu-24.04`; each passes its runner's JSON report through `.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs extract`, which turns it into one compact JSON job output and annotates failed forge and Playwright tests. A fifth job, `summary`, on `ubuntu-slim`, needs all four, runs the script's `node --test` suite, renders the counts table with `ci-counts.mjs render`, and fails in plain shell unless all four succeeded. A sixth job, `preflip`, runs only on a manual dispatch with `pre_flip: true` and scans every pull-request ref and every pull-request and issue text with the same digest-pinned gitleaks image.

**Tech Stack:** GitHub Actions, Node 24 `node:test`, gitleaks v8.30.1 and actionlint v1.7.12 images, Foundry v1.9.0, Vitest 5.1.0, Playwright 1.65.0.

**Spec:** docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-18-maat-ci-design.md

---

## Global Constraints

- The spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-18-maat-ci-design.md` (commit 71d24b0 on `chore/ci`, approved by the owner on 2026-11-18) is binding. Nothing is added that the spec, the local research notes in `E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.superpowers/ecosystem/e5/*.md` or the repository does not support. Where the spec is silent, this plan takes the smallest choice and says so in one line.
- Work happens only in the worktree `E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci`, branch `chore/ci`, cut from `main` at 8608b7d, plus the spec commit 71d24b0. The main checkout `E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon` is on `feat/docs-center` and belongs to other work: read only, never write, build or test there.
- Pins (read 2026-11-18), copied verbatim from spec section 3:

  | Dependency | Version | Pin |
  | --- | --- | --- |
  | `actions/checkout` | v7.0.1 | `3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1` |
  | `actions/setup-node` | v7.1.0 | `949feb2413d6458794dcd2491c4babbbce0c15c1` |
  | `foundry-rs/foundry-toolchain` | v1.9.2 | `dba66c27052e63476d886f22216e119ba7e615bf` |
  | `actions/upload-artifact` | v7.0.2 | `cf430e030ddbb5b0abf93d22962f4752f3646cd9` |
  | gitleaks image | v8.30.1 | `zricethezav/gitleaks@sha256:c00b6bd0aeb3071cbcb79009cb16a60dd9e0a7c60e2be9ab65d25e6bc8abbb7f` |
  | actionlint image | v1.7.12 (released 2026-03-30) | `rhysd/actionlint@sha256:b1934ee5f1c509618f2508e6eb47ee0d3520686341fec936f3b79331f9315667` |
  | Foundry | v1.9.0 (commit `6056c58`) | the toolchain's `version` input |
  | Node | 24 (v24.22.3 today) | setup-node's `node-version` |

- Every `uses:` line is `<action>@<40-hex SHA> # <version>` with the version from the table (for example `# v7.0.1`). The two images are run with `docker run` by digest; a bump is a manual commit that re-reads the digest and re-checks the stopword and allowlist notes of spec 6.1.
- Every work job (`contracts`, `agent`, `web`, `hygiene`) runs `actions/setup-node` with `node-version: 24`; `summary` uses the slim image's Node 24 and has no setup-node.
- Foundry: `foundry-rs/foundry-toolchain` with `version: v1.9.0` and `cache: false`; the first contracts step fails unless `forge --version` prints `6056c58`.
- Runners: `ubuntu-24.04` for `contracts`, `agent`, `web`, `hygiene` and `preflip`; `ubuntu-slim` for `summary`.
- Timeouts (`timeout-minutes`): contracts 15, agent 10, web 30, hygiene 10, summary 5, preflip 15. The `web` value is for the first runs (spec 3): Task 9 Step 27 sets it, with the owner's approval, to ceil(1.5 × the `web` duration in minutes of the first green run on the merged tree), and the `web` entry of `JOBS` in `workflow-policy.mjs` to the same number.
- Triggers: `push` to `main`, every `pull_request`, and `workflow_dispatch` with one boolean input `pre_flip` (default false). No `pull_request_target`. No `schedule`.
- Token: top-level `permissions: contents: read`. Only `preflip` widens it, at job level, to `contents: read`, `pull-requests: read` and `issues: read`.
- No `secrets.*` expression appears anywhere in the workflow. The automatic `GITHUB_TOKEN` is used implicitly by `actions/checkout` and `actions/setup-node`, and explicitly only in `preflip`, as `github.token` passed through `env:`.
- Event data, `head_ref` and `needs.*` values reach a script only through `env:`; no `run:` block contains `${{`.
- Every checkout sets `persist-credentials: false`.
- `defaults.run.shell: bash`.
- Every `npx` call passes `--no-install`.
- Concurrency, verbatim; no `queue:` key (actionlint 1.7.12 rejects it):

  ```yaml
  concurrency:
    group: ci-${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' && format('pr-{0}', github.event.pull_request.number) || format('run-{0}', github.run_id) }}
    cancel-in-progress: ${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' }}
  ```

- Reports go to `$RUNNER_TEMP`, outside the tree. The script is called as `node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs" …` from any working directory.
- `.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs` is a zero-dependency ES module (Node built-ins only) run on Node 24; its tests use `node:test` and `node:assert/strict`. No new npm dependency anywhere: the scratchpad tool `workflow-policy.mjs` imports yaml 2.9.1 by absolute path from `E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/web/node_modules/yaml`.
- Never `continue-on-error`. Never `forge build --locked`, `forge fmt --check`, the fork suite, `npm run docs:gen`, `npm run agent`, `npm run test:integration` or any script under `agent/scripts/`. `BASE_RPC_URL` stays unset (not empty) in CI and is never printed.
- Every gitleaks call passes `--redact`, `--no-banner`, `--ignore-gitleaks-allow`, `--exit-code 3`, `--report-format json` and `--report-path /out/<scan>.json`. Every gitleaks `docker run` mounts exactly `-v "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE:/repo:ro"` and `-v "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks:/out"`; `$RUNNER_TEMP` itself is never mounted. The actionlint `docker run` mounts only `-v "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE:/repo:ro"` (smallest choice: spec 4.4 states its two paths in the paragraph on the gitleaks image, whose runs need `/out` for their reports; actionlint writes no report, so it gets no write mount).
- All committed text is in English.
- Git: `git add -- <files>` only (never `git add -A`, `git add .` or `git commit -am`); `git commit -F <message file in the scratchpad run directory>`; every commit message ends with the line `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`; never `git stash`, `--no-verify` or `--amend`; never commit `grep.exe.stackdump`, `web/grep.exe.stackdump` or `docs/superpowers/roadmap/`.
- Commit-message files: each commit has its own file, `<run>/e5-commit-task-<N>.txt` (Task 1 uses `<run>/commit-message.txt`, which Task 9 Step 9 reuses for the merge; a second commit of a task adds a suffix, as File Structure lists). Before each Write of one, run `test -e <file> && echo "exists"`; if it prints `exists` (an earlier attempt or session wrote it), read the file with the Read tool first, because the Write tool overwrites only a file read in the same session. Its old text is not reused.
- Crash dumps: the ci worktree may hold an untracked Git Bash crash dump, `grep.exe.stackdump` at its root (and, if a crash writes one there, `web/grep.exe.stackdump`). No task deletes or commits it: deleting it is the owner's call (controller pre-step). So every local `git -C E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci status --porcelain …` and `… ls-files --others --exclude-standard` check ends with the pathspec `-- . ':(exclude)grep.exe.stackdump' ':(exclude)web/grep.exe.stackdump'`, which hides those two paths and prints every other line. Every expected output of such a check, and every "`git status --porcelain` prints nothing" in a Done-when list, means that form. The `git status --porcelain` inside `ci.yml`'s clean-tree steps runs on a fresh Linux checkout and has no pathspec.
- Commit prefixes (spec 8 step 1): `ci:` for the workflow and the counting script, `chore:` for the gitleaks and Dependabot config, `docs:` for the spec, the plan, `SECURITY.md` and the README and docs pages. The one exception is Task 8's red-proof commit, on a throwaway branch that is never merged, which uses `test:` (Task 8 says why).
- Owner-only: never push, open a PR, merge, close a PR or delete a remote branch without the owner's explicit approval in chat. Never call a paid API, never read `agent/.env` or any `.env` file, never print `BASE_RPC_URL`. A PR body ends with the line `🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)`.
- Secret safety (the plan and every commit are scanned by gitleaks): no `ghp_` followed by 36 characters, no `0x` followed by 64 hex digits, no assignment of a long random-looking string to a name containing key, token, secret, auth, access, password or credential, and no RPC URL with a key in its path, in any committed file. Test code that needs such a string builds it at run time (for example `"ghp_" + "a".repeat(36)`). The seven made-up stopwords of spec 6.1 are never written in the plan: Task 3's tool reads them from git and writes them only into `.gitleaks.toml`. The canary's four values are derived on the runner from seed strings with `sha256sum`. gitleaks fixtures put `REDACTED` in `Secret`, `Match` and `Line`. Scratchpad tools print rule names, lengths, paths and ok/FAIL, never a value.
- Windows and Linux: local runs are on Windows (Git Bash, Node v24.12.0, Foundry 1.9.0 at commit 6056c58), CI is Linux. The script builds every annotation path with forward slashes on both, never depends on CRLF or LF, and its tests create files only under `os.tmpdir()` with `mkdtempSync`.
- Local reports and commit-message files go under `C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/e5-plan-work/run`; scratch tools under `C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/e5-plan-work/tools`; never in the repository. No Docker and no Python locally; the canary and the scans first run in CI.

## Review Focus

1. **Windows path separators in annotations.** Input: a forge failure in suite `test/MandateVault.t.sol:MandateVaultTest` extracted with cwd `<workspace>/contracts`, and a Playwright failure in `smoke.spec.ts` with `config.rootDir` `<workspace>/web/e2e`, where `<workspace>` is a `mkdtempSync` directory under `os.tmpdir()` (backslashes on Windows). Expected: `file=contracts/test/MandateVault.t.sol` and `file=web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts` exactly, on Windows and on Linux (`path.relative`, then split on `path.sep` and joined with `/`). Pinned by Task 1's annotation-path tests.
2. **Variables unset by hand, or inherited from the runner.** Input: `extract` with no `GITHUB_OUTPUT`; `render` with no `GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY`; `extract` with no `STEP_OUTCOME` and a report path that does not exist; and the whole test file run inside `summary`, where the runner sets real `GITHUB_OUTPUT`, `GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` and `GITHUB_WORKSPACE`. Expected: `extract` exits 1 naming `GITHUB_OUTPUT` before it reads anything; `render` prints the table to stdout and exits 0; an unset `STEP_OUTCOME` counts as not `success` (so it writes `{"report":"missing"}` and exits 0, and Task 4's local runs always pass `STEP_OUTCOME=success`); the tests' `childEnv` helper deletes all seven variables before setting the ones a case names, so no test writes into the job's real output or summary. Pinned by Task 1 (`childEnv`, the unset `GITHUB_OUTPUT` and unset `STEP_OUTCOME` cases) and Task 2 (the unset `GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` case).
3. **gitleaks exit 0 or 3 with no usable report.** Input: `SCAN_EXIT=0` or `3` with the report file missing, empty, not JSON, or a JSON object instead of an array (a mistyped `/out` mount or `--report-path`). Expected: exit 1 and no output line, so the job fails and the table shows "no report", never "0 findings". Pinned by Task 2's gitleaks tests.
4. **Test titles and reasons that are not one clean line.** Input: a Playwright error message carrying ANSI colour codes, a reason whose first line is blank or that uses `\r\n`, and a Playwright title holding a newline. Expected: exactly one `::error` line per failed test; the reason is its first non-blank line, trimmed, with ANSI SGR codes removed; a CR or LF in a title or reason is escaped to `%0D` or `%0A`; nothing is truncated. Pinned by Task 1's escaping tests.
5. **`NEEDS_JSON` from a cancelled or timed-out run.** Input: a needed job with `"result":"cancelled"` and `"outputs":{}`, one with no `outputs` key, an output that is `""`, a job with result `skipped`, and an output that is not JSON. Expected: "no report" for an absent or empty output, "unreadable output" for one that does not parse, the job result printed as given, and exit 0; only a `NEEDS_JSON` that is unset, does not parse or is not a JSON object exits 1; the gate step, not `render`, fails the job. Pinned by Task 2's render tests.

## Shared names (defined once; every task uses exactly these)

### `ci-counts.mjs` command line

```text
node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs" extract <forge|vitest|playwright|gitleaks> <report.json> <output-name>
node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs" render
```

Any other argument list (unknown command, unknown kind, wrong count) prints `usage: ci-counts.mjs extract <forge|vitest|playwright|gitleaks> <report.json> <output-name> | render` to stderr and exits 1 (smallest choice: the spec defines only exits 0 and 1). Until Task 2 lands, `extract gitleaks` and `render` take this usage path.

### Environment variables it reads

| Variable | Read by | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `GITHUB_OUTPUT` | `extract` | File to append `<output-name>=<compact JSON>\n` to. Unset or empty: stderr `GITHUB_OUTPUT is not set`, exit 1, before the report is read (smallest choice: a lost output must not leave CI green). |
| `GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` | `render` | File to append the table to. Unset or empty: the table goes to stdout and the exit rule is unchanged (smallest choice that keeps spec 5.1's "exits 0 unless NEEDS_JSON does not parse"). |
| `GITHUB_WORKSPACE` | `extract forge`, `extract playwright` | Root that annotation paths are relative to. Unset or empty: stderr `GITHUB_WORKSPACE is not set`, exit 1, before the report is read (smallest choice: never print a path relative to the wrong root). |
| `STEP_OUTCOME` | `extract forge|vitest|playwright` | Outcome of the step that writes the report (`success`, `failure`, `cancelled`, `skipped`). Unset counts as not `success`. |
| `SCAN_EXIT` | `extract gitleaks` | gitleaks' exit code as text, from the scan step's `exit` output. |
| `COMMITS` | `extract gitleaks` | `git rev-list --all --count` as text, from the scan step's `commits` output; only the history scan sets it. |
| `NEEDS_JSON` | `render` | `${{ toJSON(needs) }}`: `{"contracts":{"result":"success","outputs":{"forge":"<json text>"}},"agent":{…},"web":{…},"hygiene":{…}}`. |

### Exact output JSON and exit codes of `extract`

Key order is fixed as written; values are JSON numbers; the line is `JSON.stringify` output (no spaces).

| Kind | Case | Line appended to `GITHUB_OUTPUT` | stdout | Exit |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| any | `GITHUB_OUTPUT` unset or empty | none | none | 1 |
| forge, playwright | `GITHUB_WORKSPACE` unset or empty | none | none | 1 |
| forge, vitest, playwright | report file absent (ENOENT) and `STEP_OUTCOME` not `success` | `<name>={"report":"missing"}` | none | 0 |
| forge, vitest, playwright | report file absent and `STEP_OUTCOME` is `success` | none | none | 1 |
| forge, vitest, playwright | report present but unreadable, empty, not JSON, or not the kind's shape | none | none | 1 |
| forge, vitest, playwright | valid report with zero tests (`passed + failed + skipped (+ flaky)` is 0), whatever `STEP_OUTCOME` is | none | none | 1 |
| forge | valid report | `<name>={"passed":P,"failed":F,"skipped":S}` | one `::error` line per `Failure` result | 0 |
| vitest | valid report | `<name>={"passed":P,"failed":F,"skipped":S}` | none (Vitest's own reporter annotates) | 0 |
| playwright | valid report | `<name>={"passed":P,"failed":F,"skipped":S,"flaky":K}` | one `::error` line per test whose `status` is `unexpected` | 0 |
| gitleaks | `SCAN_EXIT` unset or empty | `<name>={"report":"missing"}` | none | 0 |
| gitleaks | `SCAN_EXIT` neither `0` nor `3` | `<name>={"error":"scan"}` | none | 0 |
| gitleaks | `SCAN_EXIT` `0` or `3`, `COMMITS` set, non-empty and not `^\d+$` | none | none | 1 |
| gitleaks | `SCAN_EXIT` `0` or `3`, report absent, empty, not JSON or not an array | none | none | 1 (smallest choice: a scan that claims to have run must have a report, so it never shows as "0 findings") |
| gitleaks | `SCAN_EXIT` `0` or `3`, `COMMITS` unset or empty | `<name>={"findings":N}` | none | 0 |
| gitleaks | `SCAN_EXIT` `0` or `3`, `COMMITS` a decimal integer | `<name>={"findings":N,"commits":M}` | none | 0 |

Counting rules: forge counts every `test_results[*].status` (`Success`, `Failure`, `Skipped`) across all suites; vitest takes `numPassedTests`, `numFailedTests`, and `numPendingTests + numTodoTests` as skipped; playwright takes `stats.expected`, `stats.unexpected`, `stats.skipped`, `stats.flaky`; gitleaks takes the report array's length.

Shape rules (anything else is "not the kind's shape"): forge, a non-array object whose every key holds a `:` after a non-empty path and whose every value has an object `test_results` mapping names to objects with `status` in `Success`, `Failure`, `Skipped` and `reason` a string, `null` or absent; vitest, an object whose `numPassedTests`, `numFailedTests`, `numPendingTests` and `numTodoTests` are non-negative integers; playwright, an object with `config.rootDir` a string, `suites` an array, and `stats.expected`, `stats.unexpected`, `stats.skipped`, `stats.flaky` non-negative integers.

Annotation line: `::error file=<escapeProperty(file)>::<escapeData(reason === "" ? title : title + ": " + reason)>`.

- forge: for suite key `<suitePath>:<Contract>` (split at the last `:`) and failed test `<name>`, `file = workspacePath(GITHUB_WORKSPACE, path.join(process.cwd(), suitePath))`, `title = "<Contract> › <name>"`, `reason = firstLine(result.reason ?? "")`.
- playwright: for spec `s` under file suite `f`, `file = workspacePath(GITHUB_WORKSPACE, path.join(report.config.rootDir, s.file))`, `title = "<s.file> › " + [...titles of the describe suites between f and s, s.title].join(" › ")`, `reason = firstLine(message of the last result whose error.message is a string, or "")`.
- `firstLine` removes ANSI SGR codes (`/\u001b\[[0-9;]*m/g`), splits on `\r\n`, `\r` or `\n`, and returns the first line that is not blank, trimmed, or `""` (smallest choice: a reason that starts with a line break would otherwise give an empty message, and raw escape codes would reach the annotation).

### Exact output of `render`

Appended to `GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` (or written to stdout when it is unset or empty), every line ending in `\n`:

```text
| Check | Job result | Report |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Contracts, unit | <needs.contracts.result> | <cell of outputs.forge, counts> |
| Agent, unit | <needs.agent.result> | <cell of outputs.unit, counts> |
| Web, unit | <needs.web.result> | <cell of outputs.unit, counts> |
| Web, end to end | <needs.web.result> | <cell of outputs.e2e, counts with flaky> |
| Secret scan, history | <needs.hygiene.result> | <cell of outputs.history, scan with commits> |
| Secret scan, commit messages | <needs.hygiene.result> | <cell of outputs.messages, scan> |
| Secret scan, canary | <needs.hygiene.result> | <outputs.canary, outcome> |
| Workflow lint | <needs.hygiene.result> | <outputs.actionlint, outcome> |

Offline suites only. The contracts fork suite, the agent fork integration suite and Lighthouse need an archive RPC or a browser audit and run by hand; see README.md, Development.
```

(Smallest choice for the layout: one three-column table, one row per suite and scan, the footer of spec 5.2 verbatim after one blank line. The Report cell carries the same values spec 5.1 lists for each suite row, passed, failed and skipped, and flaky for end to end, as separate numbers in that fixed order, so no column stays empty in the scan and outcome rows.)

Cells: job result is `needs.<job>.result` when it is a non-empty string, else `unknown`. An output that is absent, `null` or `""` gives `no report`. Otherwise the output text is parsed: `{"report":"missing"}` gives `no report`; `{"error":"scan"}` gives `scan failed`; counts give `P passed, F failed, S skipped` (the end-to-end row adds `, K flaky` and needs `flaky`); a scan gives `N findings`, and the history row adds ` over M commits` when `commits` is present; text that does not parse, or any other shape, gives `unreadable output`. Outcome rows print the output string itself when it is non-empty, else `no report`. Exit: 1 (stderr `NEEDS_JSON does not parse`, nothing written) when `NEEDS_JSON` is unset, does not parse, or is not a non-array object; 0 otherwise.

### Exported functions (`.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs`)

```js
/** @typedef {{ passed: number, failed: number, skipped: number }} TestCounts */
/** @typedef {{ passed: number, failed: number, skipped: number, flaky: number }} E2eCounts */
/** @typedef {{ findings: number, commits?: number }} ScanCounts */
/** @typedef {{ report: "missing" }} MissingReport */
/** @typedef {{ error: "scan" }} ScanError */
/** @typedef {{ file: string, title: string, reason: string }} Failure  file: relative to GITHUB_WORKSPACE, forward slashes; reason: already firstLine'd */
/** @typedef {{ STEP_OUTCOME?: string, SCAN_EXIT?: string, COMMITS?: string, GITHUB_WORKSPACE?: string }} ExtractEnv */
/** @typedef {{ code: 0 | 1, output?: TestCounts | E2eCounts | ScanCounts | MissingReport | ScanError, annotations: string[], message?: string }} ExtractResult */

/** Thrown by the read* functions when a report is not its kind's shape. */
export class ReportError extends Error {}

/** @param {string} value @returns {string} `%`, `\r`, `\n` escaped as `%25`, `%0D`, `%0A`. */
export function escapeData(value) {}

/** @param {string} value @returns {string} escapeData plus `:` as `%3A` and `,` as `%2C`. */
export function escapeProperty(value) {}

/** @param {string} reason @returns {string} */
export function firstLine(reason) {}

/** @param {string} workspace @param {string} absolutePath @returns {string} path.relative, then split(path.sep).join("/"). */
export function workspacePath(workspace, absolutePath) {}

/** @param {Failure} failure @returns {string} one `::error` line, no trailing newline. */
export function annotation(failure) {}

/** @param {unknown} report @param {{ workspace: string, cwd: string }} where @returns {{ counts: TestCounts, failures: Failure[] }} @throws {ReportError} */
export function readForge(report, where) {}

/** @param {unknown} report @returns {{ counts: TestCounts, failures: Failure[] }} failures is always []. @throws {ReportError} */
export function readVitest(report) {}

/** @param {unknown} report @param {{ workspace: string }} where @returns {{ counts: E2eCounts, failures: Failure[] }} @throws {ReportError} */
export function readPlaywright(report, where) {}

/** Task 2. @param {string | null} reportText null when the file is absent @param {{ scanExit?: string, commits?: string }} scan @returns {ScanCounts | MissingReport | ScanError} @throws {ReportError} */
export function readGitleaks(reportText, scan) {}

/** @param {"forge" | "vitest" | "playwright" | "gitleaks"} kind @param {string | null} reportText null when the file is absent @param {ExtractEnv} env @param {string} cwd @returns {ExtractResult} (gitleaks from Task 2) */
export function extractResult(kind, reportText, env, cwd) {}

/** Task 2. @param {string | undefined} needsJson @returns {{ code: 0 | 1, markdown?: string, message?: string }} */
export function renderSummary(needsJson) {}

/** Parses argv, checks GITHUB_OUTPUT (and GITHUB_WORKSPACE for forge and playwright), reads the report (ENOENT gives null; any other read error exits 1), calls extractResult or renderSummary, writes the output line with appendFileSync, writes annotations to stdout and messages to stderr.
 * @param {string[]} argv @param {NodeJS.ProcessEnv} env @param {string} cwd @returns {0 | 1} */
export function main(argv, env, cwd) {}

// Runs only when invoked directly (import.meta.main exists from Node 24.2.0; local v24.12.0, CI v24.22.3):
if (import.meta.main) process.exitCode = main(process.argv.slice(2), process.env, process.cwd());
```

### `ci.yml` jobs, step ids, outputs and needs

Top matter: `name: CI`, the triggers, `permissions`, `concurrency` and `defaults` of the Global Constraints (smallest choice for `name`: the badge's label). No job sets `name:`.

| Job | runs-on / timeout | Needs / if | Step ids, in order | Job outputs |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `contracts` | `ubuntu-24.04` / 15 | none | checkout (`submodules: true`, default depth), foundry-toolchain, setup-node, `version`, `build`, `test`, `counts` (`if: ${{ !cancelled() }}`, env `STEP_OUTCOME: ${{ steps.test.outcome }}`, `extract forge "$RUNNER_TEMP/forge.json" forge`), `clean`; `defaults.run.working-directory: contracts` | `forge: ${{ steps.counts.outputs.forge }}` |
| `agent` | `ubuntu-24.04` / 10 | none | checkout, setup-node (`cache: npm`, `cache-dependency-path: agent/package-lock.json`), `install`, `typecheck`, `test`, `counts` (`!cancelled()`, `STEP_OUTCOME: ${{ steps.test.outcome }}`, `extract vitest "$RUNNER_TEMP/agent-unit.json" unit`), `clean`; working directory `agent` | `unit: ${{ steps.counts.outputs.unit }}` |
| `web` | `ubuntu-24.04` / 30 for the first runs (Task 9 Step 27 sets the measured value) | none | checkout (`fetch-depth: 1`), setup-node (`cache: npm`, `cache-dependency-path: web/package-lock.json`), `install`, `typecheck`, `unit`, `build`, `check_out`, `browsers`, `e2e` (env `PLAYWRIGHT_JSON_OUTPUT_FILE: ${{ runner.temp }}/e2e.json`), `unit_counts` (`!cancelled()`, `STEP_OUTCOME: ${{ steps.unit.outcome }}`, `extract vitest "$RUNNER_TEMP/web-unit.json" unit`), `e2e_counts` (`!cancelled()`, `STEP_OUTCOME: ${{ steps.e2e.outcome }}`, `extract playwright "$RUNNER_TEMP/e2e.json" e2e`), `traces` (`if: ${{ failure() }}`, upload-artifact `name: e2e-traces`, `path: web/test-results/`, `retention-days: 7`), `clean`; working directory `web`; job env `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL: https://maatonbase.xyz`, `NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED: "1"`, `NO_UPDATE_CHECK: "1"` | `unit: ${{ steps.unit_counts.outputs.unit }}`, `e2e: ${{ steps.e2e_counts.outputs.e2e }}` |
| `hygiene` | `ubuntu-24.04` / 10 | none | checkout (`fetch-depth: 0`), setup-node, `outdir`, `ignorefile`, `canary`, `scan` (`!cancelled()`, writes `exit=` and `commits=` to `$GITHUB_OUTPUT`), `messages` (`!cancelled()`, writes `exit=`), `history_counts` (`!cancelled()`, env `SCAN_EXIT: ${{ steps.scan.outputs.exit }}`, `COMMITS: ${{ steps.scan.outputs.commits }}`, `extract gitleaks "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/history.json" history`), `messages_counts` (`!cancelled()`, env `SCAN_EXIT: ${{ steps.messages.outputs.exit }}`, `extract gitleaks "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/messages.json" messages`), `actionlint` (`!cancelled()`) | `history: ${{ steps.history_counts.outputs.history }}`, `messages: ${{ steps.messages_counts.outputs.messages }}`, `canary: ${{ steps.canary.outcome }}`, `actionlint: ${{ steps.actionlint.outcome }}` |
| `summary` | `ubuntu-slim` / 5 | `needs: [contracts, agent, web, hygiene]`, `if: ${{ always() }}` | checkout (`fetch-depth: 1`, `sparse-checkout-cone-mode: false`, `sparse-checkout: /.github/` and `/web/package.json`), `tests` (`always()`, `node --test ".github/scripts/*.test.mjs"`), `render` (`always()`, env `NEEDS_JSON: ${{ toJSON(needs) }}`), `gate` (`always()`, env `CONTRACTS_RESULT: ${{ needs.contracts.result }}`, `AGENT_RESULT: ${{ needs.agent.result }}`, `WEB_RESULT: ${{ needs.web.result }}`, `HYGIENE_RESULT: ${{ needs.hygiene.result }}`; exits 1 unless all four are `success`) | none |
| `preflip` | `ubuntu-24.04` / 15 | `if: ${{ github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' && inputs.pre_flip }}`; job `permissions: contents: read, pull-requests: read, issues: read`; not in `summary`'s needs | checkout (`fetch-depth: 0`), `outdir`, `pulls` (env `TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}`, the `GIT_CONFIG_*` fetch of `+refs/pull/*:refs/remotes/pull/*`), `ignorefile`, `scan`, `messages`, `text` (env `GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}`, `gh api --paginate` into `$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/text/`), `text_scan` | none |

Paths inside the gitleaks container (host `$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/<x>` is `/out/<x>`): canary repository `/out/canary`, scanned text `/out/text` (commit messages in `/out/text/commit-messages.txt`), reports `/out/canary.json`, `/out/history.json`, `/out/messages.json`, and in `preflip` also `/out/text.json`. The repository is `/repo` (read-only); every scan passes `--config /repo/.gitleaks.toml`.

Canary seeds and derivation (shared by `ci.yml` and `canary-check.mjs`): `h(seed)` = `printf '%s' '<seed>' | sha256sum | cut -c1-64` (64 lowercase hex digits).

| Seed | Planted text | Rule that must fire |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `maat-ci-canary-evm-private-key` | `canary.txt` line `PRIVATE_KEY=0x<h>` | `maat-evm-private-key` (at least one) |
| `maat-ci-canary-github-pat` | `canary.txt` line `ghp_<first 36 of h>` | `github-pat` (with the next row, exactly two) |
| `maat-ci-canary-github-pat-allow` | `canary.txt` line `ghp_<first 36 of h> # gitleaks:allow` | `github-pat` |
| `maat-ci-canary-agent-fixture` | `agent/test/x.test.ts` line `const KEY = "<first 40 of h>";` | `generic-api-key` with `File` `agent/test/x.test.ts` (at least one) |

The canary step fails unless gitleaks exits exactly 3 and `/out/canary.json` holds those findings; any other finding is allowed. (The seeds were pre-checked on 2026-11-18 against the default stopwords of `gl-default-v8.30.1.toml`: no stopword inside any derived value; Task 5's tool re-checks them and the seven. If the re-check finds a stopword in a value, Task 5 Step 3 changes that seed by a fixed suffix, as spec 4.4 asks, and the seed in this table is then read with that suffix.)

| --- | --- | --- |

| `node <tools>/gl-history-check.mjs E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci` | `findings=<n>`, then `<commit8> <path>:<line> <rule> len=<n>` per finding | 0 when n is 0 with the `.gitleaks.toml` allowlist applied (condition AND: path regex and stopword) |
| `node <tools>/gl-history-check.mjs E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci --no-allowlist` | the same lines | 0 when there are exactly 7 findings, all `generic-api-key` |
| `node <tools>/workflow-policy.mjs E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/.github/workflows/ci.yml` | `ok <check>` or `FAIL <check>: <detail>` per check, then `policy: <a> ok, <b> failed` | 0 when b is 0 |
| `node <tools>/canary-check.mjs E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/.github/workflows/ci.yml E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/.gitleaks.toml` | per seed `<rule> len=<n> seed-in-workflow=ok|FAIL sha256sum=ok|FAIL stopword=ok|FAIL rule=ok|FAIL`, for the fixture also `seven=ok|FAIL`, then `canary: ok` or `canary: FAIL` | 0 on `canary: ok` |

Both gitleaks tools walk `git -C <repo> log -p -U0 --all --full-history --diff-merges=first-parent --diff-filter=tuxdb` (the CI scan's log options; with `--diff-merges=first-parent` each merge's diff against its first parent is included) and skip paths in the default config's global allowlist (among them `gitleaks\.toml`).

**A finding from another local branch.** `--all` reads every local ref, not only `chore/ci`: also the still active, local-only `feat/llm-providers` and `refs/stash`, which all worktrees share. When a `gl-history-check.mjs` run that a step expects to print `findings=0` prints a finding, run `git -C E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci merge-base --is-ancestor <commit8> chore/ci; echo "exit=$?"` for each printed `<commit8>`. `exit=1` means the finding comes from another local branch, not from the task: stop, change no file of the task, and ask the owner exactly Task 3 Step 6's question, ending it with "before I go on?" instead of "before I write the stopwords?", with the tool's lines pasted in. `exit=0` means the finding is on `chore/ci`: stop and report it as that step says. (Smallest choice: the spec is silent on local-only branches outside `chore/ci`.)

`gl-stopwords.mjs` exports (used by `gl-history-check.mjs` and `canary-check.mjs`):

### Controller pre-step: the crash dump, before the plan commit and Task 1

The ci worktree holds an untracked Git Bash crash dump, `grep.exe.stackdump` (1013 bytes, 2026-11-18), at its root. `.gitignore` does not cover it, and it is not part of this plan. Deleting it is the owner's call, so the controller asks once, before the plan commit, exactly this, and waits for the answer:

> The ci worktree has an untracked Git Bash crash dump, grep.exe.stackdump, at its root. It is not part of the plan. May I delete it with `rm E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/grep.exe.stackdump`?

On a yes, run:

```bash
rm E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/grep.exe.stackdump
git -C E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci status --porcelain
```

On a no, leave the file where it is and run only:

```bash
git -C E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci status --porcelain -- . ':(exclude)grep.exe.stackdump' ':(exclude)web/grep.exe.stackdump'
```

Expected either way: exactly one line, `?? docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-18-maat-ci.md`. Then the controller commits the plan (prefix `docs:`), after which the same command prints nothing. Every later clean-tree check carries the crash-dump pathspec of the Global Constraints, so a dump that a later Git Bash crash writes again never stops a step.

- Scratch, never committed: `C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/e5-plan-work/run/task-1-github-output.txt` (Step 29) and the commit message `C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/e5-plan-work/run/commit-message.txt` (Step 30).
- Test: `node --test --test-reporter=spec E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/.github/scripts/ci-counts.test.mjs`

**Interfaces** (copied from the plan's Shared names; binding):
- Consumes: the report shapes of `scratchpad/forge-test.json` (suite key `path:Contract`, `test_results[name].status` and `reason`), `scratchpad/vitest-agent.json` (the `num*Tests` totals) and `JSONReport` in `web/node_modules/playwright/types/testReporter.d.ts` (`config.rootDir`, `suites[].specs[].tests[].status`, `results[].error.message`, `stats`); none copied whole.
- Produces: `ReportError`, `escapeData`, `escapeProperty`, `firstLine`, `workspacePath`, `annotation`, `readForge`, `readVitest`, `readPlaywright`, `extractResult` (forge, vitest, playwright), `main` (extract only), the direct-invocation guard, the test helpers `SCRIPT`, `FIXTURES`, `OWN_VARS`, `childEnv`, `runCli`, `tempDir`, `outputLines`, the fixture guard with `KEY_SHAPES`, and the 15 fixtures, all exactly as in Shared names.
- Commit: `ci: count the forge, vitest and playwright reports`.

**Choices where the spec and Shared names are silent** (each the smallest that satisfies them):
- Below the top-level shape rules of Shared names, the Playwright walk reads a missing or mistyped list as empty and a missing `title` or `file` as `""`, so only those shape rules decide an exit 1.
- The stderr texts beyond the three fixed ones (`usage: …`, `GITHUB_OUTPUT is not set`, `GITHUB_WORKSPACE is not set`) are: `cannot read <path>: <error>`, `the <kind> report is missing, but the step that writes it succeeded`, `the <kind> report is not JSON: <error>`, `the <kind> report is not a <kind> report: <error>` and `the <kind> report holds zero tests`.
- `extractResult` called directly with a kind it does not know returns `{ code: 1, annotations: [], message: <usage> }`; `main` never passes one.
- The fixtures take their test titles from the repository's suites, except the todo title in `vitest-skip.json` and the describe block in `playwright-fail.json`, which are made up; their numbers (gas, durations, times) are made up.
- The tests leave their `mkdtempSync` directories under `os.tmpdir()`, 44 a run; CI runners are discarded, and locally they can be removed with `rm -rf "$(node -p "require('os').tmpdir()")"/ci-counts-*`. No cleanup hook is added.
- Steps 6 to 28 name the reporter (`--test-reporter=spec`) so that the expected summary lines are the same on every terminal, except Step 28's CI form, which is the `summary` job's command word for word (Node v24.12.0's `node:test` prints the same `ℹ` summary lines there, even with stdout piped).

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the five vitest fixtures**

Shape of Vitest 5.1.0's JSON reporter (research note 02 section 6; real sample `scratchpad/vitest-agent.json`): the `num*` totals, `snapshot`, `startTime`, `success` and `testResults[].assertionResults[]`. The suite totals count each file and each `describe` block, so one file with one `describe` gives `numTotalTestSuites: 2`, and one failed test in it fails both. The script reads none of the suite totals.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the first tests: the helpers, escaping, `firstLine`, `workspacePath`, `childEnv` and the fixture guard**

Create `.github/scripts/ci-counts.test.mjs`:

```js
// Tests for ci-counts.mjs. Run with `node --test`, locally and in the CI summary job.
import { describe, test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { annotation, escapeData, escapeProperty, firstLine, workspacePath } from "./ci-counts.mjs";

const SCRIPT = fileURLToPath(new URL("./ci-counts.mjs", import.meta.url));
const FIXTURES = fileURLToPath(new URL("./fixtures/", import.meta.url));
const OWN_VARS = ["GITHUB_OUTPUT", "GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY", "GITHUB_WORKSPACE", "STEP_OUTCOME", "SCAN_EXIT", "COMMITS", "NEEDS_JSON"];
// A GitHub token, a 32-byte hex value, and an RPC URL with a provider's key in its path (spec 5.3).
const KEY_SHAPES = [
  /ghp_[0-9A-Za-z]{36}/,
  /0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}/,
  /https:\/\/[a-z0-9.-]*(?:alchemy\.com\/v2|infura\.io\/v3|quiknode\.pro|api\.developer\.coinbase\.com\/rpc\/v1\/[a-z-]+)\/[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}/i,
];

/** @param {Record<string, string>} overrides @returns {NodeJS.ProcessEnv} process.env minus OWN_VARS, plus overrides */
function childEnv(overrides) {
  const env = { ...process.env };
  for (const name of OWN_VARS) delete env[name];
  return { ...env, ...overrides };
}

/**
 * @param {string[]} args @param {{ env?: Record<string, string>, cwd?: string }} [options]
 * @returns {{ status: number | null, stdout: string, stderr: string }}
 */
function runCli(args, options = {}) {
  const run = spawnSync(process.execPath, [SCRIPT, ...args], { cwd: options.cwd, env: childEnv(options.env ?? {}), encoding: "utf8" });
  return { status: run.status, stdout: run.stdout, stderr: run.stderr };
}

/** @returns {string} a new empty directory under the OS temp directory */
function tempDir() {
  return mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "ci-counts-"));
}

/** @param {string} file @returns {string[]} the file's non-empty lines, split on /\r?\n/ */
function outputLines(file) {
  return readFileSync(file, "utf8").split(/\r?\n/).filter((line) => line !== "");
}

describe("annotation escaping", () => {
  test("escapeData escapes %, CR and LF and leaves : and , alone", () => {
    assert.equal(escapeData("100% done\r\nnext: a,b"), "100%25 done%0D%0Anext: a,b");
  });

  test("escapeProperty also escapes : and ,", () => {
    assert.equal(escapeProperty("a%b\r\nc:d,e"), "a%25b%0D%0Ac%3Ad%2Ce");
  });

  test("a test name with , : % and :: and a reason over two lines give one line", () => {
    const line = annotation({
      file: "contracts/test/Odd,Name:1%.t.sol",
      title: "OddTest › test_a,b:c%d::e()",
      reason: firstLine("first line of the reason\nsecond line"),
    });
    assert.equal(line, "::error file=contracts/test/Odd%2CName%3A1%25.t.sol::OddTest › test_a,b:c%25d::e(): first line of the reason");
  });

  test("a line break in a title is escaped, never printed", () => {
    const line = annotation({ file: "web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts", title: "smoke.spec.ts › two\nlines\r", reason: "" });
    assert.equal(line, "::error file=web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts::smoke.spec.ts › two%0Alines%0D");
    assert.doesNotMatch(line, /[\r\n]/);
  });

  test("an empty reason leaves the title on its own", () => {
    assert.equal(annotation({ file: "agent/x.ts", title: "a title", reason: "" }), "::error file=agent/x.ts::a title");
  });
});

describe("firstLine", () => {
  test("gives the first line that is not blank, trimmed", () => {
    assert.equal(firstLine("\n   \r\n  assertion failed: 0 != 1  \nnext line"), "assertion failed: 0 != 1");
  });

  test("splits on CRLF, CR and LF alike", () => {
    for (const reason of ["one\r\ntwo", "one\rtwo", "one\ntwo"]) assert.equal(firstLine(reason), "one", JSON.stringify(reason));
  });

  test("removes ANSI colour codes", () => {
    assert.equal(firstLine("\u001b[31mError:\u001b[39m expect(\u001b[2mlocator\u001b[22m).toBeVisible()"), "Error: expect(locator).toBeVisible()");
  });

  test("gives an empty string when every line is blank", () => {
    for (const reason of ["", " \n\t\r\n", "\u001b[31m\u001b[39m"]) assert.equal(firstLine(reason), "", JSON.stringify(reason));
  });

  test("never truncates a long line", () => {
    const long = "x".repeat(5000);
    assert.equal(firstLine(long), long);
  });
});

describe("workspacePath", () => {
  test("is relative to the workspace and uses forward slashes on every OS", () => {
    const workspace = tempDir();
    const file = path.join(workspace, "contracts", "test", "MandateVault.t.sol");
    assert.equal(workspacePath(workspace, file), "contracts/test/MandateVault.t.sol");
    // A Windows shell may spell the workspace with forward slashes; on Linux this is the same string.
    assert.equal(workspacePath(workspace.split(path.sep).join("/"), file), "contracts/test/MandateVault.t.sol");
  });
});

describe("childEnv", () => {
  test("drops the runner's own variables, then sets the ones a case names", () => {
    const saved = OWN_VARS.map((name) => [name, process.env[name]]);
    try {
      for (const name of OWN_VARS) process.env[name] = "set-by-the-runner";
      const env = childEnv({ STEP_OUTCOME: "success" });
      assert.equal(env.STEP_OUTCOME, "success");
      for (const name of OWN_VARS.filter((own) => own !== "STEP_OUTCOME")) assert.equal(env[name], undefined, name);
    } finally {
      for (const [name, value] of saved) {
        if (value === undefined) delete process.env[name];
        else process.env[name] = value;
      }
    }
  });
});

describe("fixture guard", () => {
  test("the key shapes catch values built at run time", () => {
    assert.match("ghp_" + "a".repeat(36), KEY_SHAPES[0]);
    assert.match("0x" + "ab".repeat(32), KEY_SHAPES[1]);
    assert.match("https://base-mainnet.g.alchemy.com/v2/" + "A".repeat(20), KEY_SHAPES[2]);
  });

  test("no fixture holds a key-shaped string", () => {
    const names = readdirSync(FIXTURES);
    assert.ok(names.length >= 15, `${names.length} fixtures`);
    for (const name of names) {
      const text = readFileSync(path.join(FIXTURES, name), "utf8");
      // assert.ok, so that a failure names the file and the shape but never prints the matching text.
      for (const shape of KEY_SHAPES) assert.ok(!shape.test(text), `${name} holds a string shaped like ${shape}`);
    }
  });

  test("every gitleaks fixture finding has REDACTED in Secret, Match and Line", () => {
    for (const name of readdirSync(FIXTURES).filter((file) => /^gitleaks-.*\.json$/.test(file))) {
      for (const finding of JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(FIXTURES, name), "utf8"))) {
        for (const field of ["Secret", "Match", "Line"]) assert.ok(finding[field] === "REDACTED", `${name}: ${field}`);
      }
    }
  });
});
```

The gitleaks check finds no `gitleaks-*.json` file until Task 2 adds two, so it passes vacuously here.

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the escaping, `firstLine`, `workspacePath` and `annotation` functions**

Create `.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs`:

```js
// The counting script of the CI workflow (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-18-maat-ci-design.md, section 5.1).
// `extract` turns one runner's JSON report into one compact job output and annotates the failed tests.
// Zero dependencies: Node 24 built-ins only.
import path from "node:path";

/** @typedef {{ passed: number, failed: number, skipped: number }} TestCounts */
/** @typedef {{ passed: number, failed: number, skipped: number, flaky: number }} E2eCounts */
/** @typedef {{ findings: number, commits?: number }} ScanCounts */
/** @typedef {{ report: "missing" }} MissingReport */
/** @typedef {{ error: "scan" }} ScanError */
/** @typedef {{ file: string, title: string, reason: string }} Failure file: relative to GITHUB_WORKSPACE, forward slashes; reason: already firstLine'd */
/** @typedef {{ STEP_OUTCOME?: string, SCAN_EXIT?: string, COMMITS?: string, GITHUB_WORKSPACE?: string }} ExtractEnv */
/** @typedef {{ code: 0 | 1, output?: TestCounts | E2eCounts | ScanCounts | MissingReport | ScanError, annotations: string[], message?: string }} ExtractResult */

/** ANSI SGR colour codes, which Playwright writes into its error messages. */
const ANSI_SGR = /\u001b\[[0-9;]*m/g;

/** Thrown by the read* functions when a report is not its kind's shape. */
export class ReportError extends Error {}

/** @param {string} value @returns {string} `%`, `\r`, `\n` escaped as `%25`, `%0D`, `%0A`. */
export function escapeData(value) {
  return value.replaceAll("%", "%25").replaceAll("\r", "%0D").replaceAll("\n", "%0A");
}

/** @param {string} value @returns {string} escapeData plus `:` as `%3A` and `,` as `%2C`. */
export function escapeProperty(value) {
  return escapeData(value).replaceAll(":", "%3A").replaceAll(",", "%2C");
}

/**
 * The first line of a failure reason that is not blank, trimmed and without ANSI SGR codes; "" when there is none.
 * @param {string} reason @returns {string}
 */
export function firstLine(reason) {
  const lines = reason.replace(ANSI_SGR, "").split(/\r\n|\r|\n/);
  return lines.map((line) => line.trim()).find((line) => line !== "") ?? "";
}

/** @param {string} workspace @param {string} absolutePath @returns {string} path.relative, then split(path.sep).join("/"). */
export function workspacePath(workspace, absolutePath) {
  return path.relative(workspace, absolutePath).split(path.sep).join("/");
}

/** @param {Failure} failure @returns {string} one `::error` line, no trailing newline. */
export function annotation(failure) {
  const message = failure.reason === "" ? failure.title : `${failure.title}: ${failure.reason}`;
  return `::error file=${escapeProperty(failure.file)}::${escapeData(message)}`;
}
```

```js
describe("readForge", () => {
  const forgeFixture = (name) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(FIXTURES, name), "utf8"));
  // The contracts step runs in <workspace>/contracts.
  const where = (workspace) => ({ workspace, cwd: path.join(workspace, "contracts") });

  test("counts every test result in every suite", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(readForge(forgeFixture("forge-pass.json"), where(tempDir())), { counts: { passed: 3, failed: 0, skipped: 0 }, failures: [] });
    assert.deepEqual(readForge(forgeFixture("forge-skip.json"), where(tempDir())), { counts: { passed: 1, failed: 0, skipped: 1 }, failures: [] });
  });

  test("names each failure by contract and test, in a file relative to the workspace", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(readForge(forgeFixture("forge-fail.json"), where(tempDir())), {
      counts: { passed: 1, failed: 1, skipped: 0 },
      failures: [{ file: "contracts/test/MandateVault.t.sol", title: "MandateVaultTest › test_refusesStaleFeed()", reason: "assertion failed: 0 != 1" }],
    });
  });

  test("a failure with no reason, or a null one, keeps an empty reason", () => {
    const report = {
      "test/GuardLib.t.sol:GuardLibTest": { test_results: { "test_paused()": { status: "Failure" }, "test_okReturnsOraclePrice()": { status: "Failure", reason: null } } },
    };
    assert.deepEqual(readForge(report, where(tempDir())).failures.map((failure) => failure.reason), ["", ""]);
  });

  test("throws a ReportError for anything that is not a forge report", () => {
    const workspace = tempDir();
    const suite = (results) => ({ "test/GuardLib.t.sol:GuardLibTest": { test_results: results } });
    const cases = [
      null,
      [],
      "a string",
      { "test/GuardLib.t.sol": { test_results: {} } },
      { ":GuardLibTest": { test_results: {} } },
      { "test/GuardLib.t.sol:GuardLibTest": {} },
      suite([]),
      suite({ "test_paused()": null }),
      suite({ "test_paused()": { status: "Passed" } }),
      suite({ "test_paused()": { status: "Success", reason: 7 } }),
    ];
    cases.forEach((report, index) => assert.throws(() => readForge(report, where(workspace)), ReportError, `case ${index}`));
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Write `readForge`**

In `.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs`, replace the end of the file:

```js

```

with:

```js
  return `::error file=${escapeProperty(failure.file)}::${escapeData(message)}`;
}

/** @param {unknown} value @returns {boolean} true for a non-null object that is not an array */
const isObject = (value) => typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
/** Joins the suite, file or describe titles and the test's title in an annotation. */
const TITLE_SEPARATOR = " › ";
const FORGE_STATUSES = new Set(["Success", "Failure", "Skipped"]);

/**
 * A `forge test --json-file` report: one object keyed by `<path>:<Contract>`, each suite holding `test_results`.
 * @param {unknown} report @param {{ workspace: string, cwd: string }} where
 * @returns {{ counts: TestCounts, failures: Failure[] }} @throws {ReportError}
 */
export function readForge(report, where) {
  if (!isObject(report)) throw new ReportError("a forge report is an object keyed by suite");
  const counts = { passed: 0, failed: 0, skipped: 0 };
  const failures = [];
  for (const [suite, result] of Object.entries(report)) {
    const colon = suite.lastIndexOf(":");
    if (colon < 1) throw new ReportError(`suite key ${JSON.stringify(suite)} is not <path>:<Contract>`);
    if (!isObject(result) || !isObject(result.test_results)) throw new ReportError(`suite ${suite} has no test_results object`);
    const suitePath = suite.slice(0, colon);
    const contract = suite.slice(colon + 1);
    for (const [name, test] of Object.entries(result.test_results)) {
      if (!isObject(test) || !FORGE_STATUSES.has(test.status)) throw new ReportError(`test ${name} in ${suite} has no known status`);
      if (test.reason !== undefined && test.reason !== null && typeof test.reason !== "string") {
        throw new ReportError(`test ${name} in ${suite} has a reason that is not text`);
      }
      if (test.status === "Success") {
        counts.passed += 1;
      } else if (test.status === "Skipped") {
        counts.skipped += 1;
      } else {
        counts.failed += 1;
        failures.push({
          file: workspacePath(where.workspace, path.join(where.cwd, suitePath)),
          title: `${contract}${TITLE_SEPARATOR}${name}`,
          reason: firstLine(test.reason ?? ""),
        });
      }
    }
  }
  return { counts, failures };
}
```

```js
describe("readVitest", () => {
  const vitestFixture = (name) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(FIXTURES, name), "utf8"));

  test("takes the totals, with pending and todo tests as skipped, and no failures", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(readVitest(vitestFixture("vitest-pass.json")), { counts: { passed: 3, failed: 0, skipped: 0 }, failures: [] });
    assert.deepEqual(readVitest(vitestFixture("vitest-fail.json")), { counts: { passed: 2, failed: 1, skipped: 0 }, failures: [] });
    assert.deepEqual(readVitest(vitestFixture("vitest-skip.json")), { counts: { passed: 2, failed: 0, skipped: 2 }, failures: [] });
  });

  test("throws a ReportError unless the four totals are non-negative integers", () => {
    const totals = { numPassedTests: 1, numFailedTests: 0, numPendingTests: 0, numTodoTests: 0 };
    assert.doesNotThrow(() => readVitest(totals));
    const cases = [
      null,
      [],
      vitestFixture("vitest-malformed.json"),
      { ...totals, numFailedTests: -1 },
      { ...totals, numPendingTests: 1.5 },
      { ...totals, numTodoTests: "0" },
    ];
    cases.forEach((report, index) => assert.throws(() => readVitest(report), ReportError, `case ${index}`));
  });
});
```

```js
  return { counts, failures };
```

```js
/** @param {unknown} value @returns {boolean} true for a non-negative integer */
const isCount = (value) => Number.isInteger(value) && value >= 0;
const VITEST_TOTALS = ["numPassedTests", "numFailedTests", "numPendingTests", "numTodoTests"];

/**
 * A Vitest JSON report. Vitest's own github-actions reporter annotates its failures, so this gives none.
 * @param {unknown} report @returns {{ counts: TestCounts, failures: Failure[] }} failures is always []. @throws {ReportError}
 */
export function readVitest(report) {
  if (!isObject(report)) throw new ReportError("a vitest report is an object");
  for (const field of VITEST_TOTALS) {
    if (!isCount(report[field])) throw new ReportError(`${field} is not a non-negative integer`);
  }
  const counts = {
    passed: report.numPassedTests,
    failed: report.numFailedTests,
    skipped: report.numPendingTests + report.numTodoTests,
  };
  return { counts, failures: [] };
}
```

```js
describe("readPlaywright", () => {
  // The fixtures carry a GitHub runner's rootDir; a test points it into its own temp workspace.
  const playwrightFixture = (name, rootDir) => {
    const report = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(FIXTURES, name), "utf8"));
    if (rootDir !== undefined) report.config.rootDir = rootDir;
    return report;
  };
  const FAILURE = {
    file: "web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts",
    title: "smoke.spec.ts › the landing page › the moment toggle works by mouse and keyboard",
    reason: "Error: expect(locator).toHaveAttribute(expected) failed",
  };
  const failedResults = (report) => report.suites[0].suites[0].specs[0].tests[0].results;

  test("takes the stats, and annotates neither skipped nor flaky tests", () => {
    const workspace = tempDir();
    const rootDir = path.join(workspace, "web", "e2e");
    assert.deepEqual(readPlaywright(playwrightFixture("playwright-pass.json", rootDir), { workspace }), {
      counts: { passed: 2, failed: 0, skipped: 0, flaky: 0 },
      failures: [],
    });
    assert.deepEqual(readPlaywright(playwrightFixture("playwright-skip.json", rootDir), { workspace }), {
      counts: { passed: 1, failed: 0, skipped: 1, flaky: 1 },
      failures: [],
    });
  });

  test("names each unexpected test by file, describe titles and title, relative to the workspace", () => {
    const workspace = tempDir();
    const native = path.join(workspace, "web", "e2e");
    const expected = { counts: { passed: 1, failed: 1, skipped: 0, flaky: 0 }, failures: [FAILURE] };
    assert.deepEqual(readPlaywright(playwrightFixture("playwright-fail.json", native), { workspace }), expected);
    // Playwright writes rootDir with forward slashes on every OS.
    assert.deepEqual(readPlaywright(playwrightFixture("playwright-fail.json", native.split(path.sep).join("/")), { workspace }), expected);
  });

  test("takes the reason from the last result that has an error message", () => {
    const workspace = tempDir();
    const report = playwrightFixture("playwright-fail.json", path.join(workspace, "web", "e2e"));
    const results = failedResults(report);
    results.push({ ...results[0], retry: 1, error: { message: "\n\u001b[31mTimeout 5000ms exceeded.\u001b[39m\r\nCall log:" } });
    results.push({ ...results[0], retry: 2, error: undefined });
    assert.equal(readPlaywright(report, { workspace }).failures[0].reason, "Timeout 5000ms exceeded.");
  });

  test("an unexpected test with no error message keeps an empty reason", () => {
    const workspace = tempDir();
    const report = playwrightFixture("playwright-fail.json", path.join(workspace, "web", "e2e"));
    for (const result of failedResults(report)) delete result.error;
    assert.deepEqual(readPlaywright(report, { workspace }).failures, [{ ...FAILURE, reason: "" }]);
  });

  test("throws a ReportError for anything that is not a playwright report", () => {
    const workspace = tempDir();
    const good = playwrightFixture("playwright-pass.json");
    assert.doesNotThrow(() => readPlaywright(good, { workspace }));
    const cases = [
      null,
      [],
      playwrightFixture("playwright-malformed.json"),
      { ...good, config: { ...good.config, rootDir: 7 } },
      { ...good, config: null },
      { ...good, suites: {} },
      { ...good, stats: { ...good.stats, flaky: undefined } },
      { ...good, stats: { ...good.stats, unexpected: -1 } },
    ];
    cases.forEach((report, index) => assert.throws(() => readPlaywright(report, { workspace }), ReportError, `case ${index}`));
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 19: Write `readPlaywright`**

In `.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs`, replace the end of the file:

```js
  return { counts, failures: [] };
}
```

with:

```js
  return { counts, failures: [] };
}

/** Below the shape rules: the object entries of a list, or none when it is missing or not a list. */
const objectsIn = (value) => (Array.isArray(value) ? value.filter(isObject) : []);
/** Below the shape rules: a title or file name, or "" when it is missing or not text. */
const textOf = (value) => (typeof value === "string" ? value : "");
const PLAYWRIGHT_STATS = ["expected", "unexpected", "skipped", "flaky"];

/**
 * A Playwright JSON report: one suite per spec file under `suites`, describe blocks nested in `suites`, totals in `stats`.
 * @param {unknown} report @param {{ workspace: string }} where
 * @returns {{ counts: E2eCounts, failures: Failure[] }} @throws {ReportError}
 */
export function readPlaywright(report, where) {
  if (!isObject(report)) throw new ReportError("a playwright report is an object");
  if (!isObject(report.config) || typeof report.config.rootDir !== "string") throw new ReportError("config.rootDir is not a string");
  if (!Array.isArray(report.suites)) throw new ReportError("suites is not an array");
  if (!isObject(report.stats)) throw new ReportError("stats is not an object");
  for (const field of PLAYWRIGHT_STATS) {
    if (!isCount(report.stats[field])) throw new ReportError(`stats.${field} is not a non-negative integer`);
  }
  const rootDir = report.config.rootDir;
  const failures = [];
  const visit = (suite, describeTitles) => {
    for (const spec of objectsIn(suite.specs)) {
      const file = textOf(spec.file);
      for (const test of objectsIn(spec.tests)) {
        if (test.status !== "unexpected") continue;
        const withMessage = objectsIn(test.results).filter((result) => isObject(result.error) && typeof result.error.message === "string");
        failures.push({
          file: workspacePath(where.workspace, path.join(rootDir, file)),
          title: [file, ...describeTitles, textOf(spec.title)].join(TITLE_SEPARATOR),
          reason: firstLine(withMessage.at(-1)?.error.message ?? ""),
        });
      }
    }
    for (const child of objectsIn(suite.suites)) visit(child, [...describeTitles, textOf(child.title)]);
  };
  for (const fileSuite of objectsIn(report.suites)) visit(fileSuite, []);
  const { expected, unexpected, skipped, flaky } = report.stats;
  return { counts: { passed: expected, failed: unexpected, skipped, flaky }, failures };
}
```

```js
describe("extractResult", () => {
  const fixtureText = (name) => readFileSync(path.join(FIXTURES, name), "utf8");
  const workspace = tempDir();
  const cwd = path.join(workspace, "contracts");
  const TEST_KINDS = ["forge", "vitest", "playwright"];

  test("an absent report is missing, exit 0, unless its step succeeded", () => {
    for (const kind of TEST_KINDS) {
      for (const outcome of ["failure", "cancelled", "skipped", undefined]) {
        assert.deepEqual(
          extractResult(kind, null, { GITHUB_WORKSPACE: workspace, STEP_OUTCOME: outcome }, cwd),
          { code: 0, output: { report: "missing" }, annotations: [] },
          `${kind} ${outcome}`,
        );
      }
    }
  });

  test("an absent report exits 1 when its step succeeded", () => {
    for (const kind of TEST_KINDS) {
      assert.deepEqual(extractResult(kind, null, { GITHUB_WORKSPACE: workspace, STEP_OUTCOME: "success" }, cwd), {
        code: 1,
        annotations: [],
        message: `the ${kind} report is missing, but the step that writes it succeeded`,
      });
    }
  });

  test("an empty, non-JSON or wrongly shaped report exits 1 whatever the outcome", () => {
    const cases = [
      ["forge", ""],
      ["forge", fixtureText("forge-malformed.json")],
      ["forge", "[]"],
      ["vitest", ""],
      ["vitest", fixtureText("vitest-malformed.json")],
      ["vitest", "{"],
      ["playwright", ""],
      ["playwright", fixtureText("playwright-malformed.json")],
      ["playwright", "null"],
    ];
    for (const [kind, reportText] of cases) {
      for (const outcome of ["success", "failure"]) {
        const result = extractResult(kind, reportText, { GITHUB_WORKSPACE: workspace, STEP_OUTCOME: outcome }, cwd);
        assert.equal(result.code, 1, `${kind} ${outcome} ${JSON.stringify(reportText.slice(0, 12))}`);
        assert.equal(result.output, undefined);
      }
    }
  });

  test("a report with zero tests exits 1 whatever the outcome", () => {
    for (const kind of TEST_KINDS) {
      for (const outcome of ["success", "failure"]) {
        assert.deepEqual(extractResult(kind, fixtureText(`${kind}-zero.json`), { GITHUB_WORKSPACE: workspace, STEP_OUTCOME: outcome }, cwd), {
          code: 1,
          annotations: [],
          message: `the ${kind} report holds zero tests`,
        });
      }
    }
  });

  test("a valid report gives its counts, with one annotation per forge or playwright failure", () => {
    const env = { GITHUB_WORKSPACE: workspace, STEP_OUTCOME: "failure" };
    const e2e = JSON.parse(fixtureText("playwright-fail.json"));
    e2e.config.rootDir = path.join(workspace, "web", "e2e");
    assert.deepEqual(extractResult("forge", fixtureText("forge-fail.json"), env, cwd), {
      code: 0,
      output: { passed: 1, failed: 1, skipped: 0 },
      annotations: ["::error file=contracts/test/MandateVault.t.sol::MandateVaultTest › test_refusesStaleFeed(): assertion failed: 0 != 1"],
    });
    assert.deepEqual(extractResult("vitest", fixtureText("vitest-fail.json"), env, cwd), {
      code: 0,
      output: { passed: 2, failed: 1, skipped: 0 },
      annotations: [],
    });
    assert.deepEqual(extractResult("playwright", JSON.stringify(e2e), env, cwd), {
      code: 0,
      output: { passed: 1, failed: 1, skipped: 0, flaky: 0 },
      annotations: [
        "::error file=web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts::smoke.spec.ts › the landing page › the moment toggle works by mouse and keyboard: Error: expect(locator).toHaveAttribute(expected) failed",
      ],
    });
  });

  test("the output keys keep a fixed order", () => {
    const shuffled = JSON.stringify({ numTodoTests: 1, numPendingTests: 1, numFailedTests: 2, numPassedTests: 3 });
    assert.equal(JSON.stringify(extractResult("vitest", shuffled, {}, cwd).output), '{"passed":3,"failed":2,"skipped":2}');
    const e2e = JSON.parse(fixtureText("playwright-skip.json"));
    e2e.stats = { flaky: 1, skipped: 1, unexpected: 0, expected: 1 };
    assert.equal(
      JSON.stringify(extractResult("playwright", JSON.stringify(e2e), { GITHUB_WORKSPACE: workspace }, cwd).output),
      '{"passed":1,"failed":0,"skipped":1,"flaky":1}',
    );
  });

  test("an unknown kind gives the usage", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(extractResult("jest", "{}", {}, cwd), {
      code: 1,
      annotations: [],
      message: "usage: ci-counts.mjs extract <forge|vitest|playwright|gitleaks> <report.json> <output-name> | render",
    });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 23: Write `extractResult`**

In `.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs`, replace the end of the file:

```js
  return { counts: { passed: expected, failed: unexpected, skipped, flaky }, failures };

```

with:

```js
  return { counts: { passed: expected, failed: unexpected, skipped, flaky }, failures };
}

const USAGE = "usage: ci-counts.mjs extract <forge|vitest|playwright|gitleaks> <report.json> <output-name> | render";
const READERS = {
  forge: (report, env, cwd) => readForge(report, { workspace: env.GITHUB_WORKSPACE, cwd }),
  vitest: (report) => readVitest(report),
  playwright: (report, env) => readPlaywright(report, { workspace: env.GITHUB_WORKSPACE }),
};

/**
 * The failure semantics of spec 5.1 for one report.
 * @param {"forge" | "vitest" | "playwright" | "gitleaks"} kind @param {string | null} reportText null when the file is absent
 * @param {ExtractEnv} env @param {string} cwd @returns {ExtractResult}
 */
export function extractResult(kind, reportText, env, cwd) {
  if (!Object.hasOwn(READERS, kind)) return { code: 1, annotations: [], message: USAGE };
  if (reportText === null) {
    // Unless its step succeeded, that step has already failed the job; an unset STEP_OUTCOME counts as not success.
    if (env.STEP_OUTCOME !== "success") return { code: 0, output: { report: "missing" }, annotations: [] };
    return { code: 1, annotations: [], message: `the ${kind} report is missing, but the step that writes it succeeded` };
  }
  let report;
  try {
    report = JSON.parse(reportText);
  } catch (error) {
    return { code: 1, annotations: [], message: `the ${kind} report is not JSON: ${error.message}` };
  }
  let read;
  try {
    read = READERS[kind](report, env, cwd);
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof ReportError)) throw error;
    return { code: 1, annotations: [], message: `the ${kind} report is not a ${kind} report: ${error.message}` };
  }
  const { counts, failures } = read;
  if (counts.passed + counts.failed + counts.skipped + (counts.flaky ?? 0) === 0) {
    // A test filter or a report path went wrong.
    return { code: 1, annotations: [], message: `the ${kind} report holds zero tests` };
  }
  return { code: 0, output: counts, annotations: failures.map(annotation) };
}
```

```js
describe("extract on the command line", () => {
  const USAGE = "usage: ci-counts.mjs extract <forge|vitest|playwright|gitleaks> <report.json> <output-name> | render\n";
  const TEST_KINDS = ["forge", "vitest", "playwright"];
  const fixture = (name) => path.join(FIXTURES, name);

  test("prints the usage and exits 1 for any other argument list", () => {
    const output = path.join(tempDir(), "github-output.txt");
    const argLists = [
      [],
      ["extract", "forge", "report.json"],
      ["extract", "forge", "report.json", "forge", "extra"],
      ["extract", "jest", "report.json", "unit"],
      ["count", "forge", "report.json", "forge"],
    ];
    for (const args of argLists) {
      const run = runCli(args, { env: { GITHUB_OUTPUT: output, STEP_OUTCOME: "success" } });
      assert.deepEqual([run.status, run.stdout, run.stderr], [1, "", USAGE], args.join(" "));
    }
    assert.equal(existsSync(output), false);
  });

  test("exits 1 naming GITHUB_OUTPUT when it is unset or empty, before it reads the report", () => {
    const absent = path.join(tempDir(), "absent.json");
    for (const env of [{ STEP_OUTCOME: "success" }, { GITHUB_OUTPUT: "", STEP_OUTCOME: "success" }]) {
      const run = runCli(["extract", "vitest", absent, "unit"], { env });
      assert.deepEqual([run.status, run.stdout, run.stderr], [1, "", "GITHUB_OUTPUT is not set\n"]);
    }
  });

  test("exits 1 naming GITHUB_WORKSPACE for forge and playwright when it is unset or empty", () => {
    for (const kind of ["forge", "playwright"]) {
      for (const workspaceEnv of [{}, { GITHUB_WORKSPACE: "" }]) {
        const output = path.join(tempDir(), "github-output.txt");
        const run = runCli(["extract", kind, fixture(`${kind}-pass.json`), kind], {
          env: { ...workspaceEnv, GITHUB_OUTPUT: output, STEP_OUTCOME: "success" },
        });
        assert.deepEqual([run.status, run.stdout, run.stderr], [1, "", "GITHUB_WORKSPACE is not set\n"], kind);
        assert.equal(existsSync(output), false);
      }
    }
  });

  test("an absent report whose step did not succeed, or whose STEP_OUTCOME is unset, writes missing and exits 0", () => {
    for (const kind of TEST_KINDS) {
      for (const outcome of ["failure", "cancelled", undefined]) {
        const dir = tempDir();
        const output = path.join(dir, "github-output.txt");
        const env = { GITHUB_OUTPUT: output, GITHUB_WORKSPACE: dir };
        if (outcome !== undefined) env.STEP_OUTCOME = outcome;
        const run = runCli(["extract", kind, path.join(dir, "absent.json"), "out"], { env, cwd: dir });
        assert.deepEqual([run.status, run.stdout, run.stderr], [0, "", ""], `${kind} ${outcome}`);
        assert.deepEqual(outputLines(output), ['out={"report":"missing"}']);
      }
    }
  });

  test("an absent report whose step succeeded exits 1 and writes nothing", () => {
    for (const kind of TEST_KINDS) {
      const dir = tempDir();
      const output = path.join(dir, "github-output.txt");
      const run = runCli(["extract", kind, path.join(dir, "absent.json"), "out"], {
        env: { GITHUB_OUTPUT: output, GITHUB_WORKSPACE: dir, STEP_OUTCOME: "success" },
        cwd: dir,
      });
      assert.deepEqual([run.status, run.stdout, run.stderr], [1, "", `the ${kind} report is missing, but the step that writes it succeeded\n`]);
      assert.equal(existsSync(output), false);
    }
  });

  test("a report that is unreadable, empty, not JSON or not its kind's shape exits 1 and writes nothing", () => {
    const dir = tempDir();
    const empty = path.join(dir, "empty.json");
    writeFileSync(empty, "");
    const wrongStatus = path.join(dir, "wrong-status.json");
    writeFileSync(wrongStatus, JSON.stringify({ "test/GuardLib.t.sol:GuardLibTest": { test_results: { "test_paused()": { status: "Passed" } } } }));
    const cases = [
      ["forge", dir],
      ["forge", empty],
      ["forge", fixture("forge-malformed.json")],
      ["forge", wrongStatus],
      ["vitest", fixture("vitest-malformed.json")],
      ["playwright", fixture("playwright-malformed.json")],
    ];
    for (const [kind, report] of cases) {
      const output = path.join(tempDir(), "github-output.txt");
      const run = runCli(["extract", kind, report, "out"], {
        env: { GITHUB_OUTPUT: output, GITHUB_WORKSPACE: dir, STEP_OUTCOME: "failure" },
        cwd: dir,
      });
      assert.equal(run.status, 1, `${kind} ${path.basename(report)}`);
      assert.equal(run.stdout, "");
      assert.match(run.stderr, /^(?:cannot read |the (?:forge|vitest|playwright) report is not )/);
      assert.equal(existsSync(output), false);
    }
  });

  test("a report with zero tests exits 1 and writes nothing", () => {
    for (const kind of TEST_KINDS) {
      const dir = tempDir();
      const output = path.join(dir, "github-output.txt");
      const run = runCli(["extract", kind, fixture(`${kind}-zero.json`), "out"], {
        env: { GITHUB_OUTPUT: output, GITHUB_WORKSPACE: dir, STEP_OUTCOME: "success" },
        cwd: dir,
      });
      assert.deepEqual([run.status, run.stdout, run.stderr], [1, "", `the ${kind} report holds zero tests\n`]);
      assert.equal(existsSync(output), false);
    }
  });

  test("forge, run from contracts/: appends the counts and prints one annotation per failure", () => {
    const workspace = tempDir();
    const contracts = path.join(workspace, "contracts");
    mkdirSync(contracts);
    const output = path.join(workspace, "github-output.txt");
    writeFileSync(output, "earlier=kept\n");
    const failure = "::error file=contracts/test/MandateVault.t.sol::MandateVaultTest › test_refusesStaleFeed(): assertion failed: 0 != 1\n";
    // The workspace with native separators, then with the forward slashes a Windows shell may pass.
    const runs = [
      ["forge-fail.json", workspace, "failure"],
      ["forge-fail.json", workspace.split(path.sep).join("/"), "failure"],
      ["forge-pass.json", workspace, "success"],
      ["forge-skip.json", workspace, "success"],
    ].map(([name, root, outcome]) =>
      runCli(["extract", "forge", fixture(name), "forge"], { env: { GITHUB_OUTPUT: output, GITHUB_WORKSPACE: root, STEP_OUTCOME: outcome }, cwd: contracts }),
    );
    assert.deepEqual(
      runs.map((run) => [run.status, run.stdout, run.stderr]),
      [
        [0, failure, ""],
        [0, failure, ""],
        [0, "", ""],
        [0, "", ""],
      ],
    );
    assert.deepEqual(outputLines(output), [
      "earlier=kept",
      'forge={"passed":1,"failed":1,"skipped":0}',
      'forge={"passed":1,"failed":1,"skipped":0}',
      'forge={"passed":3,"failed":0,"skipped":0}',
      'forge={"passed":1,"failed":0,"skipped":1}',
    ]);
  });

  test("vitest: appends the counts, prints nothing and needs no GITHUB_WORKSPACE", () => {
    const output = path.join(tempDir(), "github-output.txt");
    const runs = [
      ["vitest-pass.json", "success"],
      ["vitest-fail.json", "failure"],
      ["vitest-skip.json", "success"],
    ].map(([name, outcome]) => runCli(["extract", "vitest", fixture(name), "unit"], { env: { GITHUB_OUTPUT: output, STEP_OUTCOME: outcome } }));
    assert.deepEqual(
      runs.map((run) => [run.status, run.stdout, run.stderr]),
      [
        [0, "", ""],
        [0, "", ""],
        [0, "", ""],
      ],
    );
    assert.deepEqual(outputLines(output), [
      'unit={"passed":3,"failed":0,"skipped":0}',
      'unit={"passed":2,"failed":1,"skipped":0}',
      'unit={"passed":2,"failed":0,"skipped":2}',
    ]);
  });

  test("playwright: appends the counts and prints one annotation per unexpected test", () => {
    const workspace = tempDir();
    const output = path.join(workspace, "github-output.txt");
    // A temp copy of a fixture whose rootDir is the temp workspace's web/e2e.
    const copy = (name) => {
      const report = JSON.parse(readFileSync(fixture(name), "utf8"));
      report.config.rootDir = path.join(workspace, "web", "e2e");
      const file = path.join(workspace, name);
      writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify(report));
      return file;
    };
    const runs = [
      ["playwright-fail.json", "failure"],
      ["playwright-pass.json", "success"],
      ["playwright-skip.json", "success"],
    ].map(([name, outcome]) =>
      runCli(["extract", "playwright", copy(name), "e2e"], { env: { GITHUB_OUTPUT: output, GITHUB_WORKSPACE: workspace, STEP_OUTCOME: outcome } }),
    );
    assert.deepEqual(
      runs.map((run) => [run.status, run.stdout, run.stderr]),
      [
        [
          0,
          "::error file=web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts::smoke.spec.ts › the landing page › the moment toggle works by mouse and keyboard: Error: expect(locator).toHaveAttribute(expected) failed\n",
          "",
        ],
        [0, "", ""],
        [0, "", ""],
      ],
    );
    assert.deepEqual(outputLines(output), [
      'e2e={"passed":1,"failed":1,"skipped":0,"flaky":0}',
      'e2e={"passed":2,"failed":0,"skipped":0,"flaky":0}',
      'e2e={"passed":1,"failed":0,"skipped":1,"flaky":1}',
    ]);
  });

  test("reads a report the same with CRLF line endings", () => {
    const dir = tempDir();
    const report = path.join(dir, "vitest-skip-crlf.json");
    writeFileSync(report, readFileSync(fixture("vitest-skip.json"), "utf8").replace(/\r?\n/g, "\r\n"));
    const output = path.join(dir, "github-output.txt");
    const run = runCli(["extract", "vitest", report, "unit"], { env: { GITHUB_OUTPUT: output, STEP_OUTCOME: "success" } });
    assert.deepEqual([run.status, run.stdout, run.stderr], [0, "", ""]);
    assert.deepEqual(outputLines(output), ['unit={"passed":2,"failed":0,"skipped":2}']);
  });
});
```

In `.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs`, replace:

```js
import path from "node:path";
```

with:

```js
import { appendFileSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
```

In `.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs`, replace the end of the file:

```js
  return { code: 0, output: counts, annotations: failures.map(annotation) };
}
```

with:

```js
  return { code: 0, output: counts, annotations: failures.map(annotation) };
}

/** The report kinds `extract` takes. */
const KINDS = ["forge", "vitest", "playwright"];
/** The kinds whose annotation paths are relative to GITHUB_WORKSPACE. */
const NEEDS_WORKSPACE = ["forge", "playwright"];

/**
 * Parses argv, checks GITHUB_OUTPUT (and GITHUB_WORKSPACE for forge and playwright), reads the report (ENOENT gives null;
 * any other read error exits 1), calls extractResult, writes the output line with appendFileSync, writes annotations to
 * stdout and messages to stderr.
 * @param {string[]} argv @param {NodeJS.ProcessEnv} env @param {string} cwd @returns {0 | 1}
 */
export function main(argv, env, cwd) {
  const [command, kind, reportPath, outputName] = argv;
  if (argv.length !== 4 || command !== "extract" || !KINDS.includes(kind)) {
    process.stderr.write(`${USAGE}\n`);
    return 1;
  }
  if (!env.GITHUB_OUTPUT) {
    process.stderr.write("GITHUB_OUTPUT is not set\n");
    return 1;
  }
  if (NEEDS_WORKSPACE.includes(kind) && !env.GITHUB_WORKSPACE) {
    process.stderr.write("GITHUB_WORKSPACE is not set\n");
    return 1;
  }
  let reportText = null;
  try {
    reportText = readFileSync(reportPath, "utf8");
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code !== "ENOENT") {
      process.stderr.write(`cannot read ${reportPath}: ${error.message}\n`);
      return 1;
    }
  }
  const result = extractResult(kind, reportText, env, cwd);
  for (const line of result.annotations) process.stdout.write(`${line}\n`);
  if (result.message !== undefined) process.stderr.write(`${result.message}\n`);
  if (result.output !== undefined) appendFileSync(env.GITHUB_OUTPUT, `${outputName}=${JSON.stringify(result.output)}\n`);
  return result.code;
}

// Runs only when invoked directly (import.meta.main exists from Node 24.2.0; local v24.12.0, CI v24.22.3):
if (import.meta.main) process.exitCode = main(process.argv.slice(2), process.env, process.cwd());
```

```text
ci: count the forge, vitest and playwright reports

JSON report into one compact line in GITHUB_OUTPUT, and prints one
```

- A Report cell checks only the keys it prints; extra keys never make it unreadable.
- A non-string output (possible only in a hand-made `NEEDS_JSON`) is `unreadable output` in a count or scan row and `no report` in an outcome row.
- The cell text is the literal of Shared names: `1 findings` has no plural rule.
