- [ ] **Step 2: Prove the anchors, the bindings and the free names**

Write `C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/e5-plan-work/tools/ci-counts-anchors.mjs`:

```js
// Read-only check before Task 2 of the CI plan edits Task 1's files. It proves that every line
// Task 2 anchors on exists exactly once, that the import bindings Task 2's code uses exist, and
// that every name Task 2 declares is still free. It prints check names only, never file contents.
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

if (process.argv.length !== 3) {
  console.error("usage: ci-counts-anchors.mjs <repo>");
  process.exit(1);
}
const repo = process.argv[2];
const read = (relative) => readFileSync(path.join(repo, relative), "utf8").replace(/\r\n/g, "\n");
const files = {
  script: read(".github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs"),
  tests: read(".github/scripts/ci-counts.test.mjs"),
};

let passed = 0;
let failed = 0;
function check(ok, id, detail) {
  if (ok) {
    passed += 1;
    console.log(`ok ${id}`);
  } else {
    failed += 1;
    console.log(`FAIL ${id}: ${detail}`);
  }
}

const GUARD_COMMENT =
  "// Runs only when invoked directly (import.meta.main exists from Node 24.2.0; local v24.12.0, CI v24.22.3):";
const GUARD = "if (import.meta.main) process.exitCode = main(process.argv.slice(2), process.env, process.cwd());";
const ANCHORS = [
  ["script", "extractResult", "export function extractResult(kind, reportText, env, cwd) {"],
  ["script", "main", "export function main(argv, env, cwd) {"],
  ["script", "guard", `${GUARD_COMMENT}\n${GUARD}`],
  ["tests", "SCRIPT", 'const SCRIPT = fileURLToPath(new URL("./ci-counts.mjs", import.meta.url));'],
];
for (const [file, id, text] of ANCHORS) {
  const count = files[file].split(text).length - 1;
  check(count === 1, `anchor ${file} ${id}`, `found ${count} times, expected once`);
}

/** @param {string} source @returns {Map<string, string>} each local name an import binds, to its module without "node:" */
function importBindings(source) {
  const bindings = new Map();
  for (const [, clause, from] of source.matchAll(/^import\s+([^;]*?)\s+from\s+["']([^"']+)["']/gm)) {
    const module = from.replace(/^node:/, "");
    const braces = clause.match(/\{([^}]*)\}/);
    for (const specifier of braces ? braces[1].split(",") : []) {
      const local = specifier.trim().split(/\s+as\s+/).pop();
      if (local) bindings.set(local, module);
    }
    for (const part of clause.replace(/\{[^}]*\}/, "").split(",")) {
      const local = part.trim().replace(/^\*\s+as\s+/, "");
      if (local) bindings.set(local, module);
    }
  }
  return bindings;
}
const imports = { script: importBindings(files.script), tests: importBindings(files.tests) };

const BINDINGS = [
  ["script", "appendFileSync", ["fs"]],
  ["script", "readFileSync", ["fs"]],
  ["script", "path", ["path"]],
  ["tests", "test", ["test"]],
  ["tests", "assert", ["assert/strict", "assert"]],
  ["tests", "path", ["path"]],
  ["tests", "readFileSync", ["fs"]],
  ["tests", "writeFileSync", ["fs"]],
  ["tests", "fileURLToPath", ["url"]],
];
for (const [file, name, modules] of BINDINGS) {
  const module = imports[file].get(name);
  check(modules.includes(module), `binding ${file} ${name}`, module ? `bound from ${module}` : "not imported");
}

for (const name of ["childEnv", "runCli", "tempDir", "outputLines"]) {
  check(new RegExp(`^function ${name}\\(`, "m").test(files.tests), `helper tests ${name}`, "no top-level function declaration");
}
check(/^const FIXTURES = /m.test(files.tests), "helper tests FIXTURES", "no top-level const");

const FREE = {
  script: [
    "readGitleaks",
    "renderSummary",
    "gitleaksResult",
    "gitleaksCommand",
    "renderCommand",
    "summaryCell",
    "summaryJobResult",
    "summaryIsObject",
    "summaryIsCount",
    "SUMMARY_ROWS",
    "SUMMARY_FOOTER",
    "GITLEAKS_EXIT_CODES",
  ],
  tests: [
    "readGitleaks",
    "renderSummary",
    "VERIFY",
    "FOOTER_LINE",
    "TABLE_HEAD",
    "gitleaksExtract",
    "gitleaksFixture",
    "greenNeeds",
    "summaryText",
  ],
};
for (const [file, names] of Object.entries(FREE)) {
  for (const name of names) {
    const declared =
      new RegExp(`\\b(?:function|class|const|let|var)\\s+${name}\\b`).test(files[file]) || imports[file].has(name);
    check(!declared, `free ${file} ${name}`, "already declared or imported");
  }
}

console.log(`anchors: ${passed} ok, ${failed} failed`);
process.exitCode = failed === 0 ? 0 : 1;
```

```js
// extract gitleaks: one secret scan's exit code, report and commit count, as the hygiene job passes them.

/**
 * Runs `extract gitleaks` on a report written to a fresh temp directory, as the history_counts step does.
 * @param {string | null} reportText null leaves the report file absent
 * @param {Record<string, string>} scanEnv SCAN_EXIT and COMMITS, as the step's env: sets them
 * @returns {{ status: number | null, stdout: string, stderr: string, lines: string[] }}
 */
function gitleaksExtract(reportText, scanEnv) {
  const dir = tempDir();
  const report = path.join(dir, "history.json");
  if (reportText !== null) writeFileSync(report, reportText);
  const output = path.join(dir, "github-output.txt");
  writeFileSync(output, "");
  const run = runCli(["extract", "gitleaks", report, "history"], {
    env: { GITHUB_OUTPUT: output, ...scanEnv },
    cwd: dir,
  });
  return { ...run, lines: outputLines(output) };
}

/** @param {string} name @returns {string} the fixture's text */
function gitleaksFixture(name) {
  return readFileSync(path.join(FIXTURES, name), "utf8");
}

test("extract gitleaks: exit 0 with an empty report counts 0 findings over the commits", () => {
  const run = gitleaksExtract(gitleaksFixture("gitleaks-none.json"), { SCAN_EXIT: "0", COMMITS: "373" });
  assert.equal(run.status, 0, run.stderr);
  assert.deepEqual(run.lines, ['history={"findings":0,"commits":373}']);
  assert.equal(run.stdout, "");
});

test("extract gitleaks: exit 3 counts the findings, and adds commits only when COMMITS is set", () => {

    [{ SCAN_EXIT: "3", COMMITS: "373" }, 'history={"findings":2,"commits":373}'],
  ]) {
    const run = gitleaksExtract(found, scanEnv);
    assert.equal(run.status, 0, run.stderr);
    assert.deepEqual(run.lines, [line], JSON.stringify(scanEnv));
  }
});

test("extract gitleaks: an exit other than 0 or 3 is a scan error, never a count", () => {

});

});

});

test("extract gitleaks: a report path that cannot be read fails and writes nothing", () => {

  const output = path.join(dir, "github-output.txt");

});
```


with

```js
/** gitleaks exit codes that mean the scan ran: 0 with no findings, 3 (`--exit-code 3`) with findings. */
const GITLEAKS_EXIT_CODES = new Set(["0", "3"]);

/**
 * Counts one gitleaks scan from its exit code and JSON report. An unset or empty SCAN_EXIT means the
 * scan step never ran; any exit other than 0 or 3 means gitleaks itself failed, so a broken scan never
 * shows as "0 findings".
 * @param {string | null} reportText null when the file is absent
 * @param {{ scanExit?: string, commits?: string }} scan
 * @returns {ScanCounts | MissingReport | ScanError}
 * @throws {ReportError} when a scan that ran left no usable report, or COMMITS is not a whole number
 */
export function readGitleaks(reportText, scan) {
  const scanExit = scan.scanExit ?? "";
  if (scanExit === "") return { report: "missing" };
  if (!GITLEAKS_EXIT_CODES.has(scanExit)) return { error: "scan" };
  const commits = scan.commits ?? "";
  if (commits !== "" && !/^\d+$/.test(commits)) throw new ReportError("COMMITS is not a whole number");
  if (reportText === null) throw new ReportError(`gitleaks exited ${scanExit} but wrote no report`);
  if (reportText.trim() === "") throw new ReportError(`gitleaks exited ${scanExit} but its report is empty`);
  let findings;
  try {
    findings = JSON.parse(reportText);
  } catch {
    throw new ReportError(`gitleaks exited ${scanExit} but its report is not JSON`);
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(findings)) {
    throw new ReportError(`gitleaks exited ${scanExit} but its report is not a JSON array`);
  }
  return commits === "" ? { findings: findings.length } : { findings: findings.length, commits: Number(commits) };
}

/** The gitleaks branch of extractResult: no annotations, and exit 1 only for a ReportError. */
function gitleaksResult(reportText, env) {
  try {
    const output = readGitleaks(reportText, { scanExit: env.SCAN_EXIT, commits: env.COMMITS });
    return { code: 0, output, annotations: [] };
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ReportError) return { code: 1, annotations: [], message: error.message };
    throw error;
  }
}

/** `extract gitleaks <report.json> <output-name>`: checks GITHUB_OUTPUT before it reads anything. */
function gitleaksCommand(reportPath, outputName, env, cwd) {
  if (!env.GITHUB_OUTPUT) {
    process.stderr.write("GITHUB_OUTPUT is not set\n");
    return 1;
  }
  let reportText = null;
  try {
    reportText = readFileSync(path.resolve(cwd, reportPath), "utf8");
  } catch (error) {
    if (error?.code !== "ENOENT") {
      process.stderr.write(`cannot read ${reportPath}: ${error.message}\n`);
      return 1;
    }
  }
  const result = extractResult("gitleaks", reportText, env, cwd);
  if (result.message) process.stderr.write(`${result.message}\n`);
  if (result.code !== 0) return 1;
  appendFileSync(env.GITHUB_OUTPUT, `${outputName}=${JSON.stringify(result.output)}\n`);
  return 0;
}

// Runs only when invoked directly (import.meta.main exists from Node 24.2.0; local v24.12.0, CI v24.22.3):
if (import.meta.main) process.exitCode = main(process.argv.slice(2), process.env, process.cwd());
```

```js
// render: the summary job's table from toJSON(needs), and the drift guard on the web job's steps.

const FOOTER_LINE =
  "Offline suites only. The contracts fork suite, the agent fork integration suite and Lighthouse need an archive RPC or a browser audit and run by hand; see README.md, Development.";

const TABLE_HEAD = ["| Check | Job result | Report |", "| --- | --- | --- |"];

const VERIFY = "npm run typecheck && npm test && npm run build && npm run check:out";

/** `toJSON(needs)` of a run in which every job passed. The counts are test inputs, not measurements. */
function greenNeeds() {
  return {
    contracts: { result: "success", outputs: { forge: '{"passed":3,"failed":0,"skipped":0}' } },
    agent: { result: "success", outputs: { unit: '{"passed":4,"failed":0,"skipped":0}' } },
    web: {
      result: "success",
      outputs: { unit: '{"passed":5,"failed":0,"skipped":0}', e2e: '{"passed":6,"failed":0,"skipped":0,"flaky":0}' },
    },
    hygiene: {
      result: "success",
      outputs: {
        history: '{"findings":0,"commits":373}',
        messages: '{"findings":0}',
        canary: "success",
        actionlint: "success",
      },
    },
  };
}

/** @param {string[]} rows the eight table rows @returns {string} the whole text render must write */
function summaryText(rows) {
  return [...TABLE_HEAD, ...rows, "", FOOTER_LINE, ""].join("\n");
}

test("render: every job green gives one row per suite and scan, then the footer", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(renderSummary(JSON.stringify(greenNeeds())), {
    code: 0,
    markdown: summaryText([
      "| Contracts, unit | success | 3 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped |",
      "| Agent, unit | success | 4 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped |",
      "| Web, unit | success | 5 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped |",
      "| Web, end to end | success | 6 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped, 0 flaky |",
      "| Secret scan, history | success | 0 findings over 373 commits |",
      "| Secret scan, commit messages | success | 0 findings |",
      "| Secret scan, canary | success | success |",
      "| Workflow lint | success | success |",
    ]),
  });
});

test("render: failed jobs show their result and the failed counts", () => {
  const needs = greenNeeds();
  needs.contracts = { result: "failure", outputs: { forge: '{"passed":2,"failed":1,"skipped":0}' } };
  needs.web = {
    result: "failure",
    outputs: { unit: '{"passed":4,"failed":1,"skipped":0}', e2e: '{"passed":5,"failed":1,"skipped":0,"flaky":0}' },
  };
  needs.hygiene = {
    result: "failure",
    outputs: {
      history: '{"findings":2,"commits":373}',
      messages: '{"findings":1}',
      canary: "failure",
      actionlint: "success",
    },
  };
  assert.equal(
    renderSummary(JSON.stringify(needs)).markdown,
    summaryText([
      "| Contracts, unit | failure | 2 passed, 1 failed, 0 skipped |",
      "| Agent, unit | success | 4 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped |",
      "| Web, unit | failure | 4 passed, 1 failed, 0 skipped |",
      "| Web, end to end | failure | 5 passed, 1 failed, 0 skipped, 0 flaky |",
      "| Secret scan, history | failure | 2 findings over 373 commits |",
      "| Secret scan, commit messages | failure | 1 findings |",
      "| Secret scan, canary | failure | failure |",
      "| Workflow lint | failure | success |",
    ]),
  );
});

});

test("render: a missing report, a job with no outputs and an absent job show no report", () => {

      "| Secret scan, canary | failure | success |",

test("render: an output that does not parse or has the wrong shape shows as unreadable output", () => {
  const needs = greenNeeds();
  needs.contracts.outputs.forge = "not json";
  needs.agent.outputs.unit = "[]";
  needs.web.outputs = { unit: '{"passed":-1,"failed":0,"skipped":0}', e2e: '{"passed":6,"failed":0,"skipped":0}' };
  needs.hygiene.outputs.history = '{"findings":0,"commits":"373"}';
  needs.hygiene.outputs.messages = '{"findings":1.5}';
  assert.equal(
    renderSummary(JSON.stringify(needs)).markdown,
    summaryText([
      "| Contracts, unit | success | unreadable output |",
      "| Agent, unit | success | unreadable output |",
      "| Web, unit | success | unreadable output |",
      "| Web, end to end | success | unreadable output |",
      "| Secret scan, history | success | unreadable output |",
      "| Secret scan, commit messages | success | unreadable output |",
      "| Secret scan, canary | success | success |",
      "| Workflow lint | success | success |",
    ]),
  );
});

test("renderSummary: a NEEDS_JSON that is unset, does not parse or is not an object fails", () => {
  for (const needsJson of [undefined, "", "not json", "[]", "null", '"needs"', "42"]) {
    assert.deepEqual(renderSummary(needsJson), { code: 1, message: "NEEDS_JSON does not parse" }, String(needsJson));
  }
});

test("render: appends the table to GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY and exits 0", () => {
  const dir = tempDir();
  const summary = path.join(dir, "step-summary.md");
  writeFileSync(summary, "earlier step\n");
  const needsJson = JSON.stringify(greenNeeds());
  const run = runCli(["render"], { env: { GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY: summary, NEEDS_JSON: needsJson }, cwd: dir });
  assert.equal(run.status, 0, run.stderr);
  assert.equal(run.stdout, "");
  assert.equal(readFileSync(summary, "utf8"), `earlier step\n${renderSummary(needsJson).markdown}`);
});

test("render: with no GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY the table goes to stdout and the exit stays 0", () => {
  const needsJson = JSON.stringify(greenNeeds());
  const run = runCli(["render"], { env: { NEEDS_JSON: needsJson }, cwd: tempDir() });
  assert.equal(run.status, 0, run.stderr);
  assert.equal(run.stdout, renderSummary(needsJson).markdown);
});

test("render: a NEEDS_JSON that is unset or does not parse exits 1 and writes nothing", () => {
  const dir = tempDir();
  const summary = path.join(dir, "step-summary.md");
  writeFileSync(summary, "");
  for (const env of [{ GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY: summary, NEEDS_JSON: "{" }, { GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY: summary }]) {
    const run = runCli(["render"], { env, cwd: dir });
    assert.equal(run.status, 1, JSON.stringify(env));
    assert.match(run.stderr, /NEEDS_JSON does not parse/);
    assert.equal(run.stdout, "");
  }
  assert.equal(readFileSync(summary, "utf8"), "");
});

test("render and extract gitleaks with the wrong argument count take the usage path", () => {
  const dir = tempDir();
  const output = path.join(dir, "github-output.txt");
  writeFileSync(output, "");
  for (const args of [["render", "now"], ["extract", "gitleaks", "history.json"]]) {
    const run = runCli(args, {
      env: { GITHUB_OUTPUT: output, SCAN_EXIT: "0", NEEDS_JSON: JSON.stringify(greenNeeds()) },
      cwd: dir,
    });
    assert.equal(run.status, 1, args.join(" "));
    assert.ok(
      run.stderr.startsWith(
        "usage: ci-counts.mjs extract <forge|vitest|playwright|gitleaks> <report.json> <output-name> | render",
      ),
      run.stderr,
    );
    assert.equal(run.stdout, "");
  }
  assert.deepEqual(outputLines(output), []);
});
```

Edit 2. Replace

with

```js
/** The table's footer. It carries no hand-measured number, so it cannot go stale. */
const SUMMARY_FOOTER =
  "Offline suites only. The contracts fork suite, the agent fork integration suite and Lighthouse need an archive RPC or a browser audit and run by hand; see README.md, Development.";

/** One row per suite and scan: the check's label, the needed job, its output, and how the cell reads it. */
const SUMMARY_ROWS = [
  ["Contracts, unit", "contracts", "forge", "counts"],
  ["Agent, unit", "agent", "unit", "counts"],
  ["Web, unit", "web", "unit", "counts"],
  ["Web, end to end", "web", "e2e", "e2e"],
  ["Secret scan, history", "hygiene", "history", "history"],
  ["Secret scan, commit messages", "hygiene", "messages", "scan"],
  ["Secret scan, canary", "hygiene", "canary", "outcome"],
  ["Workflow lint", "hygiene", "actionlint", "outcome"],
];

function summaryIsObject(value) {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function summaryIsCount(value) {
  return Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value >= 0;
}

/** `needs.<job>.result` when it is a non-empty string, else "unknown". */
function summaryJobResult(job) {
  return summaryIsObject(job) && typeof job.result === "string" && job.result !== "" ? job.result : "unknown";
}

/**
 * One Report cell. An absent, null or empty output shows as "no report" (its step never ran, for
 * example after a timeout), never as malformed.
 * @param {unknown} output the job output as GitHub passed it
 * @param {"counts" | "e2e" | "history" | "scan" | "outcome"} reads
 * @returns {string}
 */
function summaryCell(output, reads) {
  if (reads === "outcome") return typeof output === "string" && output !== "" ? output : "no report";
  if (output === undefined || output === null || output === "") return "no report";
  if (typeof output !== "string") return "unreadable output";
  let value;
  try {
    value = JSON.parse(output);
  } catch {
    return "unreadable output";
  }
  if (!summaryIsObject(value)) return "unreadable output";
  if (value.report === "missing") return "no report";
  if (value.error === "scan") return "scan failed";
  if (reads === "counts" || reads === "e2e") {
    if (![value.passed, value.failed, value.skipped].every(summaryIsCount)) return "unreadable output";
    const counts = `${value.passed} passed, ${value.failed} failed, ${value.skipped} skipped`;
    if (reads === "counts") return counts;
    return summaryIsCount(value.flaky) ? `${counts}, ${value.flaky} flaky` : "unreadable output";
  }
  if (!summaryIsCount(value.findings)) return "unreadable output";
  if (reads === "history" && value.commits !== undefined) {
    return summaryIsCount(value.commits) ? `${value.findings} findings over ${value.commits} commits` : "unreadable output";
  }
  return `${value.findings} findings`;
}

/**
 * Renders the job-summary table from `toJSON(needs)`. It fails only when NEEDS_JSON is unset, does
 * not parse or is not an object; gating belongs to the workflow's shell step.
 * @param {string | undefined} needsJson
 * @returns {{ code: 0 | 1, markdown?: string, message?: string }}
 */
export function renderSummary(needsJson) {
  let needs;
  try {
    needs = JSON.parse(needsJson ?? "");
  } catch {
    needs = undefined;
  }
  if (!summaryIsObject(needs)) return { code: 1, message: "NEEDS_JSON does not parse" };
  const lines = ["| Check | Job result | Report |", "| --- | --- | --- |"];
  for (const [label, jobName, outputName, reads] of SUMMARY_ROWS) {
    const job = Object.hasOwn(needs, jobName) ? needs[jobName] : undefined;
    const outputs = summaryIsObject(job) && summaryIsObject(job.outputs) ? job.outputs : {};
    const output = Object.hasOwn(outputs, outputName) ? outputs[outputName] : undefined;
    lines.push(`| ${label} | ${summaryJobResult(job)} | ${summaryCell(output, reads)} |`);
  }
  lines.push("", SUMMARY_FOOTER);
  return { code: 0, markdown: `${lines.join("\n")}\n` };
}

/** `render`: appends the table to GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY, or writes it to stdout when that is unset or empty. */
function renderCommand(env) {
  const result = renderSummary(env.NEEDS_JSON);
  if (result.code !== 0) {
    process.stderr.write(`${result.message}\n`);
    return 1;
  }
  if (env.GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY) appendFileSync(env.GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY, result.markdown);
  else process.stdout.write(result.markdown);
  return 0;
}

// Runs only when invoked directly (import.meta.main exists from Node 24.2.0; local v24.12.0, CI v24.22.3):
if (import.meta.main) process.exitCode = main(process.argv.slice(2), process.env, process.cwd());
```

### Task 3: The gitleaks config

**Spec refs:** 6.1 (the exact `.gitleaks.toml`), 6.2, 6.3, 3 (the image digest note).

**Branch and worktree:** `chore/ci` in `E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci`, on top of Task 2's commit (`ci: count the secret scans and render the job summary`). The main checkout `E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon` is only read through git's shared object store; nothing is written, built or tested there. Nothing is pushed in this task (Task 7 pushes, with the owner's approval).

**Files:**
- Create: `.gitleaks.toml` (spec 6.1 verbatim, 44 lines, the `stopwords` array holding only its comment line until the tool fills it; 51 lines after the tool writes the seven values)
- Create (scratchpad, never committed): `C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/e5-plan-work/tools/gl-stopwords.mjs`, `C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/e5-plan-work/tools/gl-history-check.mjs`
- Create (scratchpad, never committed): `C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/e5-plan-work/run/e5-commit-task-3.txt` (the commit message)
- Modify: none
- Test: `node C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/e5-plan-work/tools/gl-history-check.mjs E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci` (expects `findings=0`) and the same with `--no-allowlist` (expects `findings=7`, all `generic-api-key`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `scratchpad/gl-emulate.mjs` (the matching to reproduce) and `scratchpad/gl-default-v8.30.1.toml`; the local history including `feat/llm-providers` and the commits 45c22322, 50948801, b5de0e7a, efdc272d; the allowlist path regex `^agent/test/(?:llm/)?[^/]+\.test\.ts$` and `condition = "AND"`.
- Produces: `.gitleaks.toml` with seven lowercase stopwords (values never printed or written elsewhere); the tool command lines and outputs, and the `gl-stopwords.mjs` exports `DEFAULT_TOML`, `loadDefaultRules`, `entropy`, `genericFindings`, `readStopwords`, exactly as in Shared names.
- Commit: `chore: add the gitleaks config` (only `.gitleaks.toml`).

**What the tools reproduce.** The matching of `gl-emulate.mjs`, line by line on every added line of the walk: the keyword prefilter on the lowercased line, the `generic-api-key` regex with group 1 as the secret, the rule's `^[a-zA-Z_.-]+$` secret allowlist, its `regexTarget = "match"` allowlist on the whole match, a secret with entropy below 3.5 dropped, and a default stopword inside the lowercased secret dropped. Both tools read these from `gl-default-v8.30.1.toml` (a leading `(?i)` becomes the `i` flag, `\z` becomes `$`) and skip every path that the default config's global allowlist names, among them `gitleaks\.toml`, so `.gitleaks.toml` itself is never scanned (spec 6.1). The walk is the CI scan's (spec 4.4 step 2, 6.2): every ref, `--full-history`, and each merge's diff against its first parent.

**Smallest choices (the spec is silent):**
- Both tools add only output options to the walk of Shared names (`-c core.quotePath=false`, `--no-color`, `--no-textconv`, `--no-ext-diff`), so the patch text parses the same in Git Bash on Windows as git prints it inside the container.
- `gl-stopwords.mjs` writes the values in sorted order, so the same history always gives the same file.
- `gl-history-check.mjs` also runs the default `github-pat` rule and the three `maat-*` rules that `.gitleaks.toml` defines, through the same `genericFindings` walk (`gl-emulate.mjs` checks `github-pat` and the private-key rule too), so its later runs in Tasks 5, 6 and 9 also catch a token or private-key literal in a committed file.
- Commit messages are not part of this walk; the CI `messages` step scans them (spec 4.4 step 3, 6.2), and this task's message holds no key-shaped text.

**The image digest note (spec 3).** Both tools read `gl-default-v8.30.1.toml`, the default config of v8.30.1, the version that the gitleaks digest of spec 3 pins; Step 1 checks it has the 222 rules that research 03 gives for v8.30.1. Dependabot does not track the image, so a later digest bump is a manual commit that re-reads the digest and re-runs both tools against that version's default config before it touches the stopwords or the allowlist; the near misses of spec 6.3 (stopwords `ant-` and `admin`) are what may start to fire.

**Never print a value.** The tools print counts, commits, paths, line numbers, rule ids and lengths only. After Step 8, `.gitleaks.toml` holds the seven values: never `cat` it, never run `git diff`, `git show` without `--stat`/`--numstat`, or `git log -p` on it in the terminal, and never copy a line of it into another file, a message or a report.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the stopwords tool (it is also the emulation the check imports)**

Write `C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/e5-plan-work/tools/gl-stopwords.mjs` with the Write tool:

```js
// Scratch tool for the Maat CI plan, Task 3. Never committed.
// Emulates the default gitleaks v8.30.1 generic-api-key rule the way gl-emulate.mjs does, over the patch stream of
// the CI history scan, keeps the findings in agent unit-test files, and writes their seven distinct values, in
// lowercase, into the stopwords array of <repo>/.gitleaks.toml. It prints counts, lengths and paths, never a value.
import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

/** Absolute path of the default v8.30.1 config. */
export const DEFAULT_TOML =
  "C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/gl-default-v8.30.1.toml";

// The CI scan's log options; --no-color, --no-textconv and --no-ext-diff only keep the patch text the same as git
// prints it inside the gitleaks container, and core.quotePath=false keeps non-ASCII paths unquoted.
const GIT_ARGS = ["-c", "core.quotePath=false", "log", "-p", "-U0", "--all", "--full-history",
  "--diff-merges=first-parent", "--diff-filter=tuxdb", "--no-color", "--no-textconv", "--no-ext-diff"];
const FIXTURE_COMMITS = ["45c22322", "50948801", "b5de0e7a", "efdc272d"];
const UNIT_TEST_PATH = /^agent\/test\/(?:llm\/)?[^/]+\.test\.ts$/;
const COMMENT_LINE = "# The seven values, in lowercase, read from git at 45c22322, 50948801, b5de0e7a and efdc272d.";

/** Compiles a Go RE2 pattern from the config: a leading (?i) becomes the i flag and \z becomes $. */
function goRegex(source, flags) {
  const insensitive = source.startsWith("(?i)");
  const body = (insensitive ? source.slice(4) : source).replaceAll("\\z", "$");
  return new RegExp(body, insensitive ? `${flags}i` : flags);
}

/** The items of the multi-line TOML array that opens on the first line at or after `from` equal to `header`. */
function arrayItems(lines, from, header) {
  const start = lines.findIndex((line, i) => i >= from && line.trim() === header);
  const end = lines.findIndex((line, i) => i > start && line.trim() === "]");
  if (start === -1 || end === -1) throw new Error(`${header} not found in the default config`);
  return lines
    .slice(start + 1, end)
    .map((line) => line.trim().replace(/,$/, ""))
    .filter((item) => item !== "" && !item.startsWith("#"))
    .map((item) => (item.startsWith("'''") ? item.slice(3, -3) : JSON.parse(item)));
}

/** @returns {{ regex: RegExp, keywords: string[], entropy: number, stopwords: string[], secretAllow: RegExp[], matchAllow: RegExp, globalPaths: RegExp[] }} the default generic-api-key rule and the global path allowlist, matched as gl-emulate.mjs does */
export function loadDefaultRules() {
  const lines = readFileSync(DEFAULT_TOML, "utf8").split(/\r?\n/);
  const start = lines.findIndex((line) => line.trim() === 'id = "generic-api-key"');
  const end = lines.findIndex((line, i) => i > start && line.trim() === "[[rules]]");
  if (start === -1 || end === -1) throw new Error("generic-api-key not found in the default config");
  const rule = lines.slice(start, end);
  const allowlists = rule.flatMap((line, i) => (line.trim() === "[[rules.allowlists]]" ? [i] : []));
  if (allowlists.length < 2 || !rule.slice(allowlists[1], allowlists[2]).some((line) => line.trim() === 'regexTarget = "match"')) {
    throw new Error("the generic-api-key allowlists are not in the expected order");
  }
  const regexLine = rule.find((line) => line.startsWith("regex = '''"));
  const entropyLine = rule.find((line) => line.startsWith("entropy = "));
  return {
    regex: goRegex(regexLine.slice("regex = '''".length, -3), "g"),
    keywords: arrayItems(rule, 0, "keywords = ["),
    entropy: Number(entropyLine.slice("entropy = ".length)),
    stopwords: arrayItems(rule, allowlists[1], "stopwords = ["),
    secretAllow: arrayItems(rule, allowlists[0], "regexes = [").map((source) => goRegex(source, "")),
    matchAllow: goRegex(arrayItems(rule, allowlists[1], "regexes = [")[0], ""),
    globalPaths: arrayItems(lines, lines.indexOf("[allowlist]"), "paths = [").map((source) => goRegex(source, "")),
  };
}

/** @param {string} secret @returns {number} Shannon entropy in bits */
export function entropy(secret) {
  const counts = new Map();
  for (const char of secret) counts.set(char, (counts.get(char) ?? 0) + 1);
  let bits = 0;
  for (const n of counts.values()) bits -= (n / secret.length) * Math.log2(n / secret.length);
  return bits;
}

/** @param {string} patch `git log -p -U0` text @param {ReturnType<typeof loadDefaultRules>} rules @returns {{ commit: string, path: string, line: number, rule: "generic-api-key", secret: string }[]} */
export function genericFindings(patch, rules) {
  const findings = [];
  let commit = "";
  let file = null;
  let oldLeft = 0;
  let newLeft = 0;
  let lineNo = 0;
  const scan = (content) => {
    const lower = content.toLowerCase();
    if (!rules.keywords.some((keyword) => lower.includes(keyword))) return;
    for (const match of content.matchAll(rules.regex)) {
      const secret = match[1];
      if (secret === undefined) continue;
      if (rules.secretAllow.some((allow) => allow.test(secret))) continue;
      if (rules.matchAllow.test(match[0])) continue;
      if (entropy(secret) < rules.entropy) continue;
      const lowered = secret.toLowerCase();
      if (rules.stopwords.some((word) => lowered.includes(word))) continue;
      findings.push({ commit, path: file, line: lineNo, rule: "generic-api-key", secret });
    }
  };
  for (const raw of patch.split("\n")) {
    const line = raw.endsWith("\r") ? raw.slice(0, -1) : raw;
    if (oldLeft > 0 || newLeft > 0) {
      if (line.startsWith("+")) {
        newLeft -= 1;
        if (file !== null) scan(line.slice(1));
        lineNo += 1;
      } else if (line.startsWith("-")) {
        oldLeft -= 1;
      } else if (!line.startsWith("\\")) {
        throw new Error(`unexpected line inside a hunk of ${commit.slice(0, 8)}`);
      }
      continue;
    }
    const header = /^commit ([0-9a-f]{40})/.exec(line);
    if (header) {
      commit = header[1];
      file = null;
    } else if (line.startsWith("diff --git ")) {
      file = null;
    } else if (line.startsWith("+++ ")) {
      const target = line.slice(4);
      const name = target === "/dev/null" ? null : (target.startsWith('"') ? JSON.parse(target) : target).replace(/^b\//, "");
      file = name !== null && !rules.globalPaths.some((allow) => allow.test(name)) ? name : null;
    } else {
      const hunk = /^@@ -\d+(?:,(\d+))? \+(\d+)(?:,(\d+))? @@/.exec(line);
      if (hunk) {
        oldLeft = hunk[1] === undefined ? 1 : Number(hunk[1]);
        lineNo = Number(hunk[2]);
        newLeft = hunk[3] === undefined ? 1 : Number(hunk[3]);
      }
    }
  }
  return findings;
}

/** @param {string} tomlText @returns {string[]} the strings inside the generic-api-key allowlist's stopwords array of .gitleaks.toml */
export function readStopwords(tomlText) {
  const lines = tomlText.split(/\r?\n/);
  const start = lines.findIndex((line) => line.trim() === "stopwords = [");
  const end = lines.findIndex((line, i) => i > start && line.trim() === "]");
  if (start === -1 || end === -1) throw new Error("no stopwords array in .gitleaks.toml");
  return lines
    .slice(start + 1, end)
    .map((line) => line.trim())
    .filter((line) => line.startsWith('"'))
    .map((line) => JSON.parse(line.replace(/,$/, "")));
}

function main(argv) {
  if (argv.length !== 1) {
    console.error("usage: gl-stopwords.mjs <repo>");
    return 1;
  }
  const repo = argv[0];
  const patch = execFileSync("git", ["-C", repo, ...GIT_ARGS], { encoding: "utf8", maxBuffer: 1024 * 1024 * 1024 });
  const findings = genericFindings(patch, loadDefaultRules()).filter((finding) => UNIT_TEST_PATH.test(finding.path));
  const values = [...new Set(findings.map((finding) => finding.secret))].sort();
  let ok = values.length === 7;
  console.log(`count=${values.length}`);
  for (const value of values) {
    const home = findings.find((f) => f.secret === value && FIXTURE_COMMITS.some((c) => f.commit.startsWith(c)));
    console.log(`len=${value.length} path=${(home ?? findings.find((f) => f.secret === value)).path}`);
    if (home === undefined) {
      console.error(`FAIL a value of length ${value.length} is not added in ${FIXTURE_COMMITS.join(", ")}`);
      ok = false;
    }
  }
  if (values.length !== 7) console.error(`FAIL expected 7 values, found ${values.length}`);
  if (!ok) return 1;
  const tomlPath = path.join(repo, ".gitleaks.toml");
  const text = readFileSync(tomlPath, "utf8");
  if (readStopwords(text).length > 0) {
    console.error("FAIL the stopwords array of .gitleaks.toml already holds a value");
    return 1;
  }
  const eol = text.includes("\r\n") ? "\r\n" : "\n";
  const lines = text.split(eol);
  const at = lines.findIndex((line) => line.trim() === COMMENT_LINE);
  if (at === -1) {
    console.error("FAIL the stopwords comment line is not in .gitleaks.toml");
    return 1;
  }
  lines.splice(at + 1, 0, ...values.map((value) => `    ${JSON.stringify(value.toLowerCase())},`));
  writeFileSync(tomlPath, lines.join(eol));
  return 0;
}

if (import.meta.main) process.exitCode = main(process.argv.slice(2));
```

`UNIT_TEST_PATH` is the path regex of spec 6.1 written out; `gl-history-check.mjs` reads the same regex from `.gitleaks.toml`, so a typo in either one shows up as a finding in Step 10. The tool writes nothing unless the count is 7 and every value is added in one of the four commits, and it refuses to write a second time.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the history check (the test for this task)**

Write `C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/e5-plan-work/tools/gl-history-check.mjs` with the Write tool:

```js
// Scratch tool for the Maat CI plan, Task 3 (re-run in Tasks 5, 6 and 9). Never committed.
// Emulates the CI history scan over the local history: the default generic-api-key and github-pat rules and the
// three maat-* rules of <repo>/.gitleaks.toml, with the .gitleaks.toml generic-api-key allowlist applied with
// condition AND (path regex and stopword), or not applied with --no-allowlist. It prints commits, paths, lines,
// rule ids and lengths, never a value.
import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { DEFAULT_TOML, genericFindings, loadDefaultRules, readStopwords } from "./gl-stopwords.mjs";

// The same walk as gl-stopwords.mjs: the CI scan's log options plus output-only options.
const GIT_ARGS = ["-c", "core.quotePath=false", "log", "-p", "-U0", "--all", "--full-history",
  "--diff-merges=first-parent", "--diff-filter=tuxdb", "--no-color", "--no-textconv", "--no-ext-diff"];
const NEVER = /(?!)/;

/** Compiles a Go RE2 pattern from a config: a leading (?i) becomes the i flag and \z becomes $. */
function goRegex(source, flags) {
  const insensitive = source.startsWith("(?i)");
  const body = (insensitive ? source.slice(4) : source).replaceAll("\\z", "$");
  return new RegExp(body, insensitive ? `${flags}i` : flags);
}

/** One rule shaped like loadDefaultRules() so that genericFindings can run it; entropy 0 means no threshold. */
function shaped(id, regex, keywords, entropy, secretAllow, globalPaths) {
  return { id, regex, keywords, entropy, stopwords: [], secretAllow, matchAllow: NEVER, globalPaths };
}

/** The default github-pat rule; it has no capture group, so the whole match is wrapped in one, as gitleaks takes it. */
function githubPat(globalPaths) {
  const lines = readFileSync(DEFAULT_TOML, "utf8").split(/\r?\n/);
  const start = lines.findIndex((line) => line.trim() === 'id = "github-pat"');
  const end = lines.findIndex((line, i) => i > start && line.trim() === "[[rules]]");
  const value = (key) => lines.slice(start, end).find((line) => line.startsWith(`${key} = `)).slice(key.length + 3);
  const regex = goRegex(value("regex").slice(3, -3), "g");
  return shaped("github-pat", new RegExp(`(${regex.source})`, regex.flags), JSON.parse(value("keywords")), Number(value("entropy")), [], globalPaths);
}

/** The generic-api-key allowlist of .gitleaks.toml and its rules that carry a regex of their own. */
function readConfig(tomlText, globalPaths) {
  const blocks = [];
  for (const line of tomlText.split(/\r?\n/)) {
    if (line.trim() === "[[rules]]") blocks.push([]);
    else if (blocks.length > 0) blocks.at(-1).push(line.trim());
  }
  const value = (block, key) => block.find((line) => line.startsWith(`${key} = `))?.slice(key.length + 3);
  const patterns = (text) => [...(text ?? "").matchAll(/'''(.*?)'''/g)].map((match) => goRegex(match[1], ""));
  const generic = blocks.find((block) => value(block, "id") === '"generic-api-key"');
  if (generic === undefined) throw new Error("no generic-api-key rule in .gitleaks.toml");
  return {
    condition: JSON.parse(value(generic, "condition") ?? '""'),
    paths: patterns(value(generic, "paths")),
    stopwords: readStopwords(tomlText).map((word) => word.toLowerCase()),
    own: blocks
      .filter((block) => value(block, "regex") !== undefined)
      .map((block) => shaped(JSON.parse(value(block, "id")), goRegex(value(block, "regex").slice(3, -3), "g"),
        JSON.parse(value(block, "keywords")), 0, patterns(value(block, "regexes")), globalPaths)),
  };
}

function main(argv) {
  const [repo, flag, ...rest] = argv;
  if (repo === undefined || rest.length > 0 || (flag !== undefined && flag !== "--no-allowlist")) {
    console.error("usage: gl-history-check.mjs <repo> [--no-allowlist]");
    return 1;
  }
  const withAllowlist = flag === undefined;
  const rules = loadDefaultRules();
  const config = readConfig(readFileSync(path.join(repo, ".gitleaks.toml"), "utf8"), rules.globalPaths);
  if (config.condition !== "AND" || config.paths.length !== 1) {
    console.error('FAIL the generic-api-key allowlist of .gitleaks.toml needs condition = "AND" and one path');
    return 1;
  }
  const patch = execFileSync("git", ["-C", repo, ...GIT_ARGS], { encoding: "utf8", maxBuffer: 1024 * 1024 * 1024 });
  const allowed = (finding) =>
    config.paths.some((allow) => allow.test(finding.path)) &&
    config.stopwords.some((word) => finding.secret.toLowerCase().includes(word));
  const findings = [
    ...genericFindings(patch, rules).filter((finding) => !withAllowlist || !allowed(finding)),
    ...[githubPat(rules.globalPaths), ...config.own].flatMap((rule) =>
      genericFindings(patch, rule).map((finding) => ({ ...finding, rule: rule.id }))),
  ];
  console.log(`findings=${findings.length}`);
  for (const f of findings) console.log(`${f.commit.slice(0, 8)} ${f.path}:${f.line} ${f.rule} len=${f.secret.length}`);
  if (withAllowlist) return findings.length === 0 ? 0 : 1;
  return findings.length === 7 && findings.every((finding) => finding.rule === "generic-api-key") ? 0 : 1;
}

if (import.meta.main) process.exitCode = main(process.argv.slice(2));
```

The allowlist is applied as `condition = "AND"` reads: a `generic-api-key` finding is dropped only when its path matches the allowlist's path regex and its lowercased secret contains one of the stopwords.

Write `E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/.gitleaks.toml` with the Write tool, exactly this (spec 6.1 verbatim; the `stopwords` array holds only its comment line):

```toml
# Read by gitleaks from the repository root.
title = "Maat gitleaks config"

[extend]
useDefault = true

# Seven made-up provider keys that agent unit tests declared before this file existed. gitleaks
# lowercases both the secret and each stopword before it compares them, and a stopword suppresses
# any secret that contains it; the values are written in lowercase by convention. A finding is
# ignored only when it holds one of these values AND it sits in an agent unit-test file. A new
# made-up key in a test carries the word "fake" in its value instead, which the default stopwords
# already ignore.
[[rules]]
id = "generic-api-key"

  [[rules.allowlists]]
  description = "Made-up provider keys in agent unit tests, committed before this config"
  condition = "AND"
  paths = ['''^agent/test/(?:llm/)?[^/]+\.test\.ts$''']
  stopwords = [
    # The seven values, in lowercase, read from git at 45c22322, 50948801, b5de0e7a and efdc272d.
  ]

[[rules]]
id = "maat-evm-private-key"
description = "A 32-byte hex private key next to a private-key name or the --private-key flag"
regex = '''(?i)(?:private[_-]?key|priv[_-]?key|signer[_-]?key|deployer[_-]?key)["'`]?\s*(?:[:=]|=>|\s)\s*["'`]?(?:0x)?([0-9a-f]{64})\b'''
keywords = ["private", "priv", "signer", "deployer"]

[[rules]]
id = "maat-rpc-url-with-key"
description = "An RPC URL that carries a provider API key in its path"
regex = '''(?i)https://[a-z0-9.-]*(?:alchemy\.com/v2|infura\.io/v3|quiknode\.pro|api\.developer\.coinbase\.com/rpc/v1/[a-z-]+)/([A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,})'''
keywords = ["alchemy.com", "infura.io", "quiknode.pro", "api.developer.coinbase.com"]

[[rules]]
id = "maat-mnemonic"
description = "A 12 to 24 word seed phrase assigned to a mnemonic-like name"
regex = '''(?i)(?:mnemonic|seed[_ -]?phrase|recovery[_ -]?phrase)["'`]?\s*[:=]\s*["'`]?((?:[a-z]{3,8}[ \t]+){11,23}[a-z]{3,8})\b'''
keywords = ["mnemonic", "seed", "recovery"]

  [[rules.allowlists]]
  description = "The public Anvil and Hardhat default mnemonic"
  regexes = ['''^(?:test\s+){11}junk$''']
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the policy tool (the test for the workflow file)**

Write `C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/e5-plan-work/tools/workflow-policy.mjs` with exactly this content (the `PINS` map is the pins table of spec section 3, verbatim):

```js
// workflow-policy.mjs: checks the hardening rules of the Maat CI workflow (plan Task 4; Task 5 extends it).
// Usage: node workflow-policy.mjs <path to .github/workflows/ci.yml>
// Prints "ok <check>" or "FAIL <check>: <detail>" for each check, then "policy: <a> ok, <b> failed".
// Exits 0 when no check failed, 1 otherwise. It only reads the file. Scratchpad tool, never committed.
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

// yaml 2.9.1 from the main checkout's web/node_modules, imported by absolute path; never added as a dependency.
const YAML_ENTRY = "E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/web/node_modules/yaml/dist/index.js";
const { isScalar, parseDocument, visit } = await import(pathToFileURL(YAML_ENTRY).href);

// Spec section 3, "Pins (read 2026-11-18)": action -> [commit, version comment].
const PINS = new Map([
  ["actions/checkout", ["3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1", "v7.0.1"]],
  ["actions/setup-node", ["949feb2413d6458794dcd2491c4babbbce0c15c1", "v7.1.0"]],
  ["foundry-rs/foundry-toolchain", ["dba66c27052e63476d886f22216e119ba7e615bf", "v1.9.2"]],
  ["actions/upload-artifact", ["cf430e030ddbb5b0abf93d22962f4752f3646cd9", "v7.0.2"]],
]);

// Spec section 3, "Runners" and "Timeouts (minutes)": job -> [runs-on, timeout-minutes].
// Task 5 adds hygiene, summary and preflip.
const JOBS = new Map([
  ["contracts", ["ubuntu-24.04", 15]],
  ["agent", ["ubuntu-24.04", 10]],
  ["web", ["ubuntu-24.04", 30]],
]);

// Spec section 3, "Token and defaults": the top level grants contents: read, and a job may set
// permissions only when it is listed here, with exactly these values. Task 5 adds preflip.
const TOP_PERMISSIONS = { contents: "read" };
const JOB_PERMISSIONS = new Map();

function isObject(value) {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function show(value) {
  return value === undefined ? "missing" : JSON.stringify(value);
}

function sameFlat(actual, expected) {
  if (expected === undefined) return actual === undefined;
  if (!isObject(actual)) return false;
  const keys = Object.keys(expected);
  return Object.keys(actual).length === keys.length && keys.every((key) => actual[key] === expected[key]);
}

if (process.argv.length !== 3) {
  console.error("usage: node workflow-policy.mjs <ci.yml>");
  process.exit(1);
}
const file = process.argv[2];
let text;
try {
  text = readFileSync(file, "utf8");
} catch (error) {
  console.error(`cannot read ${file}: ${error.code ?? error.message}`);
  process.exit(1);
}
const doc = parseDocument(text);
const workflow = doc.errors.length === 0 ? doc.toJS() : undefined;
if (!isObject(workflow)) {
  console.error(`cannot parse ${file}: ${doc.errors[0]?.message ?? "the top level is not a mapping"}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

function linesMatching(pattern) {
  return text.split(/\r?\n/).flatMap((line, index) => (pattern.test(line) ? [index + 1] : []));
}

const jobs = isObject(workflow.jobs) ? workflow.jobs : {};
const steps = Object.entries(jobs).flatMap(([jobId, job]) =>
  (isObject(job) && Array.isArray(job.steps) ? job.steps : []).map((step, index) => ({
    where: `${jobId}.${isObject(step) && typeof step.id === "string" ? step.id : `step ${index + 1}`}`,
    step: isObject(step) ? step : {},
  })),
);
const runs = steps.filter(({ step }) => typeof step.run === "string");

const checks = [
  [
    "pins",
    () => {
      const nodes = [];
      visit(doc, {
        Pair(_, pair) {
          if (isScalar(pair.key) && pair.key.value === "uses") nodes.push(pair.value);
        },
      });
      const problems = nodes.length === 0 ? ["no uses: line"] : [];
      for (const node of nodes) {
        const value = isScalar(node) ? String(node.value) : show(node?.toJSON());
        const comment = isScalar(node) ? (node.comment ?? "").trim() : "";
        const [, action, commit] = /^([^@\s]+)@([0-9a-f]{40})$/.exec(value) ?? [];
        const pin = PINS.get(action);
        if (action === undefined) problems.push(`${value} is not <action>@<40-hex SHA>`);
        else if (pin === undefined) problems.push(`${action} is not in the pins table`);
        else if (commit !== pin[0]) problems.push(`${action} is pinned to ${commit}, not ${pin[0]}`);
        else if (comment !== pin[1]) problems.push(`${action} has the comment ${show(comment)}, not ${show(pin[1])}`);
      }
      return problems;
    },
  ],
  [
    "no-secrets",
    () => {
      const lines = linesMatching(/\bsecrets\s*[.[]/);
      return lines.length === 0 ? [] : [`secrets. on line ${lines.join(", ")}`];
    },
  ],
  ["no-expr-in-run", () => runs.filter(({ step }) => step.run.includes("${{")).map(({ where }) => where)],
  [
    "checkout-credentials",
    () =>
      steps
        .filter(({ step }) => typeof step.uses === "string" && step.uses.startsWith("actions/checkout@"))
        .filter(({ step }) => !isObject(step.with) || step.with["persist-credentials"] !== false)
        .map(({ where }) => where),
  ],
  [
    "permissions",
    () => {
      const problems = [];
      if (!sameFlat(workflow.permissions, TOP_PERMISSIONS)) problems.push(`top level is ${show(workflow.permissions)}`);
      for (const [jobId, job] of Object.entries(jobs)) {
        const actual = isObject(job) ? job.permissions : undefined;
        if (!sameFlat(actual, JOB_PERMISSIONS.get(jobId))) problems.push(`${jobId} is ${show(actual)}`);
      }
      return problems;
    },
  ],
  [
    "jobs",
    () => {
      const problems = [];
      const actual = Object.keys(jobs).sort().join(",");
      const expected = [...JOBS.keys()].sort().join(",");
      if (actual !== expected) problems.push(`jobs are [${actual}], not [${expected}]`);
      for (const [jobId, [runsOn, timeout]] of JOBS) {
        const job = jobs[jobId];
        if (!isObject(job)) continue;
        if (job["runs-on"] !== runsOn) problems.push(`${jobId} runs-on is ${show(job["runs-on"])}, not ${show(runsOn)}`);
        if (job["timeout-minutes"] !== timeout) {
          problems.push(`${jobId} timeout-minutes is ${show(job["timeout-minutes"])}, not ${timeout}`);
        }
      }
      return problems;
    },
  ],
  ["npx-no-install", () => runs.filter(({ step }) => /\bnpx\b(?!\s+--no-install\b)/.test(step.run)).map(({ where }) => where)],
  [
    "no-pull-request-target",
    () => {
      const lines = linesMatching(/pull_request_target/);
      return lines.length === 0 ? [] : [`pull_request_target on line ${lines.join(", ")}`];
    },
  ],
];

let ok = 0;
let failed = 0;
for (const [id, check] of checks) {
  const problems = check();
  if (problems.length === 0) {
    ok += 1;
    console.log(`ok ${id}`);
  } else {
    failed += 1;
    console.log(`FAIL ${id}: ${problems.join("; ")}`);
  }
}
console.log(`policy: ${ok} ok, ${failed} failed`);
process.exitCode = failed === 0 ? 0 : 1;
```

What each check enforces (spec section 3): `pins`, every `uses:` value is `<action>@<40-hex SHA>`, the action and commit are in the pins table, and the same-line comment is the table's version; `no-secrets`, no `secrets.` or `secrets[` on any line; `no-expr-in-run`, no `${{` in any `run:` text; `checkout-credentials`, every `actions/checkout` step has `persist-credentials: false`; `permissions`, the top level is exactly `contents: read` and no job sets its own; `jobs`, the job set is exactly `agent`, `contracts`, `web`, each with its `runs-on` and `timeout-minutes`; `npx-no-install`, every `npx` in a `run:` is followed by `--no-install`; `no-pull-request-target`, the text never names that trigger.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the workflow: top matter and the contracts, agent and web jobs**

Write `E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/.github/workflows/ci.yml` with exactly this content (172 lines, LF):

```yaml
name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      pre_flip:
        description: Also run the pre-flip scan of every pull-request ref and every pull-request and issue text
        type: boolean
        default: false

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: ci-${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' && format('pr-{0}', github.event.pull_request.number) || format('run-{0}', github.run_id) }}
  cancel-in-progress: ${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' }}

defaults:
  run:
    shell: bash

jobs:
  contracts:
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 15
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: contracts
    outputs:
      forge: ${{ steps.counts.outputs.forge }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          submodules: true
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: foundry-rs/foundry-toolchain@dba66c27052e63476d886f22216e119ba7e615bf # v1.9.2
        with:
          version: v1.9.0
          cache: false
      - uses: actions/setup-node@949feb2413d6458794dcd2491c4babbbce0c15c1 # v7.1.0
        with:
          node-version: 24
      - id: version
        run: |
          version="$(forge --version)"
          printf '%s\n' "$version"
          if ! grep -q 6056c58 <<< "$version"; then
            echo "::error::forge is not v1.9.0 (commit 6056c58)"
            exit 1
          fi
      - id: build
        run: forge build --sizes
      - id: test
        run: forge test --no-match-path "test/fork/*" --json-file "$RUNNER_TEMP/forge.json"
      - id: counts
        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
        env:
          STEP_OUTCOME: ${{ steps.test.outcome }}
        run: node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs" extract forge "$RUNNER_TEMP/forge.json" forge
      - id: clean
        run: |
          changes="$(git status --porcelain)"
          if [ -n "$changes" ]; then
            printf '%s\n' "$changes"
            echo "::error::the job wrote a tracked file or an untracked file that .gitignore does not cover"
            exit 1
          fi

  agent:
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 10
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: agent
    outputs:
      unit: ${{ steps.counts.outputs.unit }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: actions/setup-node@949feb2413d6458794dcd2491c4babbbce0c15c1 # v7.1.0
        with:
          node-version: 24
          cache: npm
          cache-dependency-path: agent/package-lock.json
      - id: install
        run: npm ci
      - id: typecheck
        run: npm run typecheck
      - id: test
        run: npm test -- --reporter=default --reporter=github-actions --reporter=json --outputFile.json="$RUNNER_TEMP/agent-unit.json"
      - id: counts
        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
        env:
          STEP_OUTCOME: ${{ steps.test.outcome }}
        run: node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs" extract vitest "$RUNNER_TEMP/agent-unit.json" unit
      - id: clean
        run: |
          changes="$(git status --porcelain)"
          if [ -n "$changes" ]; then
            printf '%s\n' "$changes"
            echo "::error::the job wrote a tracked file or an untracked file that .gitignore does not cover"
            exit 1
          fi

  web:
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 30
    env:
      NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL: https://maatonbase.xyz
      NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED: "1"
      NO_UPDATE_CHECK: "1"
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: web
    outputs:
      unit: ${{ steps.unit_counts.outputs.unit }}
      e2e: ${{ steps.e2e_counts.outputs.e2e }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          fetch-depth: 1
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: actions/setup-node@949feb2413d6458794dcd2491c4babbbce0c15c1 # v7.1.0
        with:
          node-version: 24
          cache: npm
          cache-dependency-path: web/package-lock.json
      - id: install
        run: npm ci
      - id: typecheck
        run: npm run typecheck
      - id: unit
        run: npm test -- --reporter=default --reporter=github-actions --reporter=json --outputFile.json="$RUNNER_TEMP/web-unit.json"
      - id: build
        run: npm run build
      - id: check_out
        run: npm run check:out
      - id: browsers
        run: npx --no-install playwright install --with-deps --only-shell chromium
      - id: e2e
        env:
          PLAYWRIGHT_JSON_OUTPUT_FILE: ${{ runner.temp }}/e2e.json
        run: npx --no-install playwright test --workers=1 --forbid-only --reporter=list,json
      - id: unit_counts
        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
        env:
          STEP_OUTCOME: ${{ steps.unit.outcome }}
        run: node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs" extract vitest "$RUNNER_TEMP/web-unit.json" unit
      - id: e2e_counts
        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
        env:
          STEP_OUTCOME: ${{ steps.e2e.outcome }}
        run: node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs" extract playwright "$RUNNER_TEMP/e2e.json" e2e
      - id: traces
        if: ${{ failure() }}
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@cf430e030ddbb5b0abf93d22962f4752f3646cd9 # v7.0.2
        with:
          name: e2e-traces
          path: web/test-results/
          retention-days: 7
      - id: clean
        run: |
          changes="$(git status --porcelain)"
          if [ -n "$changes" ]; then
            printf '%s\n' "$changes"
            echo "::error::the job wrote a tracked file or an untracked file that .gitignore does not cover"
            exit 1
          fi
```

Where each part comes from:
- Lines 3-12: spec 3, Triggers (`push` to `main`, every `pull_request`, `workflow_dispatch` with the boolean `pre_flip`, default false; no `pull_request_target`, no `schedule`). Lines 14-15 and 21-23: spec 3, Token and defaults. Lines 17-19: spec 3, Concurrency, verbatim, with no `queue:` key.
- Every expression, exactly as written above: `${{ steps.counts.outputs.forge }}`, `${{ steps.counts.outputs.unit }}`, `${{ steps.unit_counts.outputs.unit }}`, `${{ steps.e2e_counts.outputs.e2e }}`, `${{ !cancelled() }}`, `${{ steps.test.outcome }}`, `${{ steps.unit.outcome }}`, `${{ steps.e2e.outcome }}`, `${{ runner.temp }}/e2e.json` and `${{ failure() }}`. Each sits in `outputs:`, `if:` or `env:`, never in `run:`.
- `contracts` (spec 4.1): checkout with `submodules: true` and the default depth; foundry-toolchain `version: v1.9.0`, `cache: false`; setup-node 24 with no cache; step ids `version`, `build`, `test`, `counts`, `clean`; no `env:` at any level, so `BASE_RPC_URL` stays unset. Never `forge build --locked`, `forge fmt --check` or the fork suite.
- `agent` (spec 4.2): full checkout (default depth, no submodules); setup-node 24 with `cache: npm` on `agent/package-lock.json`; step ids `install`, `typecheck`, `test`, `counts`, `clean`. Naming the reporters turns off Vitest's automatic GitHub Actions reporter, so `github-actions` is listed (research note 02, section 6).
- `web` (spec 4.3): checkout `fetch-depth: 1`; setup-node 24 with `cache: npm` on `web/package-lock.json`; the job `env:` of spec 4.3; step ids `install`, `typecheck`, `unit`, `build`, `check_out`, `browsers`, `e2e`, `unit_counts`, `e2e_counts`, `traces`, `clean`, which are the four parts of `npm run verify` one by one, then the browser, the e2e run, the two extracts, the trace upload on failure (artifact `e2e-traces`, `web/test-results/`, 7 days; Playwright's `outputDir` is `test-results/e2e`) and the clean-tree check. Never `npm run docs:gen`.
- `upload-artifact`'s `path` is relative to the workspace, not to `defaults.run.working-directory`, which applies only to `run:` steps; hence `web/test-results/`.

**Smallest choices where the spec is silent (one line each):**
- Both image references are written out by digest in every `docker run`, not through a variable, so `images` and a reviewer read the pin where it is used.
- actionlint gets no file argument and runs with `/repo` as its working directory, which lints every file in `.github/workflows/`.
- The actionlint `docker run` mounts only the read-only workspace: spec 4.4 states its two mounts in the paragraph on the gitleaks image, whose runs need `/out` for their reports, and actionlint writes no report, so it gets no write mount. `docker-mounts` checks exactly that, and its comment says so.
- The canary repository commits with `-c user.name=canary -c user.email=canary@invalid`, so it does not depend on a git identity on the runner.
- The canary's containment check is a `node -e` script (the job has setup-node 24); it prints rule names and counts only.
- Several writes to one file are grouped into one redirect (`{ …; } >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"`), the form shellcheck, which the actionlint image bundles, suggests for that case.
- `preflip`'s `scan`, `messages`, `text` and `text_scan` run on `!cancelled()` like `hygiene`'s scans, so a finding in one does not hide the others; its scans write no step outputs, because nothing reads them.
- `preflip`'s `text` step writes one file per kind with `gh api --paginate … --jq`, keeping only the URL, title and body of each item, so diff hunks and other metadata are never scanned: `issues?state=all` (it lists pull requests too), `issues/comments`, `pulls/comments` (review comments), `comments` (commit comments), and `pulls/{n}/reviews` for every number from `pulls?state=all`.
- The gate prints `<job>=<result>` for each of the four jobs and exits 1 unless every result is `success`.
- Locally, `NEEDS_JSON` takes the four job outputs from Task 4's `github-output.txt` by position (contracts, agent, web unit, web end to end, as Task 4's Step 14 fixes them), and the hygiene outputs from `extract gitleaks` on the Task 2 fixture `gitleaks-none.json` with `SCAN_EXIT=0`, written to a separate `out-hygiene.txt`, because gitleaks cannot run here.

**No owner gate in this task.** Nothing is pushed, nothing is downloaded and no paid API is called. Docker is not installed locally, so the canary, the three scans and actionlint first run in CI (Task 7, spec 8 step 2). The four canary values were checked while this plan was written against `gl-default-v8.30.1.toml`: none holds a default stopword, the fixture's entropy is above 3.5 and the `ghp_` values' above 3; Step 3's tool re-checks this on the executor's machine.

```js
// Checks .github/workflows/ci.yml against the hardening rules of the Maat CI plan.
// Usage: node workflow-policy.mjs <path to ci.yml>
// Prints "ok <check>" or "FAIL <check>: <detail>" per check, then "policy: <a> ok, <b> failed".
// Exit 0 when no check failed. It reads only the workflow file and prints no secret.
// A file it cannot read or parse gives one "cannot read" or "cannot parse" line on stderr and exit 1.
// Task 4 wrote the first eight checks; Task 5 writes this whole file again with all fourteen.

import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const ACTIONLINT_IMAGE = "rhysd/actionlint@sha256:b1934ee5f1c509618f2508e6eb47ee0d3520686341fec936f3b79331f9315667";

  agent: ["ubuntu-24.04", 10],

  preflip: ["ubuntu-24.04", 15],
};
const WORKSPACE_MOUNT = "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE:/repo:ro";

  { job: "preflip", id: "messages", command: "dir", report: "/out/messages.json", target: "/out/text" },

];
```
