```js
const NOT_CANCELLED = "${{ !cancelled() }}";
const ALWAYS = "${{ always() }}";
const OUTDIR = 'mkdir -p "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks"';
const MESSAGES_LOG = "git log --all --format='%H%n%B' > \"$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/text/commit-messages.txt\"";
const PULLS_FETCH =
  "GIT_CONFIG_COUNT=1 GIT_CONFIG_KEY_0=http.https://github.com/.extraheader GIT_CONFIG_VALUE_0=\"AUTHORIZATION: basic $(printf 'x-access-token:%s' \"$TOKEN\" | base64 -w0)\" git fetch --no-tags origin '+refs/pull/*:refs/remotes/pull/*'";
const TEXT_ENDPOINTS = [
  "issues?state=all&per_page=100",
  "issues/comments?per_page=100",
  "pulls/comments?per_page=100",
  "comments?per_page=100",
  "pulls?state=all&per_page=100",
  "pulls/$number/reviews?per_page=100",
];

const file = process.argv[2];
if (!file) {
  console.error("usage: node workflow-policy.mjs <path to ci.yml>");
  process.exit(1);
}
let text;
try {
  text = readFileSync(file, "utf8");
} catch (error) {
  console.error(`cannot read ${file}: ${error.code ?? error.message}`);
  process.exit(1);
}
const parsed = YAML.parseDocument(text);
const doc = parsed.errors.length === 0 ? parsed.toJS() : undefined;
if (doc === null || typeof doc !== "object" || Array.isArray(doc)) {
  console.error(`cannot parse ${file}: ${parsed.errors[0]?.message ?? "the top level is not a mapping"}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

const jobSteps = (name) => (Array.isArray(doc.jobs?.[name]?.steps) ? doc.jobs[name].steps : []);
const allSteps = () =>
  Object.keys(doc.jobs ?? {}).flatMap((job) => jobSteps(job).map((step, index) => ({ job, step, index })));
const where = ({ job, step, index }) => (step.id ? `${job}.${step.id}` : `${job} step ${index + 1}`);
const stepById = (job, id) => jobSteps(job).find((step) => step.id === id);
const runOf = (step) => (typeof step?.run === "string" ? step.run : "");
const usesOf = (step) => (typeof step?.uses === "string" ? step.uses : "");
const sameMap = (actual, expected) =>
  actual !== null &&
  typeof actual === "object" &&
  !Array.isArray(actual) &&
  Object.keys(actual).length === Object.keys(expected).length &&
  Object.entries(expected).every(([key, value]) => actual[key] === value);

/** Splits one shell line into words, honouring single and double quotes (no escapes are needed in ci.yml). */
function shellWords(line) {
  const words = [];
  let word = null;
  let quote = null;
  for (const ch of line) {
    if (quote) {
      if (ch === quote) quote = null;
      else word += ch;
    } else if (ch === '"' || ch === "'") {
      quote = ch;
      word ??= "";
    } else if (/\s/.test(ch)) {
      if (word !== null) words.push(word);
      word = null;
    } else {
      word = (word ?? "") + ch;
    }
  }
  if (word !== null) words.push(word);
  return words;
}

/** Every `docker run` command of a run script, with its options, mounts, image and container arguments. */
function dockerRuns(script) {
  return script
    .replace(/\\\r?\n[ \t]*/g, " ")
    .split(/\r?\n/)
    .filter((line) => /\bdocker\s+run\b/.test(line))
    .map((line) => {
      const words = shellWords(line);
      let i = words.indexOf("run", words.indexOf("docker")) + 1;
      const mounts = [];
      const options = [];
      let workdir = null;
      while (i < words.length && words[i].startsWith("-")) {
        if (words[i] === "-v") {
          mounts.push(words[i + 1]);
          i += 2;
        } else if (words[i] === "-w") {
          workdir = words[i + 1];
          i += 2;
        } else {
          options.push(words[i]);
          i += 1;
        }
      }
      const end = words.indexOf("||", i);
      return { image: words[i] ?? "", mounts, workdir, options, args: words.slice(i + 1, end === -1 ? undefined : end) };
    });
}

function gitleaksArgs(args) {
  const [command, ...rest] = args;
  const values = {};
  const flags = [];
  const positionals = [];
  const unknown = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < rest.length; i += 1) {
    const word = rest[i];
    if (word in GITLEAKS_VALUES || word === "--report-path") {
      values[word] = rest[i + 1];
      i += 1;
    } else if (word.startsWith("--log-opts=")) values["--log-opts"] = word.slice("--log-opts=".length);
    else if (GITLEAKS_BOOLEANS.includes(word)) flags.push(word);
    else if (word.startsWith("-")) unknown.push(word);
    else positionals.push(word);
  }
  return { command, values, flags, positionals, unknown };
}

const results = [];
function check(id, run) {
  const problems = run();
  results.push(problems.length === 0);
  console.log(problems.length === 0 ? `ok ${id}` : `FAIL ${id}: ${problems.join("; ")}`);
}

check("pins", () => {
  const problems = [];
  const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/).filter((line) => /^\s*(?:-\s+)?uses:/.test(line));
  for (const line of lines) {
    const match = /^\s*(?:-\s+)?uses:\s*([^@\s]+)@(\S+)\s*(.*)$/.exec(line);
    const pin = match && PINS[match[1]];
    if (!pin) problems.push(`${line.trim()} is not one of the pinned actions`);
    else if (match[2] !== pin[0] || match[3].trim() !== `# ${pin[1]}`) problems.push(`${match[1]} must be @${pin[0]} # ${pin[1]}`);
  }
  const parsed = allSteps().filter(({ step }) => step.uses !== undefined).length;
  if (parsed !== lines.length) problems.push(`${parsed} steps use an action but ${lines.length} uses lines were read`);
  return problems;
});

check("no-secrets", () => {
  const expressions = [...text.matchAll(/\$\{\{([\s\S]*?)\}\}/g)].map((match) => match[1]);
  return expressions.some((expression) => /\bsecrets\b/.test(expression)) || /\bsecrets\s*[.[]/.test(text)
    ? ["the workflow reads a secrets value"]
    : [];
});

check("no-expr-in-run", () =>
  allSteps()
    .filter(({ step }) => runOf(step).includes("${{"))
    .map((entry) => `${where(entry)} has an expression inside run`),
);

check("checkout-credentials", () =>
  allSteps()
    .filter(({ step }) => usesOf(step).startsWith("actions/checkout@") && step.with?.["persist-credentials"] !== false)
    .map((entry) => `${where(entry)} does not set persist-credentials: false`),
);

check("permissions", () => {
  const problems = [];
  if (!sameMap(doc.permissions, { contents: "read" })) problems.push("top-level permissions must be exactly contents: read");
  for (const [name, job] of Object.entries(doc.jobs ?? {})) {
    if (name !== "preflip" && job?.permissions !== undefined) problems.push(`${name} sets permissions`);
  }
  if (!doc.jobs?.preflip) problems.push("no preflip job");
  else if (!sameMap(doc.jobs.preflip.permissions, { contents: "read", "pull-requests": "read", issues: "read" })) {
    problems.push("preflip permissions must be exactly contents, pull-requests and issues: read");
  }
  return problems;
});

check("jobs", () => {
  const problems = [];
  const names = Object.keys(doc.jobs ?? {});
  const expected = Object.keys(JOBS);
  if ([...names].sort().join(",") !== [...expected].sort().join(",")) {
    problems.push(`jobs are ${names.join(", ")}; expected ${expected.join(", ")}`);
  }
  for (const [name, [runsOn, timeout]] of Object.entries(JOBS)) {
    const job = doc.jobs?.[name];
    if (!job) continue;
    if (job["runs-on"] !== runsOn) problems.push(`${name} runs-on must be ${runsOn}`);
    if (job["timeout-minutes"] !== timeout) problems.push(`${name} timeout-minutes must be ${timeout}`);
    if (job.name !== undefined) problems.push(`${name} sets name`);
  }
  return problems;
});

check("npx-no-install", () =>
  allSteps()
    .filter(({ step }) => /\bnpx\b(?!\s+--no-install\b)/.test(runOf(step)))
    .map((entry) => `${where(entry)} calls npx without --no-install`),
);

check("no-pull-request-target", () => {
  const on = doc.on;
  const triggers = typeof on === "string" ? [on] : Array.isArray(on) ? on : Object.keys(on ?? {});
  return triggers.includes("pull_request_target") ? ["the workflow triggers on pull_request_target"] : [];
});

check("images", () => {
  const problems = [];
  const gitleaks = [];
  const actionlint = [];
  for (const entry of allSteps()) {
    for (const run of dockerRuns(runOf(entry.step))) {
      if (run.image === GITLEAKS_IMAGE) gitleaks.push(where(entry));
      else if (run.image === ACTIONLINT_IMAGE) actionlint.push(where(entry));
      else problems.push(`${where(entry)} runs ${run.image || "no image"}, not a pinned digest`);
    }
    if (usesOf(entry.step).startsWith("docker://")) problems.push(`${where(entry)} uses a docker:// action`);
  }
  const expected = GITLEAKS_CALLS.map((call) => `${call.job}.${call.id}`);
  if (gitleaks.join(",") !== expected.join(",")) {
    problems.push(`gitleaks runs in ${gitleaks.join(", ") || "no step"}; expected ${expected.join(", ")}`);
  }
  if (actionlint.join(",") !== "hygiene.actionlint") {
    problems.push(`actionlint runs in ${actionlint.join(", ") || "no step"}; expected hygiene.actionlint`);
  }
  if (/\bdocker\s+(?:pull|build|create|exec)\b/.test(text)) problems.push("the workflow pulls, builds or execs another image");
  return problems;
});

check("gitleaks-flags", () => {
  const problems = [];
  for (const call of GITLEAKS_CALLS) {
    const name = `${call.job}.${call.id}`;
    const step = stepById(call.job, call.id);
    if (!step) {
      problems.push(`${name} is missing`);
      continue;
    }
    const runs = dockerRuns(runOf(step)).filter((run) => run.image === GITLEAKS_IMAGE);
    if (runs.length !== 1) {
      problems.push(`${name} must run gitleaks exactly once`);
      continue;
    }
    const args = gitleaksArgs(runs[0].args);
    if (args.command !== call.command) problems.push(`${name} must run gitleaks ${call.command}`);
    for (const flag of GITLEAKS_BOOLEANS) if (!args.flags.includes(flag)) problems.push(`${name} lacks ${flag}`);
    for (const [flag, value] of Object.entries(GITLEAKS_VALUES)) {
      if (args.values[flag] !== value) problems.push(`${name} must pass ${flag} ${value}`);
    }
    if (args.values["--report-path"] !== call.report) problems.push(`${name} must pass --report-path ${call.report}`);
    if (args.values["--log-opts"] !== call.logOpts) {
      problems.push(call.logOpts ? `${name} must pass --log-opts="${call.logOpts}"` : `${name} must not pass --log-opts`);
    }
    if (args.positionals.join(" ") !== call.target) problems.push(`${name} must scan ${call.target}`);
    if (args.unknown.length > 0) problems.push(`${name} passes ${args.unknown.join(" ")}`);
  }
  return problems;
});

check("docker-mounts", () => {
  // Spec 4.4: every gitleaks run mounts exactly the read-only workspace and $RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks as /out, where its
  // report goes. The actionlint run writes no report, so it mounts the read-only workspace alone (plan, Task 5).
  const problems = [];
  let count = 0;
  for (const entry of allSteps()) {
    for (const run of dockerRuns(runOf(entry.step))) {
      count += 1;
      const lint = run.image === ACTIONLINT_IMAGE;
      const expected = lint ? [WORKSPACE_MOUNT] : [WORKSPACE_MOUNT, OUT_MOUNT];
      if ([...run.mounts].sort().join(" ") !== [...expected].sort().join(" ")) {
        problems.push(`${where(entry)} mounts ${run.mounts.join(" ") || "nothing"}`);
      }
      if (run.options.join(" ") !== "--rm") problems.push(`${where(entry)} passes ${run.options.join(" ") || "no --rm"} to docker run`);
      if (run.workdir !== (lint ? "/repo" : null)) problems.push(`${where(entry)} sets the working directory ${run.workdir ?? "none"}`);
    }
  }
  if (count === 0) problems.push("no docker run");
  return problems;
});

check("hygiene", () => {
  const job = doc.jobs?.hygiene;
  if (!job) return ["no hygiene job"];
  const problems = [];
  const steps = jobSteps("hygiene");
  if (!usesOf(steps[0]).startsWith("actions/checkout@") || steps[0].with?.["fetch-depth"] !== 0 || steps[0].with?.submodules !== undefined) {
    problems.push("step 1 must be a checkout with fetch-depth: 0 and no submodules");
  }
  if (!usesOf(steps[1]).startsWith("actions/setup-node@") || String(steps[1].with?.["node-version"]) !== "24" || steps[1].with?.cache !== undefined) {
    problems.push("step 2 must be setup-node with node-version: 24 and no cache");
  }
  const ids = steps.slice(2).map((step) => step.id ?? "no id");
  const expectedIds = ["outdir", "ignorefile", "canary", "scan", "messages", "history_counts", "messages_counts", "actionlint"];
  if (ids.join(",") !== expectedIds.join(",")) problems.push(`step ids are ${ids.join(", ")}; expected ${expectedIds.join(", ")}`);
  const conditions = { outdir: undefined, ignorefile: undefined, canary: undefined, scan: NOT_CANCELLED, messages: NOT_CANCELLED, history_counts: NOT_CANCELLED, messages_counts: NOT_CANCELLED, actionlint: NOT_CANCELLED };
  for (const [id, condition] of Object.entries(conditions)) {
    const step = stepById("hygiene", id);
    if (step && step.if !== condition) problems.push(condition ? `${id} must have if: ${condition}` : `${id} must have no if`);
  }
  if (runOf(stepById("hygiene", "outdir")).trim() !== OUTDIR) problems.push(`outdir must run ${OUTDIR}`);
  if (!runOf(stepById("hygiene", "ignorefile")).includes("if [ -e .gitleaksignore ]; then")) problems.push("ignorefile must fail when .gitleaksignore exists");
  const canary = runOf(stepById("hygiene", "canary"));
  for (const part of ['canary="$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/canary"', '[ "$code" -ne 3 ]', "evm < 1 || pat !== 2 || fixture < 1", 'count("generic-api-key", "agent/test/x.test.ts")', '"$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/canary.json"']) {
    if (!canary.includes(part)) problems.push(`canary lacks ${part}`);
  }
  const scan = runOf(stepById("hygiene", "scan"));
  for (const part of ['commits="$(git rev-list --all --count)"', 'echo "exit=$code"', 'echo "commits=$commits"', '} >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"', 'exit "$code"']) {
    if (!scan.includes(part)) problems.push(`scan lacks ${part}`);
  }
  const messages = runOf(stepById("hygiene", "messages"));
  for (const part of [MESSAGES_LOG, 'echo "exit=$code" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"', 'exit "$code"']) {
    if (!messages.includes(part)) problems.push(`messages lacks ${part}`);
  }
  const counts = {
    history_counts: [{ SCAN_EXIT: "${{ steps.scan.outputs.exit }}", COMMITS: "${{ steps.scan.outputs.commits }}" }, 'node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs" extract gitleaks "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/history.json" history'],
    messages_counts: [{ SCAN_EXIT: "${{ steps.messages.outputs.exit }}" }, 'node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs" extract gitleaks "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/messages.json" messages'],
  };
  for (const [id, [env, run]] of Object.entries(counts)) {
    const step = stepById("hygiene", id);
    if (!step) continue;
    if (!sameMap(step.env, env)) problems.push(`${id} env must be exactly ${Object.keys(env).join(" and ")} from the scan step`);
    if (runOf(step).trim() !== run) problems.push(`${id} must run ${run}`);
  }
  const outputs = {
    history: "${{ steps.history_counts.outputs.history }}",
    messages: "${{ steps.messages_counts.outputs.messages }}",
    canary: "${{ steps.canary.outcome }}",
    actionlint: "${{ steps.actionlint.outcome }}",
  };
  if (!sameMap(job.outputs, outputs)) problems.push("outputs must be exactly history, messages, canary and actionlint");
  if (job.needs !== undefined || job.if !== undefined) problems.push("hygiene must have no needs and no if");
  return problems;
});

check("summary", () => {
  const job = doc.jobs?.summary;
  if (!job) return ["no summary job"];
  const problems = [];
  const steps = jobSteps("summary");
  if (!Array.isArray(job.needs) || job.needs.join(",") !== "contracts,agent,web,hygiene") problems.push("needs must be exactly contracts, agent, web, hygiene");
  if (job.if !== ALWAYS) problems.push(`summary must have if: ${ALWAYS}`);
  if (job.outputs !== undefined) problems.push("summary must have no outputs");
  const checkout = steps[0]?.with ?? {};
  const patterns = String(checkout["sparse-checkout"] ?? "").split(/\r?\n/).map((line) => line.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  if (!usesOf(steps[0]).startsWith("actions/checkout@") || checkout["fetch-depth"] !== 1 || checkout["sparse-checkout-cone-mode"] !== false || patterns.join(" ") !== "/.github/ /web/package.json") {
    problems.push("step 1 must be a checkout with fetch-depth: 1, sparse-checkout-cone-mode: false and the patterns /.github/ and /web/package.json");
  }
  if (steps.some((step) => usesOf(step).startsWith("actions/setup-node@"))) problems.push("summary must not run setup-node");
  const ids = steps.slice(1).map((step) => step.id ?? "no id");
  if (ids.join(",") !== "tests,render,gate") problems.push(`step ids are ${ids.join(", ")}; expected tests, render, gate`);
  for (const step of steps.slice(1)) if (step.if !== ALWAYS) problems.push(`${step.id ?? "a step"} must have if: ${ALWAYS}`);
  if (runOf(stepById("summary", "tests")).trim() !== 'node --test ".github/scripts/*.test.mjs"') problems.push("tests must run the node --test suite");
  const render = stepById("summary", "render");
  if (!sameMap(render?.env, { NEEDS_JSON: "${{ toJSON(needs) }}" }) || runOf(render).trim() !== 'node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs" render') {
    problems.push("render must run ci-counts.mjs render with only NEEDS_JSON in env");
  }
  const gate = stepById("summary", "gate");
  const results = {
    CONTRACTS_RESULT: "${{ needs.contracts.result }}",
    AGENT_RESULT: "${{ needs.agent.result }}",
    WEB_RESULT: "${{ needs.web.result }}",
    HYGIENE_RESULT: "${{ needs.hygiene.result }}",
  };
  if (!sameMap(gate?.env, results)) problems.push("gate env must be exactly the four needs results");
  const gateRun = runOf(gate);
  if (/\bnode\b|ci-counts/.test(gateRun)) problems.push("gate must be plain shell, independent of the script");
  for (const name of Object.keys(results)) if (!gateRun.includes(`"$${name}"`) && !gateRun.includes(`=$${name}"`)) problems.push(`gate does not read ${name}`);
  if (!gateRun.includes("!= success") || !gateRun.includes('exit "$failed"')) problems.push("gate must exit 1 unless every result is success");
  return problems;
});

check("preflip", () => {
  const job = doc.jobs?.preflip;
  if (!job) return ["no preflip job"];
  const problems = [];
  const steps = jobSteps("preflip");
  if (job.if !== "${{ github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' && inputs.pre_flip }}") problems.push("preflip must run only on a dispatch with pre_flip");
  const input = doc.on?.workflow_dispatch?.inputs?.pre_flip;
  if (input?.type !== "boolean" || input?.default !== false) problems.push("workflow_dispatch must define the boolean input pre_flip, default false");
  if (job.needs !== undefined) problems.push("preflip must have no needs");
  if (!usesOf(steps[0]).startsWith("actions/checkout@") || steps[0].with?.["fetch-depth"] !== 0) problems.push("step 1 must be a checkout with fetch-depth: 0");
  const ids = steps.slice(1).map((step) => step.id ?? "no id");
  const expectedIds = ["outdir", "pulls", "ignorefile", "scan", "messages", "text", "text_scan"];
  if (ids.join(",") !== expectedIds.join(",")) problems.push(`step ids are ${ids.join(", ")}; expected ${expectedIds.join(", ")}`);
  const conditions = { outdir: undefined, pulls: undefined, ignorefile: undefined, scan: NOT_CANCELLED, messages: NOT_CANCELLED, text: NOT_CANCELLED, text_scan: NOT_CANCELLED };
  for (const [id, condition] of Object.entries(conditions)) {
    const step = stepById("preflip", id);
    if (step && step.if !== condition) problems.push(condition ? `${id} must have if: ${condition}` : `${id} must have no if`);
  }
  if (runOf(stepById("preflip", "outdir")).trim() !== OUTDIR) problems.push(`outdir must run ${OUTDIR}`);
  const pulls = stepById("preflip", "pulls");
  if (!sameMap(pulls?.env, { TOKEN: "${{ github.token }}" }) || runOf(pulls).trim() !== PULLS_FETCH) problems.push("pulls must run the GIT_CONFIG_* fetch of refs/pull/* with only TOKEN in env");
  if (runOf(stepById("preflip", "ignorefile")) !== runOf(stepById("hygiene", "ignorefile"))) problems.push("ignorefile must be the same as in hygiene");
  if (!runOf(stepById("preflip", "messages")).includes(MESSAGES_LOG)) problems.push("messages must write every commit message to /out/text");
  const textStep = stepById("preflip", "text");
  if (!sameMap(textStep?.env, { GH_TOKEN: "${{ github.token }}" })) problems.push("text env must be exactly GH_TOKEN");
  for (const endpoint of TEXT_ENDPOINTS) {
    if (!runOf(textStep).includes(`gh api --paginate "repos/$GITHUB_REPOSITORY/${endpoint}"`)) problems.push(`text does not read ${endpoint}`);
  }
  if (!runOf(textStep).includes('text="$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/text"')) problems.push("text must write under $RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/text");
  const tokenUses = (text.match(/github\.token/g) ?? []).length;
  if (tokenUses !== 2) problems.push(`github.token appears ${tokenUses} times; expected 2 (pulls and text)`);
  return problems;
});

const failed = results.filter((ok) => !ok).length;
console.log(`policy: ${results.length - failed} ok, ${failed} failed`);
process.exitCode = failed === 0 ? 0 : 1;
```

```js
// Checks the hygiene job's four-key canary before the first push.
// Usage: node canary-check.mjs <path to ci.yml> <path to .gitleaks.toml>
// For each seed it derives the planted value with node:crypto (sha256, hex), and checks that:
//   seed-in-workflow  the canary step derives and plants exactly this value from this seed,
//   sha256sum         the workflow's own shell derivation, run in bash, gives the same value,
//   stopword          no default generic-api-key stopword of gitleaks v8.30.1 is inside the value,
//   rule              the rule that must fire matches the planted line, captures this value and passes its entropy
//                     and allowlists,
//   seven             (fixture only) .gitleaks.toml holds seven stopwords and none is inside the value.
// Prints rule names, lengths and ok/FAIL only, never a value. Exit 0 on "canary: ok".
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";
import { DEFAULT_TOML, entropy, loadDefaultRules, readStopwords } from "./gl-stopwords.mjs";

const YAML = (await import(pathToFileURL("E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/web/node_modules/yaml/dist/index.js").href)).default;

const H_FUNCTION = String.raw`h() { printf '%s' "$1" | sha256sum | cut -c1-64; }`;
const CANARY = [
  {
    rule: "maat-evm-private-key",
    seed: "maat-ci-canary-evm-private-key",
    variable: "evm",
    take: 64,
    path: "canary.txt",
    snippets: [String.raw`evm="$(h maat-ci-canary-evm-private-key)"`, String.raw`printf 'PRIVATE_KEY=0x%s\n' "$evm"`, String.raw`} > "$canary/canary.txt"`],
    line: (value) => `PRIVATE_KEY=0x${value}`,
    secret: (value) => value,
  },
  {
    rule: "github-pat",
    seed: "maat-ci-canary-github-pat",
    variable: "pat",
    take: 36,
    path: "canary.txt",
    snippets: [String.raw`pat="$(h maat-ci-canary-github-pat | cut -c1-36)"`, String.raw`printf 'ghp_%s\n' "$pat"`, String.raw`} > "$canary/canary.txt"`],
    line: (value) => `ghp_${value}`,
    secret: (value) => `ghp_${value}`,
  },
  {
    rule: "github-pat",
    seed: "maat-ci-canary-github-pat-allow",
    variable: "allow",
    take: 36,
    path: "canary.txt",
    snippets: [String.raw`allow="$(h maat-ci-canary-github-pat-allow | cut -c1-36)"`, String.raw`printf 'ghp_%s # gitleaks:allow\n' "$allow"`, String.raw`} > "$canary/canary.txt"`],
    line: (value) => `ghp_${value} # gitleaks:allow`,
    secret: (value) => `ghp_${value}`,
  },
  {
    rule: "generic-api-key",
    seed: "maat-ci-canary-agent-fixture",
    variable: "fixture",
    take: 40,
    path: "agent/test/x.test.ts",
    snippets: [String.raw`fixture="$(h maat-ci-canary-agent-fixture | cut -c1-40)"`, String.raw`printf 'const KEY = "%s";\n' "$fixture" > "$canary/agent/test/x.test.ts"`],
    line: (value) => `const KEY = "${value}";`,
    secret: (value) => value,
  },
];

const [workflowPath, gitleaksTomlPath] = process.argv.slice(2);
if (!workflowPath || !gitleaksTomlPath) {
  console.error("usage: node canary-check.mjs <path to ci.yml> <path to .gitleaks.toml>");
  process.exit(1);
}
const workflow = YAML.parse(readFileSync(workflowPath, "utf8")) ?? {};
const canaryStep = (workflow.jobs?.hygiene?.steps ?? []).find((step) => step.id === "canary");
const canaryRun = typeof canaryStep?.run === "string" ? canaryStep.run : "";
const gitleaksToml = readFileSync(gitleaksTomlPath, "utf8");
const defaults = loadDefaultRules();
const seven = readStopwords(gitleaksToml).map((word) => word.toLowerCase());

/** A JavaScript RegExp from a gitleaks (Go) pattern whose only inline flag is a leading (?i). */
function goRegex(source, flags = "") {
  return source.startsWith("(?i)") ? new RegExp(source.slice(4), `${flags}i`) : new RegExp(source, flags);
}
/** Reads the regex, entropy and keywords of one [[rules]] block from a gitleaks TOML text. */
function tomlRule(tomlText, id) {
  const start = tomlText.indexOf(`id = "${id}"`);
  if (start === -1) throw new Error(`rule ${id} not found`);
  const next = tomlText.indexOf("[[rules]]", start);
  const block = tomlText.slice(start, next === -1 ? undefined : next);
  const regex = /^regex = '''(.*)'''\r?$/m.exec(block)?.[1];
  const entropyMatch = /^entropy = ([\d.]+)\r?$/m.exec(block);
  const keywords = [...(/^keywords = \[(.*)\]\r?$/m.exec(block)?.[1] ?? "").matchAll(/"([^"]+)"/g)].map((match) => match[1].toLowerCase());
  return { regex: goRegex(regex, "g"), entropy: entropyMatch ? Number(entropyMatch[1]) : 0, keywords };
}
const fresh = (regex, flags) => new RegExp(regex.source, (flags ?? regex.flags).replace("g", ""));
const inGlobalPaths = (path) => defaults.globalPaths.some((regex) => fresh(regex).test(path));

const RULES = {
  "maat-evm-private-key": tomlRule(gitleaksToml, "maat-evm-private-key"),
  "github-pat": tomlRule(readFileSync(DEFAULT_TOML, "utf8"), "github-pat"),
  "generic-api-key": {
    regex: new RegExp(defaults.regex.source, `${defaults.regex.flags.replace("g", "")}g`),
    entropy: defaults.entropy,
    keywords: defaults.keywords.map((word) => word.toLowerCase()),
  },
};

/** True when the rule fires on the planted line with exactly the expected secret. */
function fires(item, value) {
  const rule = RULES[item.rule];
  const line = item.line(value);
  const secret = item.secret(value);
  if (!rule.keywords.some((keyword) => line.toLowerCase().includes(keyword))) return false;
  if (inGlobalPaths(item.path)) return false;
  for (const match of line.matchAll(rule.regex)) {
    const captured = match.slice(1).find((group) => group !== undefined && group !== "") ?? match[0];
    if (captured !== secret) continue;
    if (rule.entropy !== 0 && entropy(captured) <= rule.entropy) continue;
    if (item.rule === "generic-api-key") {
      if (defaults.secretAllow.some((regex) => fresh(regex).test(captured))) continue;
      if (fresh(defaults.matchAllow).test(match[0])) continue;
    }
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

let ok = true;
const mark = (pass) => {
  if (!pass) ok = false;
  return pass ? "ok" : "FAIL";
};
for (const item of CANARY) {
  const value = createHash("sha256").update(item.seed, "utf8").digest("hex").slice(0, item.take);
  const secret = item.secret(value);
  const inWorkflow = [H_FUNCTION, ...item.snippets].every((snippet) => canaryRun.includes(snippet));
  const shell = spawnSync("bash", ["-c", `${H_FUNCTION}\n${item.snippets[0]}\nprintf '%s' "$${item.variable}"`], { encoding: "utf8" });
  const sameInShell = shell.status === 0 && shell.stdout.trim() === value;
  const noStopword = !defaults.stopwords.some((word) => secret.toLowerCase().includes(word.toLowerCase()));
  const parts = [
    `${item.rule} len=${secret.length}`,
    `seed-in-workflow=${mark(inWorkflow)}`,
    `sha256sum=${mark(sameInShell)}`,
    `stopword=${mark(noStopword)}`,
    `rule=${mark(fires(item, value))}`,
  ];
  if (item.rule === "generic-api-key") {
    parts.push(`seven=${mark(seven.length === 7 && !seven.some((word) => value.toLowerCase().includes(word)))}`);
  }
  console.log(parts.join(" "));
}
console.log(ok ? "canary: ok" : "canary: FAIL");
process.exitCode = ok ? 0 : 1;
```

The `sha256sum` check runs the workflow's own `h` function and derivation line in `bash` (Git Bash locally, where `sha256sum` is `/usr/bin/sha256sum`), and compares the result with `node:crypto`. The `rule` check for `generic-api-key` uses the default rule, entropy and allowlists from `loadDefaultRules()`. For all four values, two global lists are not re-checked: the `regexes = [` list under `[allowlist]` in `gl-default-v8.30.1.toml` (it matches `true`, `false`, `null`, one repeated letter, `$`, `%`, `{` and `@` placeholders, and `/Users/` or `/bin/`-style paths), and its `stopwords` list (a UUID and `abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz`). Neither can match a value made only of hex digits, or `ghp_` and hex digits.

Run:

```bash
node C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/e5-plan-work/tools/canary-check.mjs E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/.github/workflows/ci.yml E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/.gitleaks.toml; echo "exit=$?"
```

Expected failure (no canary step yet), exactly:

```text
maat-evm-private-key len=64 seed-in-workflow=FAIL sha256sum=ok stopword=ok rule=ok
github-pat len=40 seed-in-workflow=FAIL sha256sum=ok stopword=ok rule=ok
github-pat len=40 seed-in-workflow=FAIL sha256sum=ok stopword=ok rule=ok
generic-api-key len=40 seed-in-workflow=FAIL sha256sum=ok stopword=ok rule=ok seven=ok
canary: FAIL
exit=1
```

If a line shows `stopword=FAIL`, a default stopword sits inside that derived value, and spec 4.4 says the plan then changes that seed. Append the fixed suffix `-2` to that seed (for example `maat-ci-canary-github-pat` becomes `maat-ci-canary-github-pat-2`) in both places that name it, with the Edit tool: the `seed:` value and the first `snippets` entry of its `CANARY` item in `canary-check.mjs`, and its derivation line in Step 4's block, which is then written with the new seed (if Step 4 is already done, the same line in `ci.yml`). Then run this step's command again. If the same line still shows `stopword=FAIL`, replace the suffix with `-3`, then `-4`. If `-4` still fails, or the change turns another check of that line to `FAIL`, stop and report the line, without printing a value. A changed seed is recorded in the task report, since Shared names lists the seeds without a suffix. No value is printed at any point.

Any other `FAIL` except `seed-in-workflow` means the derivation, a rule or Task 3's config disagrees with this plan: stop and report the line, without printing a value.

- [ ] **Step 4: Append the hygiene, summary and preflip jobs**

Find the anchor:

```bash
grep -n "retention-days: 7" E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/.github/workflows/ci.yml
wc -l E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/.github/workflows/ci.yml
```

Expected: exactly one `grep` line, `164:          retention-days: 7`, and `172 E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/.github/workflows/ci.yml` (Task 4's file); call these N = 164 and L = 172. Read lines N to L of the file with the Read tool: they are the end of the `web` job's `traces` step and its whole `clean` step, the end of the file, and the last one is `          fi`. Then Edit the file:
- `old_string` is lines N to L exactly as read, from `          retention-days: 7` to `          fi`, with no newline after `fi` (the file's own final newline is not part of it).
- `new_string` is that same text, then two newline characters, then the block below without its final newline. The file's existing final newline then ends the block, so the result has one empty line between `          fi` and `  hygiene:` and no empty line at the end.

Every step after the checkout and setup-node steps carries the id Shared names gives it; the action steps carry none, as in Task 4.

```yaml
  hygiene:
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 10
    outputs:
      history: ${{ steps.history_counts.outputs.history }}
      messages: ${{ steps.messages_counts.outputs.messages }}
      canary: ${{ steps.canary.outcome }}
      actionlint: ${{ steps.actionlint.outcome }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: actions/setup-node@949feb2413d6458794dcd2491c4babbbce0c15c1 # v7.1.0
        with:
          node-version: 24
      - id: outdir
        run: mkdir -p "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks"
      - id: ignorefile
        run: |
          if [ -e .gitleaksignore ]; then
            echo "::error file=.gitleaksignore::.gitleaksignore must not exist: gitleaks reads it from the scanned tree, so it could silence a finding"
            exit 1
          fi
      - id: canary
        run: |
          h() { printf '%s' "$1" | sha256sum | cut -c1-64; }
          evm="$(h maat-ci-canary-evm-private-key)"
          pat="$(h maat-ci-canary-github-pat | cut -c1-36)"
          allow="$(h maat-ci-canary-github-pat-allow | cut -c1-36)"
          fixture="$(h maat-ci-canary-agent-fixture | cut -c1-40)"
          canary="$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/canary"
          mkdir -p "$canary/agent/test"
          {
            printf 'PRIVATE_KEY=0x%s\n' "$evm"
            printf 'ghp_%s\n' "$pat"
            printf 'ghp_%s # gitleaks:allow\n' "$allow"
          } > "$canary/canary.txt"
          printf 'const KEY = "%s";\n' "$fixture" > "$canary/agent/test/x.test.ts"
          git -C "$canary" init -q
          git -C "$canary" add canary.txt agent/test/x.test.ts
          git -C "$canary" -c user.name=canary -c user.email=canary@invalid commit -q -m canary
          code=0
          docker run --rm \
            -v "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE:/repo:ro" \
            -v "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks:/out" \
            zricethezav/gitleaks@sha256:c00b6bd0aeb3071cbcb79009cb16a60dd9e0a7c60e2be9ab65d25e6bc8abbb7f \
            git --config /repo/.gitleaks.toml --redact --no-banner --ignore-gitleaks-allow \
            --exit-code 3 --report-format json --report-path /out/canary.json \
            /out/canary || code=$?
          if [ "$code" -ne 3 ]; then
            echo "::error::canary: gitleaks exited $code, expected 3"
            exit 1
          fi
          node -e '
            const findings = JSON.parse(require("node:fs").readFileSync(process.argv[1], "utf8"));
            if (!Array.isArray(findings)) throw new Error("the canary report is not a JSON array");
            const count = (rule, file) => findings.filter((f) => f.RuleID === rule && (file === undefined || f.File === file)).length;
            const evm = count("maat-evm-private-key");
            const pat = count("github-pat");
            const fixture = count("generic-api-key", "agent/test/x.test.ts");
            console.log("canary findings " + findings.length + ": maat-evm-private-key " + evm + ", github-pat " + pat + ", generic-api-key in agent/test/x.test.ts " + fixture);
            if (evm < 1 || pat !== 2 || fixture < 1) {
              console.log("::error::canary: expected at least 1 maat-evm-private-key, exactly 2 github-pat and at least 1 generic-api-key in agent/test/x.test.ts");
              process.exit(1);
            }
          ' "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/canary.json"
      - id: scan
        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
        run: |
          commits="$(git rev-list --all --count)"
          code=0
          docker run --rm \
            -v "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE:/repo:ro" \
            -v "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks:/out" \
            zricethezav/gitleaks@sha256:c00b6bd0aeb3071cbcb79009cb16a60dd9e0a7c60e2be9ab65d25e6bc8abbb7f \
            git --config /repo/.gitleaks.toml --redact --no-banner --ignore-gitleaks-allow \
            --exit-code 3 --report-format json --report-path /out/history.json \
            --log-opts="--all --full-history --diff-merges=first-parent --diff-filter=tuxdb" \
            /repo || code=$?
          {
            echo "exit=$code"
            echo "commits=$commits"
          } >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          exit "$code"
      - id: messages
        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
        run: |
          mkdir -p "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/text"
          git log --all --format='%H%n%B' > "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/text/commit-messages.txt"
          code=0
          docker run --rm \
            -v "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE:/repo:ro" \
            -v "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks:/out" \
            zricethezav/gitleaks@sha256:c00b6bd0aeb3071cbcb79009cb16a60dd9e0a7c60e2be9ab65d25e6bc8abbb7f \
            dir --config /repo/.gitleaks.toml --redact --no-banner --ignore-gitleaks-allow \
            --exit-code 3 --report-format json --report-path /out/messages.json \
            /out/text || code=$?
          echo "exit=$code" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          exit "$code"
      - id: history_counts
        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
        env:
          SCAN_EXIT: ${{ steps.scan.outputs.exit }}
          COMMITS: ${{ steps.scan.outputs.commits }}
        run: node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs" extract gitleaks "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/history.json" history
      - id: messages_counts
        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
        env:
          SCAN_EXIT: ${{ steps.messages.outputs.exit }}
        run: node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs" extract gitleaks "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/messages.json" messages
      - id: actionlint
        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
        run: |
          docker run --rm \
            -v "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE:/repo:ro" \
            -w /repo \
            rhysd/actionlint@sha256:b1934ee5f1c509618f2508e6eb47ee0d3520686341fec936f3b79331f9315667

  summary:
    needs: [contracts, agent, web, hygiene]
    if: ${{ always() }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-slim
    timeout-minutes: 5
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          fetch-depth: 1
          persist-credentials: false
          sparse-checkout-cone-mode: false
          sparse-checkout: |
            /.github/
            /web/package.json
      - id: tests
        if: ${{ always() }}
        run: node --test ".github/scripts/*.test.mjs"
      - id: render
        if: ${{ always() }}
        env:
          NEEDS_JSON: ${{ toJSON(needs) }}
        run: node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/.github/scripts/ci-counts.mjs" render
      - id: gate
        if: ${{ always() }}
        env:
          CONTRACTS_RESULT: ${{ needs.contracts.result }}
          AGENT_RESULT: ${{ needs.agent.result }}
          WEB_RESULT: ${{ needs.web.result }}
          HYGIENE_RESULT: ${{ needs.hygiene.result }}
        run: |
          failed=0
          for pair in "contracts=$CONTRACTS_RESULT" "agent=$AGENT_RESULT" "web=$WEB_RESULT" "hygiene=$HYGIENE_RESULT"; do
            echo "$pair"
            if [ "${pair#*=}" != success ]; then
              failed=1
            fi
          done
          exit "$failed"

  preflip:
    if: ${{ github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' && inputs.pre_flip }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 15
    permissions:
      contents: read
      pull-requests: read
      issues: read
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0
          persist-credentials: false
      - id: outdir
        run: mkdir -p "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks"
      - id: pulls
        env:
          TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
        run: |
          GIT_CONFIG_COUNT=1 GIT_CONFIG_KEY_0=http.https://github.com/.extraheader GIT_CONFIG_VALUE_0="AUTHORIZATION: basic $(printf 'x-access-token:%s' "$TOKEN" | base64 -w0)" git fetch --no-tags origin '+refs/pull/*:refs/remotes/pull/*'
      - id: ignorefile
        run: |
          if [ -e .gitleaksignore ]; then
            echo "::error file=.gitleaksignore::.gitleaksignore must not exist: gitleaks reads it from the scanned tree, so it could silence a finding"
            exit 1
          fi
      - id: scan
        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
        run: |
          docker run --rm \
            -v "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE:/repo:ro" \
            -v "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks:/out" \
            zricethezav/gitleaks@sha256:c00b6bd0aeb3071cbcb79009cb16a60dd9e0a7c60e2be9ab65d25e6bc8abbb7f \
            git --config /repo/.gitleaks.toml --redact --no-banner --ignore-gitleaks-allow \
            --exit-code 3 --report-format json --report-path /out/history.json \
            --log-opts="--all --full-history --diff-merges=first-parent --diff-filter=tuxdb" \
            /repo
      - id: messages
        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
        run: |
          mkdir -p "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/text"
          git log --all --format='%H%n%B' > "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/text/commit-messages.txt"
          docker run --rm \
            -v "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE:/repo:ro" \
            -v "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks:/out" \
            zricethezav/gitleaks@sha256:c00b6bd0aeb3071cbcb79009cb16a60dd9e0a7c60e2be9ab65d25e6bc8abbb7f \
            dir --config /repo/.gitleaks.toml --redact --no-banner --ignore-gitleaks-allow \
            --exit-code 3 --report-format json --report-path /out/messages.json \
            /out/text
      - id: text
        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
        run: |
          text="$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks/text"
          mkdir -p "$text"
          gh api --paginate "repos/$GITHUB_REPOSITORY/issues?state=all&per_page=100" \
            --jq '.[] | .html_url, .title, (.body // "")' > "$text/issues-and-pulls.txt"
          gh api --paginate "repos/$GITHUB_REPOSITORY/issues/comments?per_page=100" \
            --jq '.[] | .html_url, (.body // "")' > "$text/issue-comments.txt"
          gh api --paginate "repos/$GITHUB_REPOSITORY/pulls/comments?per_page=100" \
            --jq '.[] | .html_url, (.body // "")' > "$text/review-comments.txt"
          gh api --paginate "repos/$GITHUB_REPOSITORY/comments?per_page=100" \
            --jq '.[] | .html_url, (.body // "")' > "$text/commit-comments.txt"
          gh api --paginate "repos/$GITHUB_REPOSITORY/pulls?state=all&per_page=100" --jq '.[].number' |
            while read -r number; do
              gh api --paginate "repos/$GITHUB_REPOSITORY/pulls/$number/reviews?per_page=100" \
                --jq '.[] | .html_url, (.body // "")' < /dev/null
            done > "$text/review-bodies.txt"
          wc -l "$text"/*.txt
      - id: text_scan
        if: ${{ !cancelled() }}
        run: |
          docker run --rm \
            -v "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE:/repo:ro" \
            -v "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks:/out" \
            zricethezav/gitleaks@sha256:c00b6bd0aeb3071cbcb79009cb16a60dd9e0a7c60e2be9ab65d25e6bc8abbb7f \
            dir --config /repo/.gitleaks.toml --redact --no-banner --ignore-gitleaks-allow \
            --exit-code 3 --report-format json --report-path /out/text.json \
            /out/text
```

Check the result of the Edit:

```bash
wc -l E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/.github/workflows/ci.yml
tail -n 2 E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/.github/workflows/ci.yml | cat -A
```

Expected, exactly: the file has 411 lines and ends with `            /out/text` and exactly one newline (`cat -A` marks each line end with `$`):

```text
411 E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/.github/workflows/ci.yml
            --exit-code 3 --report-format json --report-path /out/text.json \$
            /out/text$
```

What each part guarantees, against the spec:
- Every gitleaks `docker run` mounts exactly `-v "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE:/repo:ro"` and `-v "$RUNNER_TEMP/gitleaks:/out"`; `$RUNNER_TEMP` itself is never mounted (4.4). The image runs as root and presets `safe.directory '*'`, so it reads the runner-owned canary repository and writes `/out/*.json` (4.4).
- The planted values never exist in this repository: the workflow holds only the four seeds and `sha256sum` (4.4 step 1). The containment check needs exit 3, at least one `maat-evm-private-key`, exactly two `github-pat` (one of them on the `gitleaks:allow` line, which `--ignore-gitleaks-allow` turns off) and at least one `generic-api-key` in `agent/test/x.test.ts` (the path the Maat allowlist covers, with a value that holds none of its seven stopwords); any other finding is allowed.
- `scan` appends `exit=` and `commits=` before it exits with gitleaks' code; `messages` appends `exit=` (4.4 steps 2 and 3). `history_counts` and `messages_counts` pass those outputs to `extract gitleaks` through `env:` only (4.4 step 4).
- `summary` has no `name:`, no setup-node and no outputs; its three steps and the job itself run on `always()`, so a cancelled run shows a red `summary` (4.5).
- `preflip` holds the only two `github.token` uses, both through `env:` (spec 1 criterion 3, 4.6).

```bash
node -e '
const YAML = require("E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/web/node_modules/yaml");
const { spawnSync } = require("node:child_process");
const doc = YAML.parse(require("node:fs").readFileSync(process.argv[1], "utf8"));
const bad = [];
for (const [name, job] of Object.entries(doc.jobs)) {
  for (const step of job.steps) {
    if (typeof step.run === "string" && spawnSync("bash", ["-n", "-c", step.run]).status !== 0) bad.push(name + "." + (step.id ?? "?"));
  }
}
console.log(bad.length === 0 ? "bash -n: every run block parses" : "bash -n: FAIL " + bad.join(", "));
const gate = doc.jobs.summary.steps.find((step) => step.id === "gate").run;
for (const hygiene of ["success", "failure", "cancelled", "skipped", ""]) {
  const env = { ...process.env, CONTRACTS_RESULT: "success", AGENT_RESULT: "success", WEB_RESULT: "success", HYGIENE_RESULT: hygiene };
  const result = spawnSync("bash", ["-eo", "pipefail", "-c", gate], { env, encoding: "utf8" });
  console.log("gate hygiene=" + (hygiene || "(empty)") + " exit=" + result.status);
}
' E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/.github/workflows/ci.yml
```

```js
// Task 6's check of .github/dependabot.yml, SECURITY.md and the two README.md edits (spec 7.1, 7.2, 7.3).
// Usage: node repo-files-check.mjs <repo>
// Prints "ok <id>" or "FAIL <id>: <detail>" for each of five checks, then "check: <a> ok, <b> failed".
// Exits 0 when b is 0, else 1. It only reads: the files under <repo> and `git show 8608b7d:README.md`.
import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";
import { isDeepStrictEqual } from "node:util";

const YAML_ENTRY = "E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/web/node_modules/yaml/dist/index.js";
const YAML = (await import(pathToFileURL(YAML_ENTRY).href)).default;

export const BASE = "8608b7d";

export const DEPENDABOT = {
  version: 2,
  updates: [
    {
      "package-ecosystem": "github-actions",
      directory: "/",
      schedule: { interval: "monthly" },
      groups: { actions: { patterns: ["*"] } },
    },
  ],
};

// Spec 7.1, one entry per bullet that SECURITY.md must state; matched after runs of whitespace become one space.
export const SECURITY_SAYS = [
  "The `main` branch, and the contracts at the addresses listed in [`deployments/`](deployments/) once they are listed there.",
  "The contracts are unaudited.",
  "A report can cover `contracts/src`, `contracts/script`, `agent/src` and `web/`, and the contracts at the addresses listed in [`deployments/`](deployments/).",
  "(Base, Chainlink, Aerodrome, Aave V4, the B20 tokens, CDP or USDC) goes to that party.",
  "If it changes what the Maat vault or agent does, report the Maat side here.",
  "Send a direct message to [@maatonbase on X](https://x.com/maatonbase)",
  "ask for a private channel.",
  "Send the details only after that channel is agreed.",
  "Never open a public issue or a public pull request, and never post the details in a public reply.",
  "Never include a real private key, seed phrase or API key",
  "A report never needs one.",
  "**No bounty.**",
  "**Credit on request.**",
  "**No promised response time.**",
  "Every pull request and every push to `main` runs a secret scan, with redacted logs, over the full history of every branch, each merge's own changes, and every commit message.",
];

// Spec 7.1, what SECURITY.md must not say yet.
export const SECURITY_OMITS = [
  { pattern: /maatonbase\.xyz/i, why: "the docs link goes in only once that page is served" },
  { pattern: /private vulnerability report|security advisor|report form/i, why: "no GitHub report form" },
  { pattern: /\bdeployed\b|\bmainnet\b/i, why: "no claim about whether anything is deployed" },
  { pattern: /private repositor|repository is (?:private|public)/i, why: "no claim that the flip to public makes false" },
];

// Spec 7.3: the badge verbatim, and the paragraph that replaces README.md:523.
export const OLD_BADGE =
  "[![Tests: 535, 511 offline](https://img.shields.io/badge/tests-535%20(511%20offline)-15803d?style=flat-square)](#development)";
export const BADGE =
  "[![CI](https://github.com/duongnq2798/maat/actions/workflows/ci.yml/badge.svg?branch=main&event=push)](https://github.com/duongnq2798/maat/actions/workflows/ci.yml)";
export const OLD_SENTENCE = "There is no CI in this repository yet; the counts above were measured locally at this commit.";
export const PARAGRAPH = [
  "CI ([`.github/workflows/ci.yml`](.github/workflows/ci.yml)) runs the four offline suites above (contracts",
  "unit, agent unit, web unit and web end to end), a secret scan of the full history and of every commit",
  "message, and a lint of the workflow itself, on every pull request and every push to `main`. Each run's",
  "summary shows the counts each runner reported. The contracts fork suite and the agent fork integration suite",
  "need an archive RPC and run by hand.",
];

const lines = (text) => text.split(/\r?\n/);
const flat = (text) => text.replace(/\s+/g, " ");

/** @param {string} root @param {string} rel @returns {string | null} null when the file does not exist */
function read(root, rel) {
  const file = path.join(root, rel);
  return existsSync(file) ? readFileSync(file, "utf8") : null;
}

/** @param {string} text @returns {string | null} */
function dependabotDetail(text) {
  let parsed;
  try {
    parsed = YAML.parse(text);
  } catch (error) {
    return `does not parse: ${String(error.message).split("\n")[0]}`;
  }
  return isDeepStrictEqual(parsed, DEPENDABOT) ? null : `parses to ${JSON.stringify(parsed)}`;
}

/**
 * @param {string} root the repository root
 * @param {string} baseReadme README.md at 8608b7d
 * @returns {{ id: string, detail: string | null }[]} detail is null when the check passes
 */
export function runChecks(root, baseReadme) {
  const results = [];
  const add = (id, detail) => results.push({ id, detail });

  const dependabot = read(root, ".github/dependabot.yml");
  add("dependabot", dependabot === null ? ".github/dependabot.yml is missing" : dependabotDetail(dependabot));

  const security = read(root, "SECURITY.md");
  if (security === null) {
    add("security-says", "SECURITY.md is missing");
    add("security-omits", "SECURITY.md is missing");
  } else {
    const text = flat(security);
    const missing = SECURITY_SAYS.find((phrase) => !text.includes(phrase));
    add("security-says", missing === undefined ? null : `missing "${missing}"`);
    const hit = SECURITY_OMITS.map(({ pattern, why }) => ({ match: text.match(pattern), why })).find(
      ({ match }) => match !== null,
    );
    add("security-omits", hit === undefined ? null : `says "${hit.match[0]}" (${hit.why})`);
  }

  const readme = read(root, "README.md") ?? "";
  const now = lines(readme);
  const before = lines(baseReadme);
  if (now[11] !== BADGE) add("readme-badge", "line 12 is not the CI badge");
  else if (readme.includes("img.shields.io/badge/tests-")) add("readme-badge", "a static test-count badge is still there");
  else add("readme-badge", null);

  const at = before.indexOf(OLD_SENTENCE);
  if (readme.includes("There is no CI")) {
    add("readme-ci", 'the "no CI" sentence is still there');
  } else if (before[11] !== OLD_BADGE || at !== 522) {
    add("readme-ci", `${BASE}:README.md does not hold the old badge at line 12 and the old sentence at line 523`);
  } else {
    // Every line but 12 must equal 8608b7d's README with line 523 replaced by the paragraph.
    const expected = [...before.slice(0, at), ...PARAGRAPH, ...before.slice(at + 1)];
    const differs = expected.findIndex((line, i) => i !== 11 && now[i] !== line);
    if (differs !== -1) add("readme-ci", `line ${differs + 1} differs from the expected README`);
    else if (now.length !== expected.length) add("readme-ci", `${now.length} lines, expected ${expected.length}`);
    else add("readme-ci", null);
  }
  return results;
}

/** @param {string[]} argv @returns {0 | 1} */
function main(argv) {
  if (argv.length !== 1) {
    console.error("usage: repo-files-check.mjs <repo>");
    return 1;
  }
  const root = argv[0];
  const baseReadme = execFileSync("git", ["-C", root, "show", `${BASE}:README.md`], { encoding: "utf8" });
  const results = runChecks(root, baseReadme);
  for (const { id, detail } of results) console.log(detail === null ? `ok ${id}` : `FAIL ${id}: ${detail}`);
  const failed = results.filter(({ detail }) => detail !== null).length;
  console.log(`check: ${results.length - failed} ok, ${failed} failed`);
  return failed === 0 ? 0 : 1;
}

if (import.meta.main) process.exitCode = main(process.argv.slice(2));
```

Create `E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/.github/dependabot.yml` with the Write tool (the file has no expressions, steps or jobs; `"*"` is quoted because a bare `*` starts a YAML alias, and `"/"` is quoted to match):

```yaml
# Updates the actions that .github/workflows/ci.yml pins by commit SHA, once a month, all in one pull request.
# Why monthly, and why npm waits: docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-18-maat-ci-design.md, section 7.2.
version: 2
updates:
  - package-ecosystem: github-actions
    directory: "/"
    schedule:
      interval: monthly
    groups:
      actions:
        patterns:
          - "*"
```

Dependabot rewrites each pinned SHA together with its trailing `# vX.Y.Z` comment (research note 04 §2.9), so the `uses:` lines of Tasks 4-5 stay in the form the Global Constraints require. The two images run by `docker run` are not tracked by it (spec 3); their bumps stay manual.

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `SECURITY.md`**

Create `E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci/SECURITY.md` with the Write tool, exactly these 35 lines (LF endings, one final newline):

```markdown
# Security policy

How to report a security problem in Maat privately, and what to expect after you report it.

## Supported versions

The `main` branch, and the contracts at the addresses listed in [`deployments/`](deployments/) once they are
listed there. The contracts are unaudited.

## Scope

A report can cover `contracts/src`, `contracts/script`, `agent/src` and `web/`, and the contracts at the
addresses listed in [`deployments/`](deployments/). A bug in a third party's code (Base, Chainlink,
Aerodrome, Aave V4, the B20 tokens, CDP or USDC) goes to that party. If it changes what the Maat vault or
agent does, report the Maat side here.

## How to report

Send a direct message to [@maatonbase on X](https://x.com/maatonbase), say that you have found a security
problem, and ask for a private channel. Send the details only after that channel is agreed.

Never open a public issue or a public pull request, and never post the details in a public reply.

Never include a real private key, seed phrase or API key, yours or anyone's. A report never needs one.

## What to expect

- **No bounty.** Maat has no bug bounty.
- **Credit on request.** Say how you want to be named, or that you want none.
- **No promised response time.** This file promises no response time and no date for a fix.

## Continuous integration

Every pull request and every push to `main` runs a secret scan, with redacted logs, over the full history of
every branch, each merge's own changes, and every commit message.
```

The file has no link to `https://maatonbase.xyz/docs/security/report-a-vulnerability` (Task 9 adds it only if that URL serves the page), names no GitHub report form, and says nothing about whether anything is deployed or whether the repository is private, so neither the deploy nor the flip makes it false.

The tool writes only `<run>/pr-body.md` and `<run>/run-<id>.json`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the branch `chore/ci` with Tasks 1-6; `<run>/pr-body.md`.
- Produces: with the owner's explicit approval in chat, `git -C E:/APP2028/stocks-maxxing-hackathon/.claude/worktrees/ci push -u origin chore/ci` and `gh pr create --base main --head chore/ci --title "ci: add a secret-free CI workflow, a secret scan and SECURITY.md" --body-file <run>/pr-body.md`; the PR bound with the app's PR tools (no polling, no sleep loops, no `gh run watch`); from the finished run, `gh run view <run-id> --json jobs` for each job's `startedAt` and `completedAt`, written into the PR body as each job's duration and the billed minutes (each job rounded up to a whole minute; Linux and slim apart) next to the estimate of 18 to 26 Linux minutes plus 1 slim minute; the scanned-commit count in the `scan` step's log (`gh run view <run-id> --log --job <hygiene job id>`) compared with the history row's `M commits`. The PR body ends with `🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)`.
- Owner gate: the push and the PR wait for the owner's approval in chat. A Linux-only failure is reported as it is, never hidden.
- Commits: fix commits only, if any.

Smallest choices where the spec is silent, one line each:
- No earlier task writes `<run>/pr-body.md`, so this task's tool writes it: first with a "Not measured yet" section, then, after the first green run and with the owner's approval, with the measured section.
- The run reader is a scratchpad tool with its own `node:test` file, because the spec gives the PR body no committed test; it runs only read-only `git` and `gh` commands.
- Billed minutes are computed from each job's `startedAt` and `completedAt`, rounded up per job ("GitHub rounds the minutes and partial minutes each job uses up to the nearest whole minute", research note 04 §3); a skipped job bills 0; no billing API is called.
- The summary table is read on the run page, as in Task 9 (the job summary has no CLI): in the app's Browser pane when it is already signed in to GitHub, otherwise pasted by the owner.
- The executor never polls: it reads the run once each time the owner says the run has finished.
- Only the two fallbacks of spec 10 (Fix A, Fix B) and one manual re-run after a download failure are written out; any other failure, and a second download failure in the same job, is reported with its question of Step 13 and stops the task, and its fix is planned as its own step with the complete change before anything is committed.
- The `web` timeout stays 30 in this task, and the PR body records 1.5 times the measured `web` duration. Task 9 (Step 27) owns the change that spec 3 asks for after the first runs: after the first green run on the merged tree (PR #3 adds end-to-end tests, so this run's `web` time is not the final one), an owner-approved `ci:` commit sets the `web` job's `timeout-minutes` to ceil(1.5 × that run's `web` duration in minutes) and, in the same step, sets the `web` entry of `JOBS` in `workflow-policy.mjs` to the same number, so the `jobs` check still passes.
- gitleaks' commit count is read from its log line `<n> commits scanned.`, whose form, and the debug note that the number "might be smaller than expected due to commits with no additions", are in the gitleaks v8.30.1 source copy at `C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/gl/detect_detect.go:261-264` (the same copy whose `cmd_root.go` and `sources_git.go` research note 03 cites); `no leaks found` and `leaks found: <n>` are at `gl/cmd_root.go:449-451`.

In the commands below, `<tools>` is `C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/e5-plan-work/tools` and `<run>` is `C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/e5-plan-work/run`; every command is written out in full anyway. All commands are Git Bash. `<run-id>` and `<n>` appear only where a value exists once GitHub has assigned it; the step that uses one says which earlier output prints it.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the failing test for the run reader**

Create `C:/Users/eric/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/E--APP2028-stocks-maxxing-hackathon/e4ab9499-7930-44d4-a255-af9e0c2c66a4/scratchpad/e5-plan-work/tools/first-run.test.mjs` with the Write tool:

```js
// first-run.test.mjs: tests for first-run.mjs (plan Task 7). Scratchpad tool, never committed.
// Run: node --test <tools>/first-run.test.mjs
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { test } from "node:test";
import {
  TRAILER,
  billedMinutes,
  commitsSentence,
  durationMs,
  firstRunSection,
  formatDuration,
  jobRows,
  logLine,
  main,
  openingSection,
  prBody,
  verdict,
} from "./first-run.mjs";

const START = Date.parse("2026-11-20T10:00:00Z");

/** One job as `gh run view --json jobs` gives it. */
function job(name, conclusion, seconds) {
  return {
    name,
    conclusion,
    status: "completed",
    databaseId: 1000 + name.length,
    startedAt: new Date(START).toISOString(),
    completedAt: new Date(START + seconds * 1000).toISOString(),
    steps: [{ number: 1, name: "Set up job", status: "completed", conclusion }],
  };
}

const GREEN = [
  job("contracts", "success", 185),
  job("agent", "success", 100),
  job("web", "success", 720),
  job("hygiene", "success", 60),
  job("summary", "success", 12),
  job("preflip", "skipped", 0),
];

const RUN = {
  databaseId: 42,
  attempt: 1,
  headSha: "a".repeat(40),
  url: "https://github.com/duongnq2798/maat/actions/runs/42",
};

const section = (overrides = {}) =>
  firstRunSection({ run: RUN, jobs: GREEN, earlier: [], historyCommits: 383, gitleaksCommits: 383, ...overrides });

test("billed minutes round each job up to a whole minute, and a skipped job bills nothing", () => {
  assert.equal(billedMinutes(1000, "success"), 1);
  assert.equal(billedMinutes(60000, "success"), 1);
  assert.equal(billedMinutes(60001, "failure"), 2);
  assert.equal(billedMinutes(0, "cancelled"), 1);
  assert.equal(billedMinutes(0, "skipped"), 0);
  assert.equal(billedMinutes(null, "cancelled"), 0);
});

test("a duration is completedAt minus startedAt, shown in minutes and seconds", () => {
  assert.equal(durationMs(job("web", "success", 720)), 720000);
  assert.equal(durationMs({ startedAt: null, completedAt: null }), null);
  assert.equal(durationMs({ startedAt: "2026-11-20T10:00:05Z", completedAt: "2026-11-20T10:00:00Z" }), null);
  assert.equal(formatDuration(185000), "3m 05s");
  assert.equal(formatDuration(59400), "0m 59s");
  assert.equal(formatDuration(null), "-");
});

test("summary is billed as ubuntu-slim and every other job as ubuntu-24.04", () => {
  const { rows, linux, slim } = jobRows(GREEN);
  assert.deepEqual(
    rows.map((row) => [row.name, row.runner, row.billed]),
    [
      ["contracts", "ubuntu-24.04", 4],
      ["agent", "ubuntu-24.04", 2],
      ["web", "ubuntu-24.04", 12],
      ["hygiene", "ubuntu-24.04", 1],
      ["summary", "ubuntu-slim", 1],
      ["preflip", "ubuntu-24.04", 0],
    ],
  );
  assert.equal(linux, 19);
  assert.equal(slim, 1);
});

test("a run is green only when the five jobs succeed and preflip is skipped or absent", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(verdict(jobRows(GREEN).rows), { green: true, problems: [] });
  assert.deepEqual(verdict(jobRows(GREEN.filter((j) => j.name !== "preflip")).rows), { green: true, problems: [] });
  const red = GREEN.map((j) => (j.name === "web" ? { ...j, conclusion: "failure" } : j)).filter((j) => j.name !== "hygiene");
  assert.deepEqual(verdict(jobRows(red).rows), { green: false, problems: ["web failure", "hygiene missing"] });
  const ran = GREEN.map((j) => (j.name === "preflip" ? { ...j, conclusion: "success" } : j));
  assert.deepEqual(verdict(jobRows(ran).rows).problems, ["preflip success"]);
  assert.deepEqual(verdict(jobRows([...GREEN, job("lint", "success", 5)]).rows).problems, ["lint unexpected"]);
});

test("a log line loses its job and step columns, its time stamp and its colour codes", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    logLine("hygiene\tRun docker run --rm x\t2026-11-20T10:00:01.1234567Z \u001b[32mok\u001b[0m 383 commits scanned.\r"),
    { step: "Run docker run --rm x", text: "ok 383 commits scanned." },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(logLine("no tabs here"), { step: "", text: "no tabs here" });
});

test("the scanned-commit sentence compares gitleaks' count with git rev-list --all --count", () => {
  assert.equal(
    commitsSentence(383, 383),
    "Scanned commits: the history scan's `git rev-list --all --count` gave 383, and gitleaks logged `383 commits scanned.`: equal.",
  );
  assert.match(commitsSentence(383, 380), /`380 commits scanned\.`: 3 fewer\. Section 10 of the spec expects them equal;/);
  assert.match(commitsSentence(383, 385), /: 2 more\./);
  assert.match(commitsSentence(383, null), /logged no `commits scanned\.` line, so the check of section 10 of the spec stays open\.$/);
});

test("the section records each job's duration and billed minutes next to the estimate", () => {
  const text = section();
  assert.ok(text.startsWith("## First green run\n\nRun [42](https://github.com/duongnq2798/maat/actions/runs/42) on aaaaaaa, attempt 1."));
  assert.ok(text.includes("| Job | Runner | Result | Duration | Billed minutes |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\n"));
  assert.ok(text.includes("| `contracts` | `ubuntu-24.04` | success | 3m 05s | 4 |"));
  assert.ok(text.includes("| `summary` | `ubuntu-slim` | success | 0m 12s | 1 |"));
  assert.ok(text.includes("| `preflip` | `ubuntu-24.04` | skipped | - | 0 |"));
  assert.ok(
    text.includes(
      "Billed: 19 Linux minutes and 1 slim minute; the estimate is 18 to 26 Linux minutes plus 1 slim minute. " +
        "At $0.006 a Linux minute and $0.002 a slim minute that is $0.116; the estimate is about $0.11 to $0.16.",
    ),
  );
  assert.ok(text.includes("1.5 times this run's `web` duration is 18 minutes (section 3 of the spec)."));
  assert.ok(
    text.includes(
      "The timeout is set from the first green run after `main` is merged into this branch, because PR #3 adds end-to-end tests.",
    ),
  );
  assert.ok(text.endsWith("Earlier runs on this pull request, billed too: none."));
  assert.ok(!text.includes("earlier attempts"));
});

test("earlier runs are listed with their result and billed minutes, and a later attempt says so", () => {
  const earlier = [
    {
      databaseId: 41,
      headSha: "b".repeat(40),
      status: "completed",
      conclusion: "failure",
      url: "https://github.com/duongnq2798/maat/actions/runs/41",
      linux: 7,
      slim: 1,
    },
  ];
  const text = section({ run: { ...RUN, attempt: 2 }, earlier, gitleaksCommits: null });
  assert.ok(text.includes("attempt 2."));
  assert.ok(text.includes("The jobs below are attempt 2; the jobs of earlier attempts were billed too and are not counted here."));
  assert.ok(
    text.endsWith(
      "Earlier runs on this pull request, billed too:\n\n" +
        "- Run [41](https://github.com/duongnq2798/maat/actions/runs/41) on bbbbbbb: failure, 7 Linux minutes and 1 slim minute.",
    ),
  );
  assert.ok(text.includes("stays open."));
});

test("both bodies end with the trailer line and hold no key-shaped string", () => {
  assert.equal(TRAILER, "\u{1F916} Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)");
  assert.match(openingSection(), /^## First green run\n\nNot measured yet\./);
  for (const body of [prBody(openingSection()), prBody(section())]) {
    assert.ok(body.startsWith("## Summary\n"));
    assert.ok(body.endsWith(`\n\n${TRAILER}\n`));
    assert.match(body, /^## No secret$/m);
    assert.match(body, /^## Not in this pull request$/m);
    for (const shape of [/ghp_[0-9A-Za-z]{36}/, /0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}/]) assert.doesNotMatch(body, shape);
  }
});

test("main checks its arguments before it calls git or gh", () => {
  assert.equal(main([]), 1);
  assert.equal(main(["unknown"]), 1);
  assert.equal(main(["measure", "many", "none"]), 1);
  assert.equal(main(["measure", "383", "some"]), 1);
  assert.equal(main(["log", "hygiene"]), 1);
});
```
